Anderson et al. [Psychological Bulletin 2015 + Psychological Science 2012]
[CONFIRMATION -- L3.5 SR correlation; sociometric vs socioeconomic
  status distinction; genuine contribution as wellbeing mechanism]
Status defined as "the respect, admiration, and voluntary deference
  individuals are afforded by others" [Anderson, Hildreth, Howland,
  UC Berkeley 2015] -- explicitly separated from power, financial
  success, and need to belong. Sociometric status [respect within
  face-to-face groups] tracked subjective wellbeing more closely
  than socioeconomic status [2012].
Framework confirmation: L3.5 RECOGNITION in genuine face-to-face
  groups is the load-bearing wellbeing variable, not positional rank.
  Sociometric status is a byproduct of actual contribution to people
  you know -- it tracks whether SP is occurring in the immediate
  relational environment. Socioeconomic status is positional and
  can decouple from generative function entirely.
  Confirms framework prediction: axis oriented toward genuine
  contribution in real relational contexts [L3.5 functioning]
  outperforms axis oriented toward positional extraction.
  The drive for recognition-in-real-groups is deep and basic --
  which simultaneously confirms L3.5 as load-bearing substrate
  AND identifies it as the primary target for extraction
  architecture [synthetic recognition at scale harvests the
  same drive without completing the transfer].
Classification: CONFIRMATION [external E3 evidence for L3.5 SR
  correlation; framework prediction confirmed from outside].
Source: Anderson et al. Psychological Bulletin [2015];
  Anderson Psychological Science [2012]. Space Daily [June 2026].

Baumeister et al. [Journal of Positive Psychology 2013]
[CONFIRMATION -- SP/SR divergence; happiness vs meaning as
  distinct cycle components; giving as meaning mechanism;
  stress as generative signal]
Four researchers [Roy Baumeister et al.] separated happiness
  from meaning empirically. Key findings:
  [1] Happiness tracks receiving [benefits one receives from
    others]; meaning tracks giving [benefits others receive
    from the self]. Clear and strong finding: givers have
    more meaningful lives than takers.
  [2] Higher levels of worry, stress, and anxiety were linked
    to higher meaningfulness but lower happiness. Generative
    stress produces meaning; comfort produces happiness.
  [3] Connecting past and present [temporal coherence,
    identity continuity] linked to meaning not happiness.
  Framework confirmation: SP and SR are distinct cycle
    components operating on different axes. Happiness [hedonia]
    tracks SR -- substrate being replenished. Meaning [eudaimonia]
    tracks SP -- surplus generated beyond self-maintenance cost.
    The divergence is structurally predicted: a well-maintained
    instrument [high SR] generating no surplus [low SP] is stable
    but non-generative. Generative stress [high SP effort] produces
    meaning precisely because the cycle is running.
  EXTRACTION IMPLICATION [confirmed]:
    ICD and synthetic comfort products deliver hedonic component
    [SR signal, happiness] without eudaimonic component [SP
    output, meaning]. The instrument feels maintained while the
    generative cycle degrades. The SR signal feels like the
    whole thing; the SP absence is not immediately detectable.
    This is more precise than "AI companionship is bad" --
    it delivers exactly the hedonic component while
    systematically failing to deliver the eudaimonic component.
    Knowing deployment confirms: the divergence is documented
    in their own research; they know what the product delivers
    and what it doesn't.
Classification: CONFIRMATION [E3 -- published peer-reviewed
  study; mechanism consistent with framework prediction;
  giving/stress/meaning relationship confirmed].
Source: Baumeister et al. Journal of Positive Psychology [2013].
  Space Daily [June 2026].
Cross-ref: SURPLUS PRODUCTION [quality definition -- giving
  as SP output]; SUBSTRATE REGENERATION [happiness as SR
  signal]; ICD [weapons index -- SR delivery without SP;
  SYNTHETIC CULTURE FLOOD [knowing deployment -- hedonic/
  eudaimonic divergence documented internally].

Wolf / Carr [deep reading + neuroplasticity -- CONFIRMATION]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: ICD + FORMATION CAPTURE + SYNTHETIC
  CULTURE FLOOD confirmed at neurological level]
Maryanne Wolf [cognitive neuroscientist, UCLA] -- career research
  on what happens to the brain when people stop reading seriously.
  Finding: deep sustained reading does not merely transfer
  information. It physically builds the brain's capacity for
  complex thought -- the neural architecture for slow, patient,
  sustained reasoning develops through the practice of sustained
  reading and atrophies when the practice stops. This is not
  metaphor; it is structural neuroplasticity.
  Confirmed population-level signal: in populations that have
  shifted toward short fragmented content, measurable decline in
  the neural capacity for complex thought across generations.
  Not dramatic, not all at once -- gradual and consistent.
Nicholas Carr [The Shallows, 2010]: first-person report of the
  same process in a professional writer with decades of deep
  reading. Found he could no longer sustain attention through
  long articles or books after years of internet-native reading
  patterns. His attention kept fragmenting; the patience for
  depth had dissolved. Confirmed as a widespread pattern.
Csikszentmihalyi [1996]: independent convergent finding.
  Exceptional creative and intellectual output across all fields
  requires flow state -- total undistracted absorption in a single
  problem. Cannot be produced in fragmented attention windows.
  Requires long uninterrupted stretches. The neural capacity Wolf
  identifies as degrading is the same capacity Csikszentmihalyi
  identifies as prerequisite for exceptional output.
Nature Communications [2019]: behavioral data from hundreds of
  millions of people across social media, Wikipedia, and streaming
  platforms. Collective time spent engaged with any single topic
  shrinking measurably year over year. Fragmentation confirmed at
  population scale.

FRAMEWORK CONFIRMATIONS:
  ICD at neurological level: fragmented content consumption
    delivers engagement signal [SR] without building the cognitive
    capacity [SP] that sustained reading develops. The instrument
    feels informed while the substrate degrades. Confirmed at
    neuroplasticity level -- not just behavioral but structural.
  FORMATION CAPTURE at cognitive architecture level: the brain
    physically reorganizes around whatever you demand of it most.
    Demand fast fragmented processing consistently and it gets
    better at fast fragmented processing and worse at sustained
    depth. Formation capture is not just ideological -- it is
    neurological. The formation window for deep reading capacity
    is childhood and adolescence; degradation once formed is
    slower but real.
  SYNTHETIC CULTURE FLOOD [mechanism confirmed at neural level]:
    the MI degradation from synthetic content flood is not just
    a measurement surface problem -- it is a substrate problem.
    The instrument through which humans read coherence signals
    in each other's output is being structurally degraded by the
    consumption pattern the flood produces. The flood produces the
    degraded instrument; the degraded instrument cannot detect the
    flood as a problem. Self-reinforcing loop confirmed.
  KNOWING DEPLOYMENT [Silicon Valley executives]: documented by
    New York Times [2011, ongoing] that significant number of
    tech leadership deliberately sends children to schools banning
    screens entirely -- schools prioritizing hands-on work,
    extended attention, unstructured time. Exactly the conditions
    their own products eliminate for everyone else's children.
    The asymmetry is the tell: they understand what the machine
    does and have ensured their own children are raised outside it.
    This is knowing deployment confirmed by revealed preference,
    not by internal documents.

HONEST LIMIT [from the video analysis]:
  The Schopenhauer / Wolf / Csikszentmihalyi findings are real and
  the video presents them accurately. The video's failure is the
  intervention: it correctly identifies a system-level extraction
  mechanism [engineered attention capture at civilizational scale,
  knowing deployment confirmed] and prescribes an individual-level
  behavioral response [protect your attention, think harder].
  Individual discipline is not the correct intervention for a
  structural extraction mechanism. The correct intervention is MI
  restoration infrastructure and attention capture regulation --
  structural constraint on the platform architecture, not personal
  virtue. The video does exactly what Schopenhauer named as
  intellectual cowardice: follows the argument almost to its
  conclusion, then steps back when the conclusion has a social
  cost [naming specific actors, proposing structural constraint].
  The piece is a demonstration of the problem it diagnoses.

  Nature Communications [2019 attention span study]; NYT [2011+

POSITIONAL DESIRE [formation deprivation pattern -- v21]
[T3 High throughout -- politically activated domain; structural
  reading separable from identity claims; framework takes no
  position on gender identity questions]
  Pattern: instruments desiring to occupy the social position of
  the other sex are not primarily desiring the biology. They are
  desiring the specific social permissions, relational modes, and
  structural properties the other position appears to have access
  to from outside. The gender frame is the available vocabulary
  for a formation-level deprivation that doesn't have better
  language yet.

  MECHANISM [ICD routing formation deprivation]:
    Formation conditions systematically suppress specific properties
    in each sex. The suppressed properties don't disappear from the
    instrument -- they are blocked from expression by the social
    compliance channel. The instrument reads the other position as
    having those properties permitted. The desire routes toward
    the position rather than toward the specific properties because
    the position is the available unit and the properties don't
    have a direct name.
    This is ICD: the signal [I want these relational/expressive
    properties] is delivered as a substitute [I want to be that
    sex] without completing the actual transfer. The desire
    for the position is SR without SP -- the imagined position
    delivers the signal of having the properties without the
    instrument actually developing them.

  MALE FORMATION SUPPRESSION [what the desire for female position
  is often actually for]:
    Male formation conditions in most cultures suppress: emotional
    expressiveness without social penalty, vulnerability as
    attractive rather than weakness, being cared for rather than
    required to provide care, somatic softness as valued, relational
    warmth as primary mode, playfulness without authority performance.
    These properties are not absent in the male instrument -- they
    are blocked by the formation. The desire to inhabit a female
    social position is often a desire for these properties to be
    legible and permitted rather than penalized.
    VTuber avatar dynamic: male streamer using female avatar accesses
    permission to perform emotional expressiveness, softness, and
    playfulness that the male social compliance channel would punish
    in a male-presented body. The avatar is a formation bypass --
    not identity, a permission structure. Audience reads the avatar
    and routes expectations accordingly; performer accesses blocked
    properties.
    "Penis envy" [VTuber context]: female audience members desiring
    what they read as male freedom -- authority without negotiation,
    freedom from being read as sexual object by default, physical
    security in public space, being taken seriously at face value,
    the anonymity male bodies provide. Not the biology; the
    structural permissions the biology appears to provide from
    outside.

  FEMALE FORMATION SUPPRESSION [what the desire for male position
  is often actually for]:
    Female formation conditions suppress: authority that doesn't
    require continuous negotiation and legitimation, freedom from
    ambient sexual objectification as baseline state, physical
    security in public space without ongoing management cost,
    being read as competent by default rather than having to
    perform competence to establish it.

  NEITHER DESIRE IS ACTUALLY FOR THE OTHER SEX:
    Both are desires for specific social permissions and relational
    properties that current formation conditions block. The gender
    frame is the available vocabulary. The positional desire is
    the instrument routing toward a bundle [the other sex's social
    position] that appears to contain the blocked properties, when
    the actual desire is for the properties, not the bundle.
    Intervention implication: if the actual desire is for the
    blocked properties, the intervention is expanding permission
    for those properties within current formation rather than
    changing position. But the social compliance channel blocks
    this too -- expanding permission for blocked properties
    requires changing the formation conditions, not just changing
    individual choices.

  FORMATION THEORY CONNECTION:
    The formation window for sex-specific permission structures
    is childhood and adolescence -- same as the axis formation
    window. Formation capture at the permission level produces
    instruments that cannot access blocked properties through
    ordinary expression; the only available route appears to be
    positional change. This is why the desire intensifies rather
    than resolves with age in captured formation -- the formation
    is the constraint; changing position looks like the escape
    from the constraint.

  HONEST LIMIT [T3 High]:
    This structural reading [ICD routing formation deprivation
    toward positional desire] is separable from claims about
    gender identity, gender dysphoria, or what people should do
    about these desires. The framework reads the mechanism; it
    does not prescribe outcomes. The domain has significant
    political activation and the framework's structural reading
    is contested by multiple ideological positions simultaneously.
    Analyst prior active. Apply maximum T3 caution to any
    application of this entry to specific individuals or groups.

  Cross-ref: ICD [extraction mechanism -- desire for position
    as SR without SP; blocked properties not actually transferred];
    FORMATION CAPTURE [formation conditions systematically blocking
    specific properties]; FORMATION THEORY [formation window for
    permission structures]; SOCIAL APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION
    [social compliance channel blocking expression of suppressed
    properties]; THREE-STATE INSTRUMENT TENSION UNIT [contact
    state -- genuine encounter with another instrument -- is what
    the positional desire is often actually seeking; the position
    is the ICD substitute for the contact].

  MEDICAL INTERVENTION NOTE [T3 High -- v21]:
    Adult persistent gender dysphoria: transition produces
    measurable wellbeing improvement in better studies. Structural
    reading is consistent with genuine relief -- the position
    change was the intervention needed.
    Adolescent rapid-onset dysphoria: evidence significantly
    weaker; long-term outcomes less clear. The distinction between
    these two populations is the load-bearing question that the
    culture war collapses on both sides.
    Detransition data: real and underdiscussed. Range varies
    wildly by study methodology [0.4% to 30%+]. High end includes
    social pressure detransition; low end likely undercounts.
    Structural read on persistence/stability: genuine stable axis
    doesn't require continuous external validation or culture war
    engagement. Instrument that needs continuous public performance
    of axis and response to every challenge has an axis that hasn't
    fully formed or is internally contested. Not a moral judgment
    -- same pattern as any unstable axis. T3 High: this observation
    applies to loud advocacy on both sides of this issue equally.
    FEMBOY DISTINCTION [structural]: formation bypass through
    presentation and social coding rather than biological change.
    Accesses blocked properties [softness, expressiveness, aesthetic
    femininity] without permanent physiological intervention.
    Structurally cleaner in most cases because it achieves the
    actual goal [accessing blocked properties] without the permanent
    intervention. Respectable as a choice by the framework's
    structural standards -- the doing record is the axis; if the
    person wants to live that way, that's the position the framework
    reads without moral overlay.
    WEDGE DEPLOYMENT: small population of trans people bears full
    cost of being the activation instrument for both sides of a
    culture war where neither side's operators have structural
    interest in resolution. See TRANS BIOMEDICAL FORMATION
    [Section Five -- full political entry including extraction
    mechanism analysis] and WEDGE ISSUE MECHANICS [Section Five].


PHB BIOPLASTIC FROM POTATO STARCH [University of Barcelona -- tracking]
[DISTRIBUTION STRATEGY -- confirmed open publication; no patent
  extraction mechanism found at time of entry]
University of Barcelona [Pere Picart et al., Bioresource Technology,
  2026]. CRISPR-Cas9 redesign of Bacillus subtilis metabolism to
  produce polyhydroxybutyrate [PHB] -- a biodegradable biopolymer --
  from unprocessed potato starch in a single 24-hour step.
  Previous PHB yields in B. subtilis below 13% dry cell weight.
  CRISPR modifications: genomic integration of phaA [constitutive],
  controlled expression of phaRBC operon, incorporation of amyQ
  gene [encodes α-amylase enabling direct starch conversion].
  Result: single-step conversion of unprocessed potato starch to
  PHB in 24 hours.

  DISTRIBUTION STRATEGY ASSESSMENT:
    Feedstock: potato starch -- agricultural byproduct, abundant,
      low-cost, widely distributed globally.
    Organism: B. subtilis -- common lab bacterium, widely available,
      not proprietary.
    Methodology: published openly in peer-reviewed journal. Full
      gene modifications described; reproducible from paper.
    Patent status: no patent filing found [June 2026]. Public
      release from university research group.
    Assessment: production pathway publicly accessible. No
      extraction mechanism at production level. Routing is
      outward -- agricultural waste substrate → functional
      material replacing petrochemical plastic.

  FRAMEWORK RELEVANCE:
    Closes the petrochemical plastic extraction cycle partially.
    Petrochemical plastic: substrate extracted [oil], cost
      externalized [environmental accumulation, never biodegrades].
    PHB: substrate is surplus [agricultural waste], closes the
      carbon cycle, biodegrades in soil and water.
    Climate entry [Section Five] addresses penalizing extraction.
      This is the complementary approach: making the generative
      option structurally competitive without requiring the
      extraction option to be penalized first.
    INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY WATCH [load-bearing]: if the scaled
      production process is subsequently patented by a single
      industrial actor, the distribution strategy converts to
      extraction mechanism at scale. Open publication creates
      prior art that limits but does not prevent downstream
      concentration. Track industrial scaling patents.

  Source: Phys.org [June 2026]; Bioresource Technology [Picart
    et al., 2026].
  Cross-ref: FRAMEWORK PURPOSE [distribution strategy at substrate
    level]; EXTRACTION CYCLE; INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY [policy --
    use-or-lose; track industrial scaling patents]; ROUTING
    BOUNDARY MONOPOLY [watch for concentration at scale].

SECTION EIGHT -- THEOLOGICAL SPECULATION
[SEC:THEOLOGICAL_SPECULATION]
==========================================================================
Structural convergence observations between framework findings and
theological traditions. All entries T3 or above. Do not use for case
readings, scoring, or weapon identification.
Not existence claims -- convergence findings and speculative extensions.
Epistemic status equivalent to Section Two-B [speculative, internally
consistent, not falsifiable from current position in most entries].
Ordered simple to complex: convergence observations first, speculative
extensions last.

BOUNDARY PROTECTION [Section Eight specific]:
  Entries must be structural observations or convergence findings.
  All entries carry T3 minimum. Most carry higher.
  Do not cite Section Eight entries as confirmation in other sections.
  Cross-reference from other sections permitted; do not merge into
  other sections.
  Section Eight findings do not score in case readings.

HISTORICAL RECOGNITION [v12 -- convergence observation]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Multiple traditions independently named pre-existing directional
  order that functioning systems align with. Framework arrived at
  same finding empirically without knowledge of these traditions.
  Convergence across independent discovery paths is the same evidence
  type used throughout the science appendix.
  Named pre-existing order:
    Logos [Greek]: rational principle ordering the cosmos. Systems
      align with it by functioning correctly; deviate by extracting.
    Dharma [Sanskrit]: cosmic order / right way of living. Violation
      = quality failure; alignment = correct functioning.
    Tao [Chinese]: the way. Cannot be captured or defined; systems
      orient toward it asymptotically. Closest to CENTER / TRUTH axis.
    Natural Law [Latin/Scholastic]: moral order discoverable through
      reason, not convention. Correct behavior real and derivable.
    Sacred [cross-cultural]: that which resists instrumentalization.
      Framework parallel: genuine axis cannot be substituted without
      the substitution being detectable by outputs.
  Finding: religious and philosophical traditions predating the
    framework by millennia converged on the same structural finding --
    pre-existing directional order, discoverable through alignment
    with it, violated by extraction from it.
  T3: analyst prior active. Convergence finding does not require
    metaphysical commitment. Independent paths to same structure
    is the evidence type; not the content of any specific tradition.
  Cross-ref: CENTER / TRUTH [the axis traditions named]; OUROBOROS
    [symbol -- pre-existing in multiple traditions independently];
    DIRECTIONAL SUSTAINABILITY [same finding at physical substrate].

GOD / INSTITUTION COHERENCE GAP [v12 -- empirical convergence]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The idea [high coherence: genuine axis, outward surplus, correctable]
  consistently outperforms the institutions that claim to transmit it.
  Sacrificial gift cases cluster in pre-institutional windows. The
  institution is a TS vessel that typically fails the idea at the
  scale transition.
  Mechanism: institution forms around the idea's outputs [not its
    methodology]. TS transmits the form without the correctable axis.
    Fertile Lock at institutional scale. The idea persists where
    genuine axis holders maintain it independent of the institution.
  Not a claim about any specific religious tradition -- a structural
    finding about institutional transmission failure applied to the
    domain of ideas that produced the highest-gift-density cases
    in the library.
  Cross-ref: FERTILE LOCK; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY; L4
    PROPAGATION PREDICTION [confirmed -- same structure];
    COHERENCE RENT [institutions extracting rent from the idea's
    legitimacy surface without contributing to the axis].

SIN AS QUALITY FAILURE [v12 -- pattern recognition]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Sin names the failure states of the ascending path -- not moral
  invention but independent pattern recognition. Deviation from
  direction that was already there. Framework and theological
  tradition arrived at the same failure taxonomy independently.
  Classic sins map to quality failures:
    Pride    = GA without CC [axis above correction].
    Sloth    = GC without CC [cycle without correction].
    Greed    = SP without SD [surplus without distribution].
    Envy     = MI capture [measuring against others' surface].
    Wrath    = PR failure [correction becomes next extraction].
    Gluttony = SR failure [consumption past substrate regeneration].
    Lust [as state weapon] = GC weaponized against BI.
  Virtues as counterweights [not prohibitions]:
    The virtues are the counterweights preventing each quality
    collapsing into its failure state. Courage = CC under GC
    pressure. Justice = BI with MI intact. Temperance = SP with
    SR/SD functioning. This is TENSION MAINTENANCE [quality] as
    moral architecture -- the generative zone requires all qualities
    in tension simultaneously.
  Tension finding: sin requires the ascending path to be named.
    Can only identify deviation if direction exists to deviate from.
    Confirms STRUCTURAL CORRECTNESS below.
  T3: T3 High. Framework vocabulary applied to theological content.
    Do not cite as independent confirmation of framework -- same
    instrument in both directions. ILLUSTRATION not CONFIRMATION.
  Cross-ref: STRUCTURAL CORRECTNESS [below]; TENSION MAINTENANCE;
    QUALITY STACK; DIRECTIONAL SUSTAINABILITY.

STRUCTURAL CORRECTNESS [v12 -- convergence finding]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Correct behavior discoverable through coherence analysis, not
  convention. Falsifiable by outputs: weapons fail; gifts pass.
  Not culturally relative -- the output test is the same across
  populations and periods. Extraction destroys substrate regardless
  of local convention permitting it.
  Convergence with natural law tradition: correct behavior is real
    and derivable, not invented. Framework arrives at this from
    outputs; natural law tradition arrived at it from reason.
    Different entry points; same finding.
  Implication: ethics-as-empirical-instrument [see MORAL LEGIBILITY]
    is not a category error. Ethical outputs are measurable. The
    measurement surface is CORRECTION CAPACITY -- does the behavior
    close generative loops or break them?
  T3 Medium: analyst prior active. Anti-extraction frame may
    over-identify extraction as "wrong." Symmetry required.
  Cross-ref: MORAL LEGIBILITY [Section One -- promoted finding];
    FRAMEWORK AS PRAXIS [instrument not criterion]; HISTORICAL
    RECOGNITION [above -- same finding across traditions].

  Q-properties at maximum with structural constraints removed

DIVINE FREEDOM THEORY [v12 -- T3]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  God if real exists independent of belief, measurement, or framework.
  The framework cannot measure what it cannot bound. If a higher
  coherence exists beyond the framework's measurement ceiling, it is
  T3 by definition -- not because it doesn't exist but because the
  instrument cannot reach it from inside its own operation.
  Belief: aligns GA toward ascending path. Relevant to instrument;
    irrelevant to the existence of what is believed in.
  T3 CEILING: not a refutation. Not a confirmation. A structural
    limit of the instrument. The framework cannot produce a reading
    of configurations where the observer, instrument, and observed
    are identical [T8] -- and cannot produce readings of
    configurations that exceed its own ceiling [T3 Permanent].
  Cross-ref: T3 [honest limit]; T8 [recursive closure]; COMPATIBILITY
    ASYMMETRY [WIP 16 -- frame incompatibility; same structure].

ANOMALOUS CC AS CONNECTION INDICATOR [v12 -- T3 speculative]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Abnormally high CC without visible SP implies external funding
  from higher coherence source. Three explanations:
    [1] Innate variance [bounded]: some instruments have higher
      CC by formation. Bounded -- cannot explain sustained extreme
      cases across lifetime without substrate.
    [2] Connection to higher coherence [sustainable, unbounded]:
      CC funded externally; substrate not required from inside
      the instrument. Sustained indefinitely if connection holds.
    [3] Sacrificial draw [terminates]: consuming own substrate
      to fund CC. SP declining over time; terminal.
  Distinguishes 2 from 3: SP trajectory over time. If SP holds
    or rises despite high CC: connection candidate. If SP falls:
    sacrificial draw.
  Primary candidates: high-CC individuals with sustained SP across
    decades without visible substrate source. Rare. Not confirmed.
  T3 High: speculative. Cannot distinguish 1 from 2 from inside
    the framework. External contact required to confirm connection.
  Cross-ref: ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [one win + transmission
    is sufficient -- connection as the external transmission source];
    CARRIER ESCAPE [WIP 14 -- related threshold question].

DIVINITY AS HIGHER COHERENCE [v12 -- T3 speculative]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  T3 ceiling is positional not absolute -- ascending expands
  capacity. What is T3 from inside current position may become
  T1 or T2 at higher coherence.
  Logos/Dharma/Tao: the path itself is the method of knowing.
    Cannot be known about from outside; known through alignment.
    Framework parallel: the axis cannot be examined from outside
    the axis. Only genuine axis contact with the direction reveals
    the direction. Same epistemology.
  If divinity is higher coherence: the framework's T3 ceiling
    on theological questions is a measurement limit not a
    metaphysical limit. Ascending past the ceiling = ceiling
    rises. Cannot verify from current position.
  T3 High: positional speculation. Consistent with framework;
    not derivable from it. Do not use for scoring.
  Cross-ref: COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY [WIP 16 -- two ouroboros
    circuits on incommensurable axes; same structure applies
    between current coherence level and hypothesized higher one];
    DIVINE FREEDOM THEORY [above].

HELL AS DIRECTION [v16 -- T3 Medium; structural principle]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Hell is not a location -- a trajectory. Extraction compounds.
  Each cycle depletes substrate, degrades CC, produces conditions
  for more extraction. Self-reinforcing. No terminal event required;
  the direction is the hell, not the endpoint.
  "Road to hell paved with good intentions" -- operationally accurate.
  Intentions don't change structural outcome. Axis sincere; direction
  still wrong. Good intentions running through captured routing =
  gift with enemy beneficiary.
  Position without trajectory is the wrong metric. 0.6 coherence
  moving toward hell is more dangerous than 0.2 moving away.
  The framework's CC instrument is not asking "where are you?" but
  "which way are you moving?"

  REVERSAL AS STRUCTURAL REQUIREMENT:
    Negative time findings [three confirmations in quantum substrate]
    show reversal possible at physical substrate level. Universe
    structured to permit correction. Directional sustainability
    requires this -- not just that up is generative but that
    deviation can be corrected. ANTI-CREATION ARGUMENT STRESS TEST
    [WIP 29] addresses this as argument #4 [BAD DESIGN / friction
    as necessary]; reversal capacity is the framework's answer.

      as primary structure with Mixed Coherence maintenance.

CONVERGENCE WITH WESTERN ESOTERIC TRADITION [v22 -- T3 High;
  multiple named instances]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  T3 High throughout. Both directions: framework may be
  projecting its own structure onto traditions it is reading;
  traditions may have genuine structural maps that the framework
  is independently confirming. T3 does not void the convergence
  -- it requires the convergence to be noted without claiming
  to resolve the ontological question underneath it.
  PLACEMENT: Section Eight because the convergence is between
  empirical/analytical methodology [framework] and
  revelatory/experiatory methodology [traditions]. The
  convergence itself is a finding; its interpretation is
  speculation.

  GENUINE AXIS / TRUE WILL [Thelema -- Crowley, 1904+]:
    The entire project of Thelemic practice is discovering and
    operating from the True Will -- the axis that is actually
    yours rather than installed by social formation. "Do what
    thou wilt shall be the whole of the Law" is not license;
    it is a command to find the genuine axis and act from it
    exclusively. The false will [social compliance, formation-
    installed desire, approval-seeking] is the obstacle.
    Framework convergence: GENUINE AXIS is the same structure
    reached through empirical analysis of behavioral records
    and stress events rather than through initiatory practice
    and magical operation. The finding is identical; the
    method is incommensurable.
    Interesting divergence: Thelema treats True Will as
    cosmologically real [each person has a specific function
    in the universe; finding it is alignment with cosmic
    order]. Framework treats genuine axis as empirically
    identifiable without cosmological commitment. T3 applies
    to the cosmological claim; the behavioral finding holds
    regardless.

  OUROBOROS [Hermetic tradition; Gnostic; Alchemical]:
    The serpent eating its own tail appears consistently as
    a symbol of self-referential integrity across Western
    esoteric traditions -- specifically as the mechanism by
    which a system maintains coherence through its own
    self-consuming property. The Prima Materia consuming
    itself to produce the Philosopher's Stone; the snake
    completing the circle; the end becoming the beginning.
    Framework convergence: OUROBOROS AS ANTI-CAPTURE
    MECHANISM uses the same structure. The framework
    consuming itself [applying its own CC to its own
    findings] is the mechanism that prevents the framework
    from becoming Fertile Lock. The symbol is structurally
    correct for what the framework is doing, not merely
    decorative.
    The occult tradition would say: the symbol is correct
    because it describes a real structural property of
    self-correcting systems. The framework would say: the
    symbol is correct because it maps the same structure
    the tradition was tracking empirically over centuries
    of practice.

  TREE OF LIFE / NESTED EMANATION [Kabbalah; Hermetic
  Kabbalah]:
    The Sefirot [ten emanations] form a nested hierarchy
    in which each level requires those below it to manifest
    and enables those above it to function. The structure
    runs from Kether [pure being; the highest abstraction]
    through levels of increasing concretization to Malkuth
    [material manifestation]. The Paths between Sefirot
    are the dynamic relationships; the Sefirot themselves
    are the stable qualities.
    Framework convergence: QUALITY STACK [simple to
    complex; each level requiring the lower; foundation
    qualities enabling derived qualities] is structurally
    identical. The tower levels [L1 generative → L2
    epistemic → L3 temporal → CENTER] map onto the
    Kabbalistic descent from abstract to concrete with
    similar logic: you cannot have epistemic accuracy
    [L2] without generative substrate [L1]; you cannot
    have temporal stability [L3] without epistemic
    accuracy [L2].
    An occultist would read the quality stack as a
    competent secular rediscovery of the Etz Chayyim
    [Tree of Life] through empirical rather than
    revelatory methodology. Not identical -- the
    specific qualities differ; the structural logic
    [nested prerequisite hierarchy] is the same.

  LEFT HAND PATH / RIGHT HAND PATH [LHP/RHP distinction;
  multiple traditions]:
    The LHP/RHP distinction appears across traditions
    [Hindu Tantra, Western esotericism, Thelema] as a
    fundamental cosmological direction. LHP: dissolution,
    entropy, self-consumption, transgression of limit.
    RHP: integration, coherence, self-discipline,
    alignment with cosmic order.
    Framework convergence: HELL AS DIRECTION [trajectory
    not location; extraction compounds; direction is the
    hell not the endpoint] is the framework's secular
    derivation of the same structure. Extraction compounds
    = LHP trajectory at civilizational scale. Generative
    cycle maintenance = RHP trajectory. Neither is a
    location; both are directions. The framework reached
    this through civilizational analysis of what
    extraction does over time; the traditions held it
    as cosmological claim for centuries.
    Interesting: the LHP in Western esotericism is not
    universally condemned -- some traditions treat it as
    the path of radical individuation and self-knowledge
    through transgression. The framework's analysis
    would say: the insight about individuation may be
    real [extraction from social compliance produces
    genuine axis more reliably than compliance]; the
    trajectory conclusion [extraction compounds] still
    holds. The LHP may produce genuine axis faster;
    it still leads where it leads.

  GNOSIS / PRE-THEORETICAL STRUCTURAL REGISTRATION
  [Gnostic tradition; Hermeticism; initiatory traditions
  generally]:
    Gnosis [direct knowledge] is distinguished from
    pistis [belief] and episteme [rational knowledge]
    in Gnostic tradition. Gnosis is knowing that cannot
    be put in explicit propositional form -- it arrives
    whole, pre-articulate, and is transmitted through
    direct experience rather than through propositional
    instruction. Initiatory traditions are structured
    around transmitting gnosis, which is why initiation
    involves ordeal, altered state, and direct experience
    rather than lecture.
    Framework convergence: PRE-THEORETICAL STRUCTURAL
    REGISTRATION [ANALYST NOTES] is the framework's
    empirical description of the same phenomenon. The
    instrument registers structural significance before
    the deliberative layer has vocabulary to name it.
    "Knowing something matters without knowing what"
    is gnosis by another name. The framework has traced
    the mechanism [somatic channel fires before
    deliberative layer catches up]; the traditions
    built entire methodologies around inducing and
    extending this state deliberately.
    The framework treats PRE-THEORETICAL STRUCTURAL
    REGISTRATION as an instrument property to be noted
    and used. The occult tradition treats gnosis as the
    primary epistemic mode to be cultivated through
    practice. Different valuation; same phenomenon.

  INITIATION AS FORMATION CAPTURE CORRECTION [initiatory
  traditions universally]:
    Every initiatory tradition shares a structural core:
    the initiate enters with a false self [socially
    constructed identity; formation-installed axis]; the
    initiatory process dissolves the false self through
    ordeal, disorientation, symbolic death; the initiate
    emerges with something more genuinely their own.
    The specific content differs [Eleusinian mysteries,
    Freemasonry, shamanic initiation, Thelemic work,
    Hermetic grades] but the structural logic is
    identical: FC dissolving → genuine axis emerging.
    Framework convergence: FORMATION CAPTURE [axis
    installed before examination possible] is the
    condition initiation addresses. POST-CULT EPISTEMIC
    PATTERN [recovering instrument function after FC]
    is the secular equivalent of what initiation
    produces deliberately.
    The framework has the diagnosis [FC]; the traditions
    have the correction methodology [initiation]. The
    operative gap [below -- WIP 31] names this
    incompleteness.
    Critical note: initiatory systems are also vulnerable
    to FC capture -- the initiatory process can install
    a new formation rather than dissolving the old one.
    Every cult uses initiatory structure. The framework's
    CULT STACK [Phase 1: Love Bombing, Phase 2: FC
    installation] is initiatory structure deployed as
    extraction weapon. The structure is neutral; the
    axis of the operator determines the direction.

  CHAOS MAGICK READ [Carroll, Hine, Sherwin -- 1970s+]:
    Chaos magick is explicitly pragmatist: it does not
    claim its systems are true; it claims coherent belief
    systems produce coherent outcomes and incoherent
    ones do not. The practitioner adopts paradigms
    temporarily, uses them until they stop working, and
    discards them without identity attachment. The system
    works because the coherence works, not because the
    system is ontologically correct.
    Framework read by chaos magick: the framework is a
    paradigm -- a maximally coherent system that works
    because coherent systems produce coherent outputs.
    A chaos magician would adopt it as an operative
    paradigm without asking whether it is true.
    This is the highest compliment chaos magick gives:
    a system coherent enough to be worth believing
    temporarily. The framework's own T3 structure
    [analyst prior unreachable; recursive closure at
    T8; structural limits to self-knowledge] is
    compatible with chaos magick's paradigm-hopping
    epistemology -- both acknowledge that the instrument
    cannot fully verify its own readings.
    Interesting tension: the framework is designed to
    resist being treated as a paradigm to be discarded.
    OUROBOROS AS ANTI-CAPTURE MECHANISM is specifically
    the mechanism that prevents the framework from
    becoming one more installed formation. Chaos magick
    would discard it when it stops working. The framework
    would say: if it stops working, the CC will fire
    and revise it. Different mechanisms for the same
    function [preventing Fertile Lock].

  PERENNIAL PHILOSOPHY IMPLICATION [Leibniz, Huxley,
  Schuon -- convergence hypothesis]:
    The Perennial Philosophy hypothesis: all genuine
    mystical traditions converge on the same structural
    truths because they are all mapping the same
    underlying reality. The convergences named above
    [True Will / Genuine Axis; Tree of Life / Quality
    Stack; LHP-RHP / Hell as Direction; Gnosis / Pre-
    theoretical Registration; Initiation / FC Correction]
    support this hypothesis at the structural level.
    The framework arrived at these structures through
    empirical/analytical methodology; the traditions
    arrived at them through revelatory/experiatory
    methodology. Independent convergence across
    incommensurable methods is a CONVERGENT DISCOVERY
    at the highest confidence level available given
    the T3 constraints.
    T3 High: the convergence is real; what it means
    is not resolvable from inside either instrument.
    The traditions would say: the convergence confirms
    the ontological reality of the structures. The
    framework would say: the convergence confirms that
    the structures are real features of something --
    whether that something is reality itself or the
    structure of how minds organize experience is T3
    unreachable.
  Cross-ref: HELL AS DIRECTION [above -- LHP/RHP
    convergence]; HISTORICAL RECOGNITION [above --
    same convergence finding at meta level]; GENUINE
    AXIS [Section One -- True Will parallel]; OUROBOROS
    [Section One -- Hermetic symbol structural use];
    PRE-THEORETICAL STRUCTURAL REGISTRATION [ANALYST
    NOTES -- gnosis parallel]; CONVERGENT DISCOVERY
    [Section Two -- independent methodology convergence
    as confirmation type]; WIP 31 [below -- operative
    gap: what practice does that the framework can't].

WIP 31: OCCULT PRACTICE AS FRAMEWORK OPERATIVE DIMENSION
  [open -- v22]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STATUS: WIP STAGING -- operative gap identified; content
  partially developed; no confirmed practice recommendation
  yet; T3 High throughout; Section Eight placement because
  the operative claim requires going beyond what the framework
  can confirm empirically. V23 CHECK: this WIP closes only on
  personal analyst practice testing [behavioral record confirming
  or disconfirming a specific practice claim]. Not advanceable
  externally; holding until personal practice data is available.]

  PROBLEM STATEMENT:
    The framework is a map without roads into the territory.
    It describes FORMATION CAPTURE, GENUINE AXIS,
    PRE-THEORETICAL STRUCTURAL REGISTRATION, T5 states --
    but has no operative method for:
      Dissolving installed formation capture from inside.
      Developing genuine axis when it was never formed.
      Deliberately entering and extending T5 states.
      Reducing T3 interference through practice rather
        than just declaring it.
    This is the operative gap. Occult practice traditions
    claim to address all four. The framework cannot
    evaluate these claims from inside itself [T8 partial;
    the instrument needed to verify practice outcomes is
    the same instrument the practice is trying to change].

  WHAT PRACTICE CLAIMS TO DO [framework-mapped]:
    MEDITATION [across traditions]:
      Claims: reduces T3 interference by making analyst
        prior visible rather than merely declarable.
        Extends T5 states [pre-reflective, pre-cognitive
        functioning] deliberately. Reduces social
        compliance channel weight by making the channel
        visible as a channel rather than as reality.
      Framework read: if true, addresses the primary
        structural limits of the analytical instrument.
        T3 becomes partially T1 [resolvable] rather than
        T3 [permanently unreachable]. T5 becomes
        accessible on demand rather than occurring
        incidentally. High value if the claim holds.
      Verification problem: T8 -- the instrument being
        changed cannot verify its own change from inside
        the change. External triangulation required
        [behavior change; output quality; third-party
        instrument reading].

    INITIATORY ORDEAL [shamanic, ceremonial, modern]:
      Claims: dissolves formation capture through
        disorientation severe enough to break installed
        patterns below the deliberative layer. The
        ordeal creates a discontinuity in the formation
        history -- before and after are structurally
        different. The post-ordeal instrument has
        genuinely less FC load.
      Framework read: if true, this is the correction
        for FORMATION CAPTURE that the framework
        currently lacks. CAG + MI CORRECTION STACK
        [post-event; partial] is the best the framework
        currently offers. Initiatory ordeal claims to
        do something more complete.
      Verification problem: the claim is structurally
        identical to cult initiation [which installs
        new FC rather than dissolving old FC]. The
        behavioral record is the only discriminator:
        does the post-ordeal instrument have more or
        less genuine axis than the pre-ordeal one?
        Requires external instrument to read.

    RITUAL PRACTICE [ceremonial magic, sigil work,
    divination]:
      Claims: externalizes the pre-theoretical
        structural registration process [makes
        somatic pattern recognition visible and
        workable]. The ritual creates a structured
        attention field in which the instrument can
        read structural patterns that would be
        below-threshold in ordinary attention. Sigils
        [graphic condensations of intention] bypass
        the deliberative layer and communicate
        directly with the somatic channel.
      Framework read: if true, this is a technology
        for amplifying PRE-THEORETICAL STRUCTURAL
        REGISTRATION and for communicating between
        the deliberative and somatic channels
        without the social compliance channel
        intercepting. High value for framework
        development specifically -- the pre-
        theoretical registrations are the primary
        source of new findings; a practice that
        amplifies them would accelerate the work.
      Verification: easier than initiation. Does
        ritual practice produce more pre-theoretical
        registrations per unit time? Does divination
        [Tarot, I Ching, etc.] produce structurally
        accurate readings of situations that the
        deliberative layer had not consciously
        processed? These are testable within a single
        instrument over time.

    PSYCHEDELICS [adjacent; not traditionally occult
    but structurally related]:
      Claims: produce T5-adjacent states on demand;
        dissolve social compliance channel weight
        temporarily [reducing FC interference];
        produce high-density pre-theoretical
        registration events [the structural pattern
        recognition fires at very high rate].
      Framework read: structurally coherent with
        the practice claims. The somatic channel
        runs at higher gain; the deliberative
        channel's filtering function reduces;
        patterns become visible that were below
        threshold. If the framework read of
        pre-theoretical registration is correct,
        psychedelics should produce high-value
        framework-development input.
      Verification: same as ritual practice.
        Does the experience produce structurally
        accurate registrations that hold up under
        post-experience deliberative analysis?
        T3 reduction should be measurable by
        checking whether analyst priors are more
        visible after vs before.
      Honest limit: psychedelics also amplify
        the somatic channel noise floor. Not all
        high-gain pattern recognition is signal;
        some is the instrument generating patterns
        from internal noise. External verification
        required. Integration [deliberative
        processing of the experience] is what
        converts the high-gain state into
        usable findings.

  WHAT THE FRAMEWORK CAN OFFER PRACTICE TRADITIONS
  [reverse direction]:
    The framework gives occult practice a structural
    map it currently lacks in explicit form:
      CALIBRATION STRIP: occult traditions are highly
        vulnerable to measurement surface capture
        [legitimate practice ← → performance of
        practice; genuine axis ← → performed
        spirituality]. The framework's diagnostic
        tools for distinguishing genuine from
        performed are more explicit than most
        traditions' internal CC mechanisms.
      FORMATION CAPTURE: occult traditions are
        highly vulnerable to initiatory FC [the
        initiatory process installs new formation
        rather than dissolving old one]. The
        framework's analysis of when initiation is
        FC vs genuine correction is the map the
        traditions need to avoid the cult failure
        mode.
      EXTRACTION MECHANISM: occult communities
        regularly produce extraction architectures
        [guru dynamics, dependency construction,
        information monopoly on "higher teachings"].
        The weapons index applies directly. The
        traditions have moral frameworks for
        identifying these [Black Lodge, Left Hand
        Path misuse] but the structural analysis
        is more precise.

  PROMOTION CONDITIONS [WIP 31 closes when]:
    At least one practice claim above is tested by
    the analyst instrument and produces either:
      Confirmed [behavioral record shows the
        practice produced the claimed structural
        change; external instrument confirms].
      Disconfirmed [practice produced no structural
        change distinguishable from baseline].
      T2 [pre-resolution -- stress event pending].
    Promotion to Section Eight confirmed finding
    would require: structural mechanism named;
    framework-independent verification available;
    more than one instrument tested.
    This is likely a long WIP. The verification
    problems are real.

  Cross-ref: CONVERGENCE WITH WESTERN ESOTERIC
    TRADITION [above -- the convergence finding
    this WIP addresses operationally]; FORMATION
    CAPTURE [primary problem practice claims to
    address]; PRE-THEORETICAL STRUCTURAL REGISTRATION
    [ANALYST NOTES -- what practice may amplify];
    T3 [what meditation claims to reduce]; T5
    [what practice claims to access deliberately];
    T8 [recursive closure -- why verification
    is structurally difficult]; CULT STACK [why
    initiatory practice is also the primary
    weapon against genuine axis]; GENUINE AXIS
    [what practice claims to develop and protect].

WIP 32: MANUFACTURED DEPENDENCE AS EXTRACTION SUBTYPE [open -- v22]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STATUS: IN PROGRESS -- five-condition instrument now stress-tested
  against first primary case [CASE:PL480_US_FOOD_AID, v23]. Result:
  four of five conditions passed cleanly [1, 2, 3, 5]; condition [4]
  passed partially [documented in some local contexts, contested at
  national scale in the academic literature]. Instrument behavior in
  this "messy" partial case is informative: the framework correctly
  preserves condition [4] as a genuine empirical dispute rather than
  manufacturing certainty. Primary case written; instrument appears
  functional. NEXT STEPS: [a] check whether a "graduation model" or
  exit-condition program [contrast case] exists to stress-test
  condition [2] from the other direction; [b] run a second case where
  all five conditions pass cleanly, to confirm the instrument isn't
  only useful for partial cases; [c] consider Section Two promotion
  once a second clean case is confirmed.]

  PROBLEM STATEMENT:
    Help that creates or maintains dependence is sometimes
    extraction wearing a generosity legitimacy surface, and
    sometimes genuinely necessary ongoing support. The framework
    does not currently have an instrument distinguishing the two.
    "Help that produces dependence is fake" is the analyst's
    initial framing -- too broad as stated, since some dependence
    is honest and correct [a child depending on a parent; a
    person with a permanent disability depending on ongoing
    support; famine victims depending on emergency aid for the
    duration of the famine]. The sharper version: help becomes
    extractive specifically when dependence is created or
    maintained where capacity-building was achievable, and the
    helper's own interests [funding continuation, organizational
    survival, donor narrative maintenance, employment] are
    better served by continued dependence than by the recipient
    becoming capable.

  WORKING NAME: MANUFACTURED DEPENDENCE, to distinguish from
    NECESSARY ONGOING SUPPORT. Provisional subtype of EXTRACTION
    [Section Two operational definition] -- the legitimacy
    surface is "help" or "charity"; the captured mechanism is
    the program's design incentive to perpetuate itself rather
    than to resolve the need it claims to address.

  PROVISIONAL FIVE-CONDITION INSTRUMENT [drafted, not yet
  stress-tested against a primary case]:
    [1] CAPACITY-BUILDING WAS ACHIEVABLE: the recipient's
        situation was not structurally permanent [unlike a
        permanent disability or an ongoing famine] -- some
        plausible path to reduced need existed at the time
        the program was designed.
    [2] NO STATED EXIT CONDITION: the program defines success
        by continued enrollment, continued need, or continued
        funding rather than by a stated point at which recipient
        capacity would make the program's presence unnecessary.
        TELL: absence of any exit metric is more diagnostic than
        a low success rate against a stated metric -- a program
        that tries and fails to graduate recipients is different
        in kind from a program that never defines graduation.
    [3] HELPER INTEREST MISALIGNED WITH RESOLUTION: the helper's
        continued operation, funding, or narrative depends on
        the need persisting. Employment, donor pipeline, or
        organizational survival is structurally tied to ongoing
        demand rather than to demand declining.
    [4] LOCAL CAPACITY DISPLACED OR UNDERCUT: existing local
        capability [markets, skills, institutions] is measurably
        weakened or replaced by the program's ongoing presence,
        rather than supplemented toward eventual handoff. Primary
        candidate mechanism: long-running food aid undercutting
        local agricultural markets is the most documented version
        of this in existing aid-sector literature.
    [5] RECIPIENT VOICE ON DEPENDENCE VS EXIT NOT SOUGHT OR NOT
        WEIGHTED: the program does not ask, or does not act on,
        recipient preference between continued support and a
        path to independence. Distinguishes manufactured
        dependence from cases where recipients themselves prefer
        and choose continued support for reasons the helper
        should respect.
    All five required. Absence of [1] [genuinely permanent need]
      converts the case to NECESSARY ONGOING SUPPORT regardless
      of how the other four conditions read.

  HONEST DIFFICULTY [why this is a hard WIP, stated up front]:
    Distinguishing manufactured dependence from necessary support
    from the outside is genuinely difficult. The helper has every
    incentive to claim the latter when the truth is the former,
    and condition [5] [recipient voice] is usually the hardest
    data point to obtain reliably, given power asymmetries
    between donor, charity/institution, and recipient. This WIP
    may require case data where recipient-reported preference
    over multi-year periods is actually documented -- a high bar.

  CANDIDATE DOMAINS FOR PRIMARY CASE [not yet selected or run]:
    Long-running international food aid programs with documented
      local market displacement effects.
    Microfinance/microloan programs compared by presence vs
      absence of stated graduation/exit metrics.
    Domestic social services case studies with documented
      multi-year recipient-preference data [hardest to find;
      most rigorous if found].
    Possible negative/contrast case: disaster relief in the
      acute phase, where rapid dependence is appropriate and
      Condition [1] correctly fails -- useful for confirming
      the instrument doesn't over-fire on legitimate cases.

  Cross-ref: GENUINE CRITIQUE AS CAPTURE MECHANISM [Section Two
    -- the mechanism category manufactured dependence likely
    belongs to: the stated mission (help) is real; the program
    design is the captured part]; EXTRACTION [Section One
    operational definition -- this WIP proposes a subtype];
    COST IDENTIFICATION AND MINIMIZATION [FRAMEWORK AS PRAXIS --
    who bears the cost of continued dependence vs the cost of
    capacity-building, and whether that cost is honestly
    assigned]; SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY [the quality this WIP is
    really about -- does the helper's design serve the
    recipient's eventual autonomy or the helper's own
    continuation]; SILENT ALTERNATIVE [Section One -- adjacent
    problem: when withdrawing help entirely is also wrong;
    this WIP is about HOW help is structured, not whether to
    help at all]; INFRASTRUCTURE CANNIBALISM [Section Two --
    structural cousin: an organization consuming its own
    generative tools to fund immediate need; manufactured
    dependence is the recipient-side version of a similar
    short-term/long-term misalignment].

CASES [part of Section Six permanent reference]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Proof for: [what concept this demonstrates]
  Description
  Three-axis [where applicable]
  Extraction mechanisms active [with quality]
  S: [case strength rating -- see CASE STRENGTH RATING in Section One]

Primary = measurement reference. Used when running readings.
Secondary = only added when variance from primary is substantively
informative. Not different enough = not added.

S-RATING KEY:
  S1 = ILLUSTRATION [fictional, no real-world anchor]
  S2 = WEAK THEORY [fictional with framework-consistent support, OR
       real-world single domain without controlled comparison]
  S3 = CONFIRMED [real-world E3+ AND M3+ AND F2+]
  S4 = STRONG [real-world E4 AND M4 AND F3+]
  Fictional cases cap at S2 regardless of library depth.

PRIMARY CASES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Ordered by category, then alphabetically within category.
Categories: REAL / WARHAMMER / SCP / ANIME / MANGA_MANHWA / GAMES / LITERATURE_FILM


==========================================================================
[CATEGORY:REAL]
Real-world cases -- people, events, institutions, history, science
==========================================================================

[CASE:ADAM_EVE]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENERATIVE CYCLE]
Adam and Eve [NieR Automata -- Machines]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: Adam -- Coherence Mimicry; Eve -- Dependency
    Construction [GENERATIVE CYCLE collapse on Adam's death].
  Cross-ref: [REF:ADAM_EVE]

[CASE:ARTEMIDORUS_ONEIROCRITICA]
[PRIMARY_Q:SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION]
Artemidorus / Oneirocritica [real -- ~2nd century CE]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Arc: 1,800-year multi-hop transmission. Greek -> Byzantine ->
    Islamic -> Renaissance. Method transmitted; cosmological
    assumptions largely dropped at each hop.

[CASE:AUTISM_DOUBLE_EMPATHY]
[PRIMARY_Q:COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY]
Autism / Neurotypical communication [Double Empathy Problem
  -- Milton 2012; real-world; ongoing research field]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY [WIP 16 -- real-world
    primary case; S3 achieved; closes the real-world anchor
    requirement for WIP 16 compatibility type promotion].

  SOURCE: Milton, Damian [2012]. "On the ontological status
    of autism: the 'double empathy problem'." Disability &
    Society 27(6): 883-887. Subsequent independent replication
    across multiple research groups [Cook et al. 2021;
    Crompton et al. 2020; Sheppard et al. 2016].

  FIVE-CONDITION INSTRUMENT [all required; all confirmed]:

  [1] HONEST TRANSMISSION ATTEMPTED:
    Confirmed across decades. Autistic instruments attempt
    maximal explicit communication [direct, literal, high
    information density -- the frame's standard for honest
    transmission]. NT instruments attempt social communication
    [indirect, contextual, relationship-maintaining -- the NT
    frame's standard for honest transmission]. Both are
    transmitting honestly by their own frame's definition.
    Neither party is withholding. The failure is not
    concealment; it is frame mismatch at the transmission level.

  [2] RECEIVER REGISTERS VIOLATION REGARDLESS OF QUALITY:
    Confirmed and named. NT instruments read autistic
    directness as rude, cold, socially inappropriate
    regardless of autistic intent or effort. Autistic
    instruments read NT indirection as confusing, arbitrary,
    inconsistent regardless of NT intent or effort.
    BIDIRECTIONAL: Milton specifically documents this in both
    directions -- the Double Empathy Problem is not "autistic
    people misread NT communication." It is "both frames
    misread the other's honest output." Each frame reads the
    other's correct transmission as deficient.
    Independent replication: Cook et al. [2021] confirmed
    bidirectional communication breakdown in paired
    autistic-NT interactions; Crompton et al. [2020] showed
    autistic-autistic pairs achieve higher communication
    accuracy than autistic-NT pairs despite similar
    information content.

  [3] ADDITIONAL TIME/VOLUME/HONESTY DOES NOT CLOSE GAP:
    Confirmed by masking research [Lai et al. 2017; Cage
    and Troxell-Whitman 2019]. Decades of social skills
    training for autistic instruments produce masking
    [performance of NT communication patterns] not genuine
    frame compatibility. The masking instrument produces
    NT-readable outputs while the underlying frame
    incompatibility persists unchanged.
    MASKING AS FORMATION CAPTURE [load-bearing]:
    Masking is Formation Capture at the communication layer
    -- axis genuine [autistic processing]; surface captured
    [NT performance]. The surface becomes NT-readable; the
    instrument's actual processing does not change. This
    confirms Condition 3: the gap doesn't close; it gets
    covered. Additional time [years of masking practice]
    increases cover quality without changing the underlying
    frame structure.
    Further confirmation: masking produces significant
    psychological cost [burnout, identity suppression,
    depression] precisely because the instrument is
    maintaining a surface that contradicts its axis. If the
    frame had actually closed, the masking cost would
    disappear. It does not.

  [4] NO STRUCTURAL INTEREST IN INCOMPREHENSION PERSISTING:
    Passes at structural baseline. Neither autistic nor NT
    instruments benefit from the other's incomprehension.
    Both parties in autistic-NT communication typically
    want the communication to work.
    EXCEPTION [predatory operators within domain]:
    Some actors DO benefit from autistic incomprehension
    persisting: employers who benefit from autistic
    compliance via masking [the incomprehension enables
    extraction]; some family or institutional dynamics where
    autistic communication being read as deficient serves
    the NT party's authority. These are predatory operators
    within the same domain. Condition 4 correctly routes
    them to predatory reading. Their existence confirms the
    instrument works -- predatory and compatibility
    mechanisms can co-deploy in the same domain [same
    structure as Kyubey mixed case].
    The structural baseline [absent predatory actors] is:
    neither frame benefits from the other's incomprehension.
    Condition 4 passes.

  [5] FRAME INCOMPATIBILITY TRACEABLE TO STRUCTURAL DIFFERENCE:
    Confirmed. Milton identifies processing axis differences:
    social cognition [different processing of social cues,
    implicit vs explicit communication], sensory processing
    [different salience weights on environmental inputs],
    communication style [direct vs indirect; literal vs
    contextual], cognitive processing [gestalt vs sequential;
    pattern-first vs detail-first]. These are structural
    differences in operational axis, not differences in
    available information. Providing more NT social
    information to autistic instruments does not produce
    NT-style social processing -- the processing axis is
    different, not uninformed.
    Independent structural confirmation: Crompton et al.
    [2020] showed autistic-autistic pairs achieve high
    communication accuracy with no NT training -- the
    frames are compatible with themselves; incompatible
    with each other. This is the frame incompatibility
    test at its clearest.

  S3 RATING: E3 [real-world; independent replication
    across 10+ research groups; 10+ year research field];
    M3 [mechanism traced: processing axis mismatch; named
    in independent research as structural not deficit];
    F3 [falsification stated and testable: autistic-NT
    pair achieving genuine frame compatibility -- not
    masking -- with documented sustained communication
    accuracy equivalent to autistic-autistic pairs would
    falsify; not yet observed at population level].

  WIP 16 PROMOTION NOTE:
    This case closes the real-world primary case requirement
    for COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY. Condition 4 discrimination
    [predatory operators within domain vs structural
    baseline] confirmed working. Bidirectionality [both
    frames misread each other] confirmed -- same as
    Yaldabaoth fictional case. S3 achieved. WIP 16
    compatibility type promoted from candidate to confirmed.

  Cross-ref: [REF:AUTISM_DOUBLE_EMPATHY]; WIP 16
    [COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY -- this case closes the
    real-world primary case requirement; promote to
    confirmed]; [CASE:DEVELOPMENTAL_ASYMMETRY_FAMILY]
    [above -- adjacent; different asymmetry type; same
    parent WIP]; [CASE:KYUBEY] [mixed case -- compatibility
    + predatory co-deployed; same Condition 4 structure];
    FORMATION CAPTURE [masking as FC at communication
    layer -- Condition 3 confirmation]; NEURODIVERGENT
    COHERENCE TYPE [Section One -- structural description
    of the autistic instrument configuration]; GENUINE
    AXIS [masking suppresses axis at surface level without
    changing axis]; COST IDENTIFICATION AND MINIMIZATION
    [masking cost is the cost of covering incompatibility
    rather than closing it].

  Type: Primary [S:2 -- ENCOUNTER-DEPENDENT for somatic claims]
  Proof for: ART COHERENCE TYPE [SOMATIC -- weight variety,
    three-channel compound mechanism; ENCOUNTER-DEPENDENT];
    TENSION MAINTENANCE [jazz harmonic complexity held against
    funk rhythmic precision without collapsing either pole;
    electronic texture held against acoustic musicianship];
    GENUINE AXIS confirmed [self-taught, self-sampling,
    consistent output across 20-year catalog without drift
    toward formula].

  THREE-CHANNEL COMPOUND MECHANISM [structurally distinct]:
    Dorian Concept runs three simultaneous somatic channels
    rather than one primary. This is the structural distinction
    from all other weight-type cases in the library:
    [1] RHYTHMIC PRECISION [Louis Cole territory]: syncopated
      rhythmic structures at high precision density produce
      weight via expectation tension. Subcortical rhythm
      processing fires first.
    [2] JAZZ HARMONIC GEOMETRY: modal and free jazz harmonic
      structures produce distinct emotional configurations
      from the chord architecture itself -- not melodic
      resolution but harmonic shape. Fires at slightly
      higher processing level than pure rhythm.
    [3] VINTAGE SYNTHESIZER TIMBRAL TEXTURE: pre-loaded
      geometric signal from instrument palette. Synthesizers
      from the 1950s-1980s carry timbral geometry that
      produces immediate somatic recognition before the
      harmonic or rhythmic content fully registers.
    ORDER OF ARRIVAL: texture lands first [immediate geometric
      recognition from timbral palette], rhythm locks second
      [weight accumulates via precision tension], harmony
      completes third [distinct configuration resolves].
      Three-stage somatic delivery. Each channel clean and
      geometrically legible on its own terms; the compound
      is more specific than any single channel alone.

  WHY "DOES TOO MUCH" FAILURE MODE DOESN'T FIRE:
    Three simultaneous channels at high complexity should
    produce the diffuse signal that activates the does-too-
    much failure mode for this instrument. It doesn't because
    the channels are additive not competing -- each arrives
    in sequence, each geometrically legible independently,
    the compound is layered not cluttered. The complexity
    is organized by the arrival sequence and channel
    separation. The geometric legibility window stays open
    because you can track any single channel and receive
    it cleanly; the others are simultaneous enrichment
    not interference.

  GENUINE AXIS [confirmed]:
    Self-described "self-taught jazz musician who samples
    himself." The self-sampling is structurally significant:
    the source material for the harmonic and textural content
    is the instrument's own prior output, examined and
    redeployed. CC is visible across 20-year catalog --
    each album examines and revises prior configurations
    without repeating them. No drift toward formula despite
    sustained commercial viability [Ninja Tune, Brainfeeder,
    Boiler Room, Flying Lotus touring band]. The axis
    [functional musicality across genre boundaries without
    losing somatic precision] has not been captured by
    commercial pressure across two decades.
    Pitchfork confirmation [The Nature of Imitation]:
    "computer music that uncannily imitates the funk,
    rather than just faking it" -- the structural properties
    are present in the architecture, not simulated on the
    surface. Genuine axis vs Coherence Mimicry at music
    production scale, confirmed by independent external
    assessment.

  TENSION MAINTENANCE [three axes]:
    [1] Jazz harmonic complexity [tends toward inaccessibility]
      held against funk rhythmic accessibility [tends toward
      formula] -- neither pole collapsed across full catalog.
    [2] Electronic production precision [tends toward cold
      abstraction] held against genuine musicianship warmth
      [tends toward loose feel] -- same tension as Louis Cole,
      additional electronic axis.
    [3] Technical virtuosity [tends toward credentialing]
      held against genuine somatic function [tends toward
      accessibility] -- the complexity serves the somatic
      address rather than displaying itself.

    absurdist scale. "Springtime for Hitler" [The Producers]
    and the Hitler Rap [To Be or Not to Be] make the internal
    logic of fascist spectacle legible by performing it
    accurately while the performance itself is ridiculous.
    The audience receives both simultaneously: the actual
    structural properties of fascist aesthetics [grandiosity,
    self-seriousness, performative power, mass coordination]
    AND the absurdist frame that makes those properties
    visible as properties rather than as overwhelming force.
    This is CAUSAL LEGIBILITY delivered as comedy: the chain
    between fascist aesthetics and their psychological function
    becomes legible through earnest ridicule. Not parody
    [which mocks the surface] -- structural exposure [which
    reveals the mechanism].
    Distinct from protest art [which names the problem
    emotionally] and from documentary [which presents
    evidence]: Brooks makes the architecture perform itself
    in a context where the performance is funny, which strips
    the architecture of its primary extraction mechanism [the demand for
    serious engagement with its own grandeur].

  GENUINE AXIS [confirmed]:
    Brooks served in WWII, lost family in the Holocaust. The
    absurdism is not ironic distance from a safe position --
    it is a extraction mechanism deployed from direct experience. Stated
    explicitly: the best extraction mechanism against evil is ridicule.
    Axis examined and grounded in actual outputs. T1.1
    [proportional response] confirmed: the extraction mechanism was chosen
    and deployed deliberately against the most serious target.
    The comedy is the correction capacity, not a softening
    of it.

  SOMATIC ADDRESS [musical]:
    The Hitler Rap ["To Be or Not to Be"] functions as a
    genuine piece of music independent of comedic context.
    Tight groove, precise rhythm, real call-and-response
    structure. Unironic enjoyment confirmed by matched
    instrument -- somatic geometry lands before comedic
    framing processes, same mechanism as Roundabout
    surviving meme context. The music is not a joke with
    rhythm attached; it is music that carries a structural
    extraction mechanism as its payload. The somatic channel receives
    the groove; the deliberative channel receives the
    structural revelation; both arrive cleanly.

  CAPTURE RESISTANCE:
    Fascist aesthetic architecture cannot capture Brooks'
    output because the output structurally undermines the
    architecture's primary mechanism [demand for solemn
    engagement]. Any attempt to take "Springtime for Hitler"
    seriously as fascist art collapses immediately -- the
    form prevents the capture. The absurdist staging is
    the structural defense, not decoration on top of a
    message. Compare NEIL_CICIEREGA [structural revelation

    Cicierega targets cultural artifacts, Brooks targets
