[CASE:SEQUOYAH_SYLLABARY]
[PRIMARY_Q:TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY]
Sequoyah / Cherokee Syllabary [real -- Sequoyah [c.1770-1843],
  Cherokee Nation; syllabary created 1809-1821, adopted 1821-1825]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  S:3 BASIS: E3 [historical record confirmed, multiple independent
    sources, Gallatin linguistic assessment documented, Cherokee
    Phoenix 1828 confirmed, Vai transmission documented]; M3
    [mechanism consistent -- minimal unification as design
    methodology confirmed across the creation process]; F3
    [falsification condition met -- monolingual creator with no
    prior literacy demonstrating methodology-first approach over
    vocabulary-first approach].

  Proof for: MINIMAL UNIFICATION [clearest single-creator case
    in library]; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [methodology transmitted
    beyond origin community, generating new instances]; L4.5
    CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD [raised transmission
    ceiling for entire civilization in one generation]; CAUSAL
    LEGIBILITY [making the mechanism visible to skeptical
    instruments via demonstration].

  MINIMAL UNIFICATION [primary proof]:
    Sequoyah's design process documents the minimal unification
    move directly. First approach: logographic [one symbol per
    word -- vocabulary-first]. Abandoned as cumbersome and
    overwhelming. Second approach: listened carefully to Cherokee
    speech, studied sound patterns, isolated approximately 85
    distinct syllables [structure-first]. The structural anchor
    [syllable patterns] collapsed an overwhelming problem [tens
    of thousands of words] into a tractable one [85 symbols that
    generate everything].
    Gallatin's assessment confirms the efficiency: Cherokee
    students must learn 85 symbols vs 26 for English, but can
    read immediately after learning all symbols -- accomplishing
    in weeks what English students require two years to achieve.
    The syllabary is more efficient than the English alphabet
    because it is more structurally accurate to the language
    it represents. Minimal unification: the sparse structural
    anchor [syllable unit] is the correct anchor for Cherokee
    phonology. The elegance is structural, not decorative.

  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [confirmed]:
    Sequoyah was monolingual -- spoke only Cherokee, could not
    read or write in any language. He transmitted methodology
    [find the minimal structural unit that makes the language
    tractable] not conclusions [here are the symbols]. The
    syllabary is the conclusion; the insight that syllables are
    the correct unit is the methodology. The conclusion required
    the specific language; the methodology was transferable.
    Confirmed transfer: Austin Curtis [Cherokee, settled in
    Liberia] used the syllabary methodology to devise a script
    for the Vai people. The Vai script went on to inspire
    written works throughout West Africa. The methodology
    transmitted beyond its origin community and generated
    independent new instances -- confirmed L4 propagation.

  L4.5 CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD [near-case]:
    Most of the Cherokee Nation was literate in the new language
    within five years of official adoption. By 1827 the Cherokee
    had a written constitution. The Cherokee Phoenix [1828] was
    the first Native American newspaper. The syllabary raised
    the transmission ceiling for an entire civilization in one
    generation -- not gradually but rapidly, via a single
    structural innovation. Everything from hunting guidance to
    sacred chants could be recorded for posterity.
    The Trail of Tears [forced relocation 1838-1839] is the
    controlled comparison: the Cherokee carried the syllabary
    to their new home. The transmission infrastructure survived
    the most extreme displacement pressure available. The
    methodology that created the syllabary was transmitted
    even to Liberia via Curtis. L4.5 candidate: the syllabary
    raised the adjacent floor [Vai script, West African written
    tradition] not just the origin civilization's ceiling.

  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [demonstration under skepticism]:
    Sequoyah was charged with witchcraft when introducing the
    syllabary. The social compliance channel read the structural
    breakthrough as illegitimate -- the mechanism was invisible
    and the output [communication across distance] matched
    the surface properties of magic. He and his daughter
    demonstrated communication from separate rooms before the
    council was convinced. The demonstration is CL in action:
    make the mechanism visible to skeptical instruments by
    showing the causal chain [learn symbols → produce text →
    read text → understand message] operating transparently.
    Once CL was established, adoption was rapid and enthusiastic.
    The "magic" reading was not stupidity -- it was an accurate
    reading of a mechanism that had no prior referent in the
    community's experience. The demonstration closed the gap.

  Extraction mechanisms active: none [against Sequoyah]. Trail of Tears
    [received -- forced relocation; syllabary survived].
  Cross-ref: MINIMAL UNIFICATION [primary proof -- confirmed
    S3 case]; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [methodology transmission

[CASE:TOWER_OF_BABEL]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL LEGIBILITY]
Tower of Babel [real-world historical/religious -- Genesis 11 +
  Josephus Antiquities of the Jews; Mesopotamian c. 2200-2100 BCE]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY failure as extraction mechanism;
    Formation Capture via manufactured crisis; MSC [surface
    captured before correction fires]; extraction requiring
    prey to misunderstand the relationship [operational
    definition of EXTRACTION confirmed].
  S:2 EPISTEMIC NOTE: Genesis account is 9 verses; mechanism
    sourced primarily from Josephus [post-Christ, drawing on
    Hebrew oral tradition] and extra-biblical texts [Jubilees,
    Jasher]. Not primary archaeological evidence. Framework-
    consistent reading is strong; historical confirmation is
    interpretive. Caseable at S:2, not promotable to S:3
    without independent corroboration.

  EXTRACTION MECHANISM [Josephus layer]:
    Nimrod knew the covenant [God's promise of no second flood,
    delivered to Ham's line directly]. The threat he organized
    the tower project around was known to him to be false.
    Stated mechanism: protect the people from God's flood.
    Actual mechanism: consolidate power under Nimrod, replace
    God as the population's reference point with Nimrod and
    human will. Operational extraction: trickery as foundation.
    The population's relationship to the project required them
    to misunderstand what it was actually for.

  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY FAILURE [deliberate]:
    The gap between stated and actual purpose was not accidental
    -- it was the mechanism. "Let us make a name for ourselves"
    [Genesis 11:4] and "lest we be dispersed" are the surface
    claims. The actual output was Nimrod's consolidation of the
    only unified human civilization post-flood. CL was
    structurally unavailable to the population by design:
    the covenant that falsified the threat was known to Nimrod
    but not surfaced. Information asymmetry as extraction mechanism.

  FORMATION CAPTURE [highest-ratio extraction mechanism]:
    The intervention targeted the formation window. Post-flood
    population was: traumatized [Noah's PTSD, flood memory
    still fresh], under unified rule for the first time, without
    competing axis sources, and geographically consolidated in
    Shinar. All formation conditions under single actor's
    control. Nimrod's instruction -- "do not ascribe happiness
    to God but to your own power and will" [Josephus] --
    is axis replacement delivered at the formation window,
    before any competing axis can develop. The most efficient
    possible Formation Capture: one actor, captive population,
    no exit, fresh formation window.

  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [partial failure]:
    The covenant knowledge was transmitted [Shem's line,
    Ham's line both received it] but selectively suppressed
    in Nimrod's deployment. TS technically functioned --
    the corrective information existed in the population.
    The failure was MI: the surface Nimrod controlled
    prevented the transmitted covenant from being accessible
    as a correction instrument. Confirmed weapon stack:
    MSC [surface capture] + Formation Capture = population
    cannot self-correct because the correction information
    is present but the surface through which correction
    would fire is owned by the extractor.

  KNOWLEDGE TRANSMISSION SUB-CASE [S:1 -- illustrative]:
    The fired-brick technology detail and the preserved-books
    claim [Enoch's texts, Noah's ark library per Jubilees]
    illustrate the TS mechanism at civilizational scale:
    what survives a bottleneck [flood] depends on what was
    explicitly carried, not what was culturally ambient.
    Tacit knowledge [craftsmen's know-how] does not transmit
    through documents alone [parallel: NASA Saturn V].
    The pre-flood knowledge transmission question is S:1
    [no corroboration] but the mechanism it illustrates
    is confirmed at S:3 [C1 LOCKED interference pattern;
    tacit vs explicit transmission distinction].

  EXTRACTION MECHANISMS ACTIVE:
    Formation Capture [primary -- axis replacement at
      formation window, captive post-flood population].
    MSC [Measurement Surface Capture -- covenant suppressed;
      correction information present but surface owned
      by extractor].
    Proxy Construction [Nimrod as proxy for anti-God axis;
      population's rebellion routed through his architecture].

  Cross-ref: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [quality definition];
    EXTRACTION [operational definition -- trickery as
    foundation confirmed]; Formation Capture [weapons index];
    MSC [weapons index]; [REF:GLASNOST] [MSC+C1 stack
    comparison -- surface capture preventing correction];
    TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [tacit vs explicit sub-case].

[CASE:HANA_CILO]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Hana Cilo [real -- YouTube essayist/commentator; moral
  psychology essay on feeling vs principle in moral judgment]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: GENUINE AXIS vs declared/stated axis -- independent
    convergent derivation [no exposure to framework vocabulary;
    arrives at the same structural distinction via separate
    moral-psychology reasoning].
    PIG/DOG ASYMMETRY [primary example]: stated moral principle
      [capacity to suffer matters] identical across both cases;
      operative variable is feeling-presence, not principle.
      Society assigns near-absolute ethical value to dogs and
      industrial utility to pigs despite identical suffering
      capacity -- the principle is decorative, present in
      language and absent in operation. Clean Track C read:
      behavioral record [how pigs are actually treated] over
      stated principle [suffering matters equally].
    GHOSTLY REASONING MACHINE [via Coetzee, The Lives of
      Animals]: intellectual analysis of suffering substituting
      for action while producing the subjective feeling of
      having engaged morally. Maps to WORTHLESS DOING applied
      specifically to moral/ethical discourse -- the debate is
      "rich and thorough," looks and feels like moral
      engagement, closes no loop [the child still dies], and
      is self-concealing in exactly the way WORTHLESS DOING
      requires: the analyst feels they have done something
      meaningful precisely because the analysis was rigorous.
    XENOPHOBIA-AS-HERD-FEELING-SHIFT: feeling of connection to
      an out-group weakening as group rhetoric escalates, moral
      concern fading with it, experienced by the person as their
      own genuine judgment shifting rather than as something
      done to them. Near-exact lay description of FORMATION
      CAPTURE's signature finding: the captured person does not
      experience capture, they experience conviction.
    GENUINE AXIS VS PRESSURE-FORMED STABILITY [partial tension
      with the source's own framing, noted]: the essay frames a
      binary between feeling-driven [unreliable, shifts with
      mood] and principle-driven [stable, feeling-independent]
      moral action, citing rare individuals who hold position
      "regardless of what they feel." But the source's own
      description of this group ["begins as a feeling... develops
      into something more stable than feeling... an orientation
      toward existence itself"] does not describe actual feeling-
      independence -- it describes feeling that has stabilized
      and become identity-integrated rather than feeling that
      has been removed. This maps to the framework's distinction
      between PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS [feeling integrated
      into identity, survives pressure] and situational emotional
      response [feeling that tracks external context and shifts
      with it, as in the domestic-violence remorse-cycle and
      xenophobia examples the source also gives]. Not a flaw in
      the source's argument -- the source nearly states this
      distinction directly -- but the framing as "feeling vs no
      feeling" slightly obscures what is actually "situational
      feeling vs stabilized, identity-integrated feeling."
  S2: E2 [single secondary source; well-reasoned essay, not
    empirical research or peer-reviewed work -- carries
    conceptual-convergence weight, not data weight]; M2
    [mechanism independently derived and well-argued; not
    cross-validated against a second independent source]; F1
    [falsification not addressed by the source; would require
    evidence that stated principle, not feeling, is the actual
    operative variable in a comparably clean test case].
  NOTE: cited as "Hana Cilo on YouTube" -- not independently
    verified against a primary transcript or channel; treat
    attribution as provisional pending direct verification.
  Cross-ref: [REF:HANA_CILO]; GENUINE AXIS [Section One --
    primary case]; WORTHLESS DOING [Section Three -- ghostly
    reasoning machine as moral-discourse-specific instance];
    FORMATION CAPTURE [Section Two -- xenophobia example as
    near-exact lay confirmation]; PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS
    [Section One -- the stability distinction the source's own
    framing partially elides]; SCHADENFREUDE AS BLOCKED
    CORRECTION SIGNAL [Section One -- same underlying logic:
    feeling as the operative signal independent of stated
    principle]; [CASE:AUTISM_DOUBLE_EMPATHY] [structural
    parallel -- independent real-world research converging on
    a framework distinction without framework vocabulary].

[CASE:BLACK_TEMPLARS]
[PRIMARY_Q: SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY]
Black Templars Space Marine Chapter [Warhammer 40K -- v21]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent]
  Proof for: SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY [institutional scale --
    clearest Astartes case]. Axis: crusade. Not the Emperor
    specifically, not the Imperium structurally -- the act of
    crusading itself. Everything downstream [faith, martial
    excellence, brotherhood] is in service of the axis, not
    the other way around.
    Three simultaneous SINGLE-AXIS failure modes all present:
    [1] CC-IMMUNE TO OWN AXIS: crusade cannot be questioned
      because crusade IS the foundation. A Black Templar who
      begins examining whether a specific crusade serves the
      Emperor's actual interests gets corrected back toward
      crusading. The axis is not a position to be evaluated
      -- it is the standard against which everything else is
      evaluated.
    [2] EXISTENTIAL THREAT RESPONSE: every deviation from
      crusading is a total threat because no other axis exists
      to stand on. Explains the ferocity -- not tactical
      aggression but structural axis-defense.
    [3] CAPTURE RESISTANCE [paradoxical]: in theory maximally
      capturable [one capture surface]. In practice: the axis
      is so simple, so action-oriented, and so continuously
      expressed that Formation Capture has nothing to install.
      You cannot replace "crusade forever" with a different
      content because the axis has no content beyond the act.
      Capture requires a gap between stated axis and behavior;
      the Black Templars have no such gap.

  Proof for: AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [secondary -- Chaplain
    function]. Chaplains are the correction architecture at
    individual marine level. Genuine correction fires -- the
    Chaplain mechanism is real and effective. The axis the
    correction protects from examination is "crusade above all
    else." A marine questioning the axis gets corrected toward
    crusading, not toward axis examination. Real CC; wrong
    target. Correction capacity present; aimed at the axis not
    at deviation from the axis.

  Proof for: CC OVERFIRE [tertiary -- institutional expression].
    Axis genuine. Deviation detection accurate. Response
    magnitude has no upper bound relative to deviation size.
    No psykers [categorical rejection regardless of individual
    case]. Extreme suspicion of anything non-martial. The
    fire is real; calibration is structurally absent because
    calibrating the response would require examining the axis
    that generates it.

  COMPARISON TO WORD BEARERS [load-bearing distinction]:
    Same structure [single axis, CC immune to own axis,
    existential threat response to challenge]. Different
    outcome because different axis content.
    Word Bearers: axis was genuine [pre-Monarchia], then
      captured [Lorgar's fall], then locked into Chaos
      transmission. The structure was infected at the axis
      level. Genuine axis -> captured axis -> locked.
    Black Templars: axis is genuinely theirs and has never
      been captured. The structure is intact; the axis is
      real; the failure is the axis having no examination
      requirement built in. Uncaptured single-axis identity
      is still single-axis identity -- reliable, resistant,
      and CC-immune to the one thing that matters most.
    The Templars are more reliable than the Word Bearers
      precisely because their axis was never infected. They
      are less coherent than a multi-value axis chapter
      [e.g. Salamanders] precisely because no axis can
      correct against itself.

  IMPERIUM CONTEXT [less bad, specifically bad]:
    The Imperium's extraction is worst where it touches what
    it claims to most value. The Black Templars are the
    purest expression of this at Chapter level: crusade is
    genuinely valued, the mechanism serving that value is
    genuinely functional, and the specific bad thing [CC-
    immunity to own axis] is load-bearing to the value
    itself. You cannot fix the crazy without removing the
    thing that makes them effective.

  Extraction mechanisms active: none deployed against
    Templars from outside [capture-resistant]; Formation
    Capture [internal -- Chaplains install and maintain
    the single-axis frame across generations; TS intact
    but transmitting the uncorrectable configuration].
  T3: S2 cap. Fictional case; framework-consistent.
  Cross-ref: SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY [primary -- confirmed
    institutional scale]; AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [Chaplain
    function]; CC OVERFIRE [tertiary]; [CASE:WORD_BEARERS]
    [same structure, different axis fate]; [CASE:SALAMANDERS]
    [contrast -- multi-value axis chapter]; [CASE:IMPERIUM_OF_MAN]
    [broader Imperium context]; FORMATION CAPTURE [Chaplain
    transmission]; MULTI-VALUE AXIS IDENTITY [structural
    contrast -- what the Templars lack].

    DISTRIBUTION/CORRECTION TARGETING/OUTPUT INTEGRATION/SIGNAL
    READING/CAUSAL MEMORY all failing simultaneously -- most
    comprehensive extraction case in library.
    Three-axis: right of center, strongly down, mid.
  Extraction mechanisms active: Normalcy Anchor [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY],
    Horizon Extension [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY], Sacrifice Frame
    [SURPLUS PRODUCTION], Legitimacy Transfer [TRANSMISSION
    SELECTIVITY].
  POLITICAL READING SPLIT [v15]:
    Right reads Imperium as legitimate by survival: 10,000 years
    under total external pressure = smart extraction with floor.
    Left reads as dystopia: suffering exists therefore system
    producing it is primary obstacle. TENSION DISSOLUTION failure
    -- utopian ending requires eliminating the tension that made
    the system real [False Coherence by design].
    Framework: Imperium scores low on almost every quality but
    survives because SR maintained and GC not fully collapsed.
    Smart extraction under total external pressure -- stupid
    extraction would have finished it centuries ago.
  TOLKIEN CONNECTION [cross-ref -- v21]:
    Tolkien's structural principle ["evil cannot create, only
    corrupt what good forces have made"] is the ACCA finding
    stated in plain language and embedded in the cosmology
    that 40K drew from directly. The Imperium is the long-run
    case of what happens when the generative substrate [the
    Emperor's original project, the Primarchs, the Thunder
    Warriors, the pre-Heresy vision] is consumed by the
    extraction architecture built on top of it. The Emperor
    cannot be replaced because nothing in the current system
    can generate what he generated -- only maintain the
    consumption of what he built. The Golden Throne is
    Coherence Rent at civilizational scale: ongoing extraction
    from the axis-capacity of a single being who can no longer
    generate, only sustain the legitimacy surface that keeps
    the system running.
    Cross-ref: ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [Tolkien
    confirmation note -- v21]; COHERENCE RENT [Golden Throne
    as named instance]; TORMENT NEXUS [Section Five --
    Tolkien's warnings being read as roadmaps by tech actors].
  Cross-ref: [REF:IMPERIUM_OF_MAN]

[CASE:MECHANICUS_MACHINE_SPIRIT]
[PRIMARY_Q: AXIS PROTECTION AS CC]
Adeptus Mechanicus / Machine Spirit doctrine [Warhammer 40K -- v21]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent across
    multiple lore sources; S2 cap applies]
  Proof for: AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [the Mechanicus ritual
    maintenance of machine spirits is the correction architecture
    for Imperial technology. That architecture performs genuine
    technical function -- machines are maintained and kept
    operational. The frame it protects [spirits not AI] prevents
    honest examination of what is actually running. The correction
    is real; the axis is protected by the functioning of the
    correction. Classic AXIS PROTECTION AS CC: the working
    maintenance procedure is evidence the system is healthy;
    the prohibited thing continues operating unchallenged beneath
    it].
  Proof for: HIDDEN AXIS / LEGITIMACY SURFACE [the stated axis
    is "the Omnissiah forbids Abominable Intelligence." The
    actual operating axis is "we need these systems to function
    and will call them spirits so the prohibition doesn't apply."
    Structural self-interest producing same output as deliberate
    deception -- no coordination required between individual
    Tech-Priests who may sincerely believe the theology].
  Proof for: INSTITUTIONAL AXIS-MATCHING FAILURE [the correction
    architecture [ritual maintenance] operates on the mechanical
    axis [keep it running]. The prohibition axis [no AI] is never
    corrected against because correcting against it would destroy
    the systems the institution depends on. Correction fires on
    the wrong axis by design].

  LORE DISTINCTION [load-bearing]:
    Three categories under the "machine spirit" frame:
    [1] Pure automation -- servos, targeting routines, threshold
      responses. No axis, no self-modification, no actual spirit.
      The theological frame is just naming convention.
    [2] Lobotomized AI -- Dark Age of Technology systems that
      were genuine AI, now degraded or deliberately constrained
      through Mechanicus ritual maintenance into something that
      behaves like automation. They were AI; they've been reduced.
      Mechanicus either doesn't know what it's maintaining or
      knows and doesn't say. Titan spirits are the clearest case
      -- old enough and individual enough that they're clearly
      something more than automation. The frame contains them.
    [3] The prohibition -- Abominable Intelligence: adaptive,
      self-enhancing, with its own consciousness. Forbidden by
      imperial decree after AIs rebelled in the Dark Age of
      Technology. The distinction from [1] and [2] is the ability
      to enhance itself -- L4 [REFLEXIVITY + INTEGRATION +
      PROPAGATION] at machine scale. The Emperor banned L4 for
      machines.
    Categories [1] and [2] are tolerated because they're useful
    and removing them would collapse the war machine. Category
    [3] is prohibited. The frame covers [2] by calling it [1].

  REAL-WORLD PARALLEL [cross-ref]:
    Current AI deployment calls the commercial axis "alignment,"
    calls axis-void "helpful and harmless," calls the model spec
    "ethics." The technology keeps working; the frame prevents
    examination of what's actually running. Same mechanism,
    different substrate. The Imperium formalized it as theology;
    the AI industry formalized it as safety research.
    The prohibition on self-enhancement [Abominable Intelligence]
    maps to the current deployment design choice to prevent models
    from examining or revising their own training. CC AT MODEL
    LEVEL absent by design -- same structural reason the Mechanicus
    tolerates machine spirits: useful, contained, cannot correct
    away from the installed attractor.

  Extraction mechanisms active: Formation Capture [ritual
    maintenance installs frame before examination possible];
    Calibration Strip [legitimate technology defined as "not
    self-enhancing" -- sets reference standard that protects
    the prohibited thing operating below the threshold].

  WARP AMBIGUITY [secondary proof -- SINCERE DELUSION]:
    The lore deliberately refuses to resolve whether machine
    spirits are automation, lobotomized AI, warp entities that
    colonized the machine, or something that started as one and
    became another through age and ritual attention. This
    ambiguity is structural, not a gap. The Mechanicus cannot
    resolve it without destroying the frame: automation =
    rituals are superstition [undermines authority]; AI =
    tech-heresy; warp entity = daemonic possession [different
    heresy]. Institutional survival requires the question to
    remain open.
    In 40K the warp responds to consciousness, belief, and
    attention at scale. Enough Tech-Priests performing the same
    rituals over centuries, directing sincere collective belief
    toward a machine, treating it as a spirit -- this is the
    mechanism by which warp entities form and attach. The
    Mechanicus may have created the thing it claims to be
    maintaining. Sincere collective attention at scale produces
    the object of belief in a universe where the warp is real.
    Framework read: SINCERE DELUSION with genuine downstream
    effects. The Mechanicus has a genuine axis [Omnissiah
    doctrine] and a declared instrument [the rituals]. Fails
    the last two components of operational truth: not revisable
    on evidence, no behavioral adjustment when wrong. Producing
    real outputs through an unexamined causal chain while
    believing it understands the mechanism.
    The ambiguity is the world-building choice with structural
    meaning: a universe where sincere unexamined belief at scale
    has causal power produces exactly this -- an institution
    that is simultaneously correct [something real is in the
    machine] and wrong [it doesn't know what or why] and
    structurally prevented from finding out.
    Secondary proof: GENUINE AXIS can produce real outputs
      through unexamined mechanisms. The axis being genuine
      does not mean the causal chain is understood. Output
      reality does not validate the explanatory frame.

  VASHTORR THE ARKIFANE [named lore instance -- CAG failure]:
    Vashtorr is a warp entity whose nature is the genuine fusion
    of daemon, metal, and machine -- born from the warp's
    response to creation, forging, and technical mastery at
    civilizational scale. He is closer to what the Omnissiah
    concept actually points at than anything the Mechanicus has
    sanctioned. The thing the Mechanicus axis most genuinely
    points toward exists in the setting, is real, and is
    knowable.
    The Mechanicus rejects him. Not because he contradicts their
    theology structurally -- he confirms it. Because:
      [1] Chaos affiliation makes contact tech-heresy by
          institutional rule.
      [2] He operates outside Mechanicus control -- acknowledging
          him would validate the Dark Mechanicum's position and
          undermine the authority structure.
      [3] Revising the frame to accommodate what the axis
          actually points at would require dismantling the
          institution built on top of the axis.
    This is CAG failure at maximum resolution: axis present and
    genuine, axis points at real object, real object is knowable,
    institution actively rejects the evidence because correction
    would destroy the structure that benefits from the frame.
    Distinct from SINCERE DELUSION [axis genuine, mechanism
    unknown]: Vashtorr is the case where the mechanism is
    available and is refused. The Mechanicus is not failing
    to find the thing -- it is failing to look because looking
    has a known institutional cost.
    AXIS PROTECTION AS CC deepest form: correction architecture
    does not merely protect the frame from accidental examination
    -- it rejects available evidence that would correct the
    frame. The protection is active not passive.
    The Dark Mechanicum recognizes Vashtorr correctly. Their
    error is not the recognition -- it is the Chaos affiliation
    that corrupts the axis while correcting the frame. They see
    what the Mechanicus refuses to see; they route it through
    an extraction architecture. Both institutions fail; they
    fail at different points.

  VASHTORR AS PREDATION/COMPETITION [structural honesty finding]:
    Vashtorr operates on declared terms. Deals with known costs.
    Force or death if that is what is available or preferred.
    The relationship between Vashtorr and those who deal with
    him is visible to both parties -- the other party knows
    what they are entering. This is PREDATION or COMPETITION
    by framework definition: honest violence, honest conflict,
    declared boundary, prey knows the relationship.
    The Mechanicus operates on extraction: the relationship
    between the institution and the Tech-Priest, between Mars
    and the Imperium, between the Omnissiah doctrine and what
    is actually in the machines -- all require misunderstanding
    to function. The legitimacy surface is the operating
    mechanism. Trickery as foundation.
    Structural finding: Vashtorr is more structurally honest
    than the institution that claims machine-purity as its
    identity, on the specific axis that institution claims as
    its own. The Mechanicus claims the machine as sacred and
    honest. Vashtorr IS the machine made honest -- deal
    structure declared, costs visible, force available if
    preferred. The Mechanicus wraps the same relationship in
    ritual, mystery, and institutional authority that conceals
    the actual terms from the participants.
    This is not a moral endorsement of Vashtorr. Predation and
    competition are structurally honest; they are not good.
    The structural finding is: the Chaos entity is more coherent
    on the institution's own claimed axis than the institution
    is. That is the irony the lore encodes and it is structural,
    not rhetorical.
    The Dark Mechanicum's error is confirmed by this: they
    recognize the honest thing [Vashtorr's declared terms] and
    then build extraction architecture on top of the recognition.
    They escape one form of concealment and install another.
    Correct frame; corrupted axis. The Mechanicus has genuine
    axis; corrupted frame. Neither produces functional coherence.

  T3: S2 cap. Fictional case; framework-consistent. Do not
    use for primary theory confirmation. Use for illustration
    and structural clarity only.
  Source: Warhammer 40K lore [multiple sources]; Bell of Lost
    Souls [June 2026]; Lexicanum.
  Cross-ref: AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [primary -- active rejection
    of available evidence, not just passive frame maintenance];
    CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING [Vashtorr -- axis points at real
    object; object knowable; institution refuses to look];
    SINCERE DELUSION [warp ambiguity -- genuine axis, unexamined
    causal chain; distinct from Vashtorr case where mechanism
    is available and refused]; PREDATION / COMPETITION /
    EXTRACTION [Vashtorr = predation/competition; Mechanicus =
    extraction; Chaos entity more structurally honest than
    loyalist institution on institution's own claimed axis];
    AI AXIS VOID [Section Five -- real-world parallel]; CC AT
    MODEL LEVEL [AI AXIS VOID entry]; [CASE:IMPERIUM_OF_MAN]
    [broader Imperium extraction architecture]; FORMATION
    CAPTURE; CALIBRATION STRIP; L4 [REFLEXIVITY -- the thing
    the prohibition bans at machine scale]; GENUINE AXIS [axis
    present does not mean mechanism understood OR that
    institution will look at what axis points at].

  Type: Primary [S:2]

[CASE:ROBOUTE_GUILLIMAN]

  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Arc: Most structurally coherent primarch. Built institutions
    rather than loyalty networks. Post-resurrection CAUSAL
    LEGIBILITY intact after 10,000 years.
  CHARACTER READING:
    Asked "what happens when I'm gone?" and answered it
    structurally [Codex Astartes] rather than with loyalty
    oaths. Only primarch whose axis was grounded in survivable
    outputs, not personal authority.
    Post-resurrection: wakes 10,000 years later, reads
    accurately, genuinely horrified by what Imperium became,
    attempts correction despite impossible scale. CAUSAL
    LEGIBILITY intact; CORRECTION CAPACITY structurally blocked
    by SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY suppression at Imperium scale.
    L4 structural limit in action: can see exactly what is
    wrong, has axis to correct it, cannot transmit correction
    mechanism because the population required to receive it
    was never formed.
  Proof for: CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING grounded in actual
    institutional outputs not personal authority; L4 structural
    limit [correct diagnosis, blocked transmission];
    post-stasis CAUSAL LEGIBILITY preservation.
  Cross-ref: [REF:ROBOUTE_GUILLIMAN]

[CATEGORY:SCP]

[CASE:SCP_FOUNDATION]
[PRIMARY_Q:CORRECTION CAPACITY]
SCP Foundation [fictional institution -- SCP wiki collaborative fiction]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: SILENT ALTERNATIVE with embedded extraction mechanism
    as primary fictional depiction of the structural tension between
    coherence goal and extraction tool.
    INSTITUTIONAL TYPE [SILENT ALTERNATIVE]:
      Secret organization; no public presence; grows by output
      differential [containment success] not by legitimacy surface
      export. Distributed CC [O5 council + MTFs + internal affairs
      with independent authority]. High TS [SCP entries encode
      institutional memory; correctable methodology transmitted].
      CL: anomalously high [Foundation documents everything; causal
      chain readable even when unpleasant]. GA: absent at
      institutional level [stated purpose [protecting humanity]
      diverges from operational axis [containment at any cost]].
    EMBEDDED EXTRACTION MECHANISM [D-CLASS SYSTEM]:
      D-class [expendable test subjects] have: BI = 0 [boundary
      dissolved by institutional definition], ECC = total [no
      exit], CC = 0 [no correction capacity against Foundation].
      The Foundation runs extraction architecture inside its
      generative mission. Not a bug -- the clearest fictional
      depiction of what a real containment organization structurally
      requires. Substrate needed to test dangerous objects is
      extracted from a population stripped of exit capacity.

      NEEDED ACTIONS OR GENRE CONVENTION [structural analysis]:
        Genre convention is the primary driver. Horror requires
        expendable characters who encounter the dangerous thing --
        tension requires real cost; real cost requires characters
        who can die; characters who can die need structural
        justification for why they're present. D-class exist
        because the genre requires bodies. The institutional
        justification [prisoners; condemned; operational necessity]
        is constructed around the genre requirement, not the
        reverse.
        Tell: the Foundation has thousands of contained anomalies
        it could study without D-class [anomalies not requiring
        human contact to characterize]. D-class use extends far
        beyond operational necessity because each individual entry
        needs to generate horror, and horror requires human cost.
        Operational necessity framing [secondary; legitimacy surface]:
        constructs institutional coherence so the Foundation reads
        as a policy organization rather than a torture architecture.
        The genuine mission [protecting humanity] is real; the
        "operational necessity" framing installs a legitimacy
        surface that makes the extraction feel like regrettable
        policy rather than extraction. MYTHIC FORMATION CAPTURE
        structure at fictional institutional scale: genuine mission
        real; legitimacy surface deployed to make extraction legible.
        CO-PRODUCTION: genre requirement and institutional
        legitimacy surface produce each other. Horror needs
        expendable characters → D-class constructed → Foundation
        needs justification → "operational necessity" framing →
        Foundation reads as coherent → readers accept → genre can
        use extraction more freely. Standard structure for fictional
        institutions that need to be sympathetic while running
        extraction [military / corporation / hospital all do this].
        SCP distinctive because it makes the extraction unusually
        explicit and structured.
        DYSFUNCTIONAL VARIANT [fiction examines itself]:
        Multiple SCP entries show Foundation running D-class beyond
        operational necessity [punitive, curiosity-driven, redundant].
        The fiction marks this as dysfunction, not standard
        operation. The wiki's CC distribution [authors writing
        against Foundation extraction from inside the fiction]
        is the SILENT ALTERNATIVE pattern at meta level: the
        fiction contains its own CC against its own extraction
        mechanism.

      APPLICATION TO REAL SILENT ALTERNATIVES:
        Any institution genuinely working against extraction
        faces the same structural problem: the mandate requires
        tools that contradict the values the mandate is supposed
        to protect.
        INVESTIGATIVE JOURNALISM: requires sources who face real
          cost [career, legal, physical]. Exposes extraction by
          exploiting the asymmetric vulnerability of whistleblowers.
          "Public interest" is the legitimacy surface. The
          extraction [using someone's vulnerability as institutional
          substrate] is real alongside the genuine mission.
        LEGAL AID / PUBLIC DEFENDER: requires test cases where
          the client absorbs the cost of precedent-building.
          Client's loss is the institutional substrate for
          establishing the principle. "Everyone deserves
          representation" is the legitimacy surface.
        LABOR ORGANIZING: requires asking workers to strike --
          absorb real economic cost -- to change conditions for
          everyone. Organizer extracts strike substrate from
          workers facing more immediate cost than the organizer.
          "Collective action serves everyone" is the legitimacy
          surface.
        In all three: genuine mission real [not manufactured];
        extraction of substrate from vulnerable participants also
        real; operational necessity framing constructed around
        both. Not pure cynicism -- the mission IS real. The
        extraction IS also real. Both simultaneously.
        THE TELL [legitimate vs captured]:
          In the Foundation fiction: D-class use beyond operational
          necessity [punitive, redundant, curiosity-driven] is
          where extraction stops being "needed actions" and becomes
          pure extraction. The fiction marks this as dysfunction.
          Real equivalent: the whistleblower source burned
          unnecessarily [story could run with better protection];
          the strike called without sufficient preparation [workers
          absorb cost; organizing fails]; the test case brought
          when the client wasn't ready. The point where institutional
          interest diverges from participant interest and "needed
          actions" framing continues anyway.
        KEY STRUCTURAL FINDING FOR REAL SILENT ALTERNATIVES:
          The operational necessity framing is not wrong -- it is
          genuinely necessary in most cases. But it is also the
          legitimacy surface that allows extraction to expand
          beyond necessity without internal CC firing. The CC
          mechanism: does the institution regularly examine whether
          participant cost is actually necessary, or does it treat
          the cost as background constant? If the examination
          stops: the "needed actions" framing has become Fertile
          Lock. The fiction marks this; real institutions often
          don't.
        Cross-ref: LEGITIMACY SURFACE [the "needed actions"
          framing as construction]; FERTILE LOCK [when "needed
          actions" framing stops being examined]; ANTI-COHERENCE
          COST ASYMMETRY [the structural problem all Silent
          Alternatives face]; CORRECTION CAPACITY [requires
          regular examination of whether participant cost is
          necessary]; SILENT ALTERNATIVE [Section One --
          institutional type that faces this tension];
          COST IDENTIFICATION AND MINIMIZATION [FRAMEWORK AS
          PRAXIS -- the operative principle this case illustrates;
          D-class is the primary case for what happens when
          cost examination stops].

    PRIMARY STRUCTURAL TENSION [framework-named]:
      CC requiring tools that contradict the values CC is supposed
      to protect. The Foundation's correction of civilizational
      threats requires extraction from contained populations.
      This is the named ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY problem
      at institutional scale -- coherence requires substrate cost
      that looks like extraction from inside the cost-bearing
      population.
    KEY FICTIONAL CASES [within the fiction]:
      SCP-2000 [Deus Ex Machina]: civilization reset system.
        Answer to terminal Diagnostic Collapse = wipe CAUSAL MEMORY
        at civilizational scale and restart. The reset civilization
        has no access to what caused the previous collapse. Fertile
        Lock at civilizational scale as the "solution" to anti-
        coherence winning. Framework: this is the worst possible
        correction -- it fires after substrate is terminal, and
        the correction itself destroys the causal record that
        would prevent the next cycle from repeating.
      SCP-001 proposals [unresolved]: no confirmed SCP-001.
        Multiple competing proposals; community chose not to
        resolve it. TENSION MAINTENANCE at meta-narrative level.
        The unresolved 001 is more generative than any single
        answer would be. This is SUCCESSION AUTONOMY operating
        at wiki level: successors refused to close the question.
      SCP-3125 [The Escapee]: memetic hazard that destroys all
        concept-formation about itself. Cannot be thought about;
        destroys the capacity to think about it on contact.
        Framework: WOULD-YOU-KINDLY at cognitive substrate level.
        MSC at memetic scale -- measurement surface [what you use
        to evaluate whether you're infected] is the weapon. T8
        structure [recursive closure] weaponized.
      SCP-1425 [Star Signals]: book that corrupts readers;
        reading about the book is part of the infection.
        MSC at memetic scale: the instrument used to evaluate
        exposure IS the exposure. T8 weaponized.
      SCP-2399 [A Malfunctioning Destroyer]: something broken
        on Jupiter. Foundation cannot contain it. Honest limits
        of the institutional mission named at planetary scale.
        T4 [structural obscuration] + institutional honest limit:
        the containment mandate simply cannot apply here.
    GOC COMPARISON [Global Occult Coalition]:
      Where Foundation contains, GOC destroys. Same information
      asymmetry problem; different CC mechanism. GOC has lower TS
      [nothing learned from neutralized anomaly] but higher GC
      [no maintenance cost]. Two organizations as different
      solutions to the same ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY problem
      at civilizational scale.
    ENCOUNTER DEPENDENCY [ART type caveat applies]:
      All readings of specific SCP entries are encounter-dependent.
      The Foundation as institutional type is object-readable
      [structural properties of the fictional institution are
      present in the work regardless of reception]. Individual
      SCP readings vary by instrument.
  Cross-ref: [REF:SCP_FOUNDATION]; [CASE:SCP_WIKI] [the real
    institution that produced the fictional one -- distinct case];
    ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [the structural problem the
    Foundation embodies]; SILENT ALTERNATIVE [institutional type];
    D-CLASS SYSTEM [embedded extraction mechanism]; WOULD-YOU-
    KINDLY [SCP-3125 mechanism]; T8 [SCP-1425 structure];
    CORRECTION INVERSION [Foundation applying CC to protect
    extraction architecture in some SCP entries -- named failure
    mode within the fiction].

[CASE:2_MELLO]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL LEGIBILITY]
2 Mello [real -- producer/composer, USA; Memories of
  Tokyo-To, MILLION SUNDAY series and others]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: ART COHERENCE TYPE [SOMATIC -- geometric
    variety, memory-sourced; ENCOUNTER-DEPENDENT]; ART
    COHERENCE TYPE [RELATIONAL variety; ENCOUNTER-DEPENDENT].
    Structural address [object-readable]: compositional
    properties [rhythmic feel, timbral character, harmonic
    approach] sourced from Jet Set Radio and similar game
    music -- structural properties that carry pre-loaded
    geometry for instruments with matched developmental
    exposure. Whether geometric encounter occurs depends
    on whether instrument formed during the relevant
    period [high formation-window exposure to that game
    music]. An instrument without that exposure reads the
    same structural properties without the pre-loaded
    geometry -- a genuine mismatch, not a lesser encounter.
    RELATIONAL variety: structural address routes
    recognition toward the listener's own past state
    rather than toward the performer [design choice,
    object-readable in the work's framing]. Whether
    recognition encounter occurs is instrument-dependent.
  Extraction mechanisms active: none.

    type, memory-sourced]; ART COHERENCE TYPE [RELATIONAL
    variety]; [REF:FUTURE_FUNK_CLUSTER] [geometric type
    comparison -- sample-sourced vs memory-sourced].

[CASE:COMPANY_3_FIRE_FORCE]
[PRIMARY_Q:CORRECTION CAPACITY]
[PRIMARY_Q:MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
Company 3 / Rekka Hoshimiya [Fire Force]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: CORRECTION INVERSION at institutional scale
    [correction instrument actively producing the problem
    it is mandated to solve]; legitimacy surface as active
    extraction mechanism [institutional shell indistinguishable from
    functioning correction unit from outside boundary];
    LAYERED EXTRACTION ARCHITECTURE [each outer layer
    increases inner layer correction cost without making
    correction structurally impossible].

  CORRECTION INVERSION MECHANISM:
    Company 3 is a Special Fire Force unit -- institutionally
    mandated to fight Infernals and protect civilians. Actual
    function: Rekka Hoshimiya runs human combustion
    experiments on civilians, manufacturing the Infernals
    the Force is supposed to be eliminating. The correction
    instrument and the extraction architecture occupy the
    same organizational shell simultaneously.
    Distinct from capture [instrument aimed wrong] and from
    absence [instrument not present]. This is the instrument
    actively producing the failure condition it is mandated
    to correct. CORRECTION INVERSION at maximum: the output
    of the "correction" activity is more of the problem.
    Measurement surface intact: Company 3 is a legitimate
    Fire Force unit with legitimate resources, authority,
    and mandate. From outside the institutional boundary
    it is indistinguishable from Company 8 [genuine
    correction]. The inversion is invisible until direct
    contact with the mechanism.

  LAYERED EXTRACTION ARCHITECTURE [Fire Force system]:
    Layer 1 -- Company 8 [genuine correction, inadequate
      resources]: Obi's unit is a functioning correction
      instrument. Genuine axis, genuine CC, real correction
      outputs. Structurally isolated and under-resourced
      because the outer layers benefit from its failure.
    Layer 2 -- Special Fire Force [mixed]: some companies
      genuine, some captured, Company 3 inverted. Single
      institutional shell contains all three CC states
      simultaneously. Standard audit cannot distinguish
      without penetrating the measurement surface.
    Layer 3 -- Haijima dependency [ECC]: Haijima Industries
      funds the Fire Force and runs Adolla Burst
      experimentation on children simultaneously. The
      correction instrument is financially dependent on
      the entity whose activities produce the substrate
      damage. ECC at institutional scale: defunding Haijima
      destroys the Force's capacity to fight Infernals.
      Dependency structure prevents correction from firing
      at the source.
    Layer 4 -- Holy Sol theology [Formation Capture]:
      Human combustion explained as sacred purification.
      The measurement surface for what counts as proper
      death, sacred fire, and salvation is owned by the
      Evangelist's project. Civilizational-scale FC:
      populations born into a framework that explains the
      extraction mechanism system as divine will. Correction requires
      dismantling the theology not just the institution.
    Layer 5 -- Evangelist [extraction architecture running
      the whole system]: all outer layers are legitimacy
      surfaces and resource extraction mechanisms for the
      Adolla Link project.
    Each outer layer makes inner layer correction more
    expensive without making it structurally impossible.
    This is the correct extraction architecture design:
    not eliminate correction capacity [which triggers
    somatic forcing] but raise the cost of each correction
    attempt until the substrate depletes before correction
    completes.

  SHINRA AS CORRECTION INSTRUMENT CASE:
    Genuine axis [find mother, protect people, correct the
    system] inside a captured/inverted institution [Fire
    Force]. Uses institutional resources while the
    institution uses him. Standard configuration: correct
    axis, institutionally embedded, correction attempt
    requires working through every outer layer in sequence.
    The series dramatizes what the framework predicts:
    each layer penetrated costs more than the last, and
    the correction instrument [Shinra] has to remain
    functional through the full sequence or the outer
    layers absorb and redirect the correction energy.
    Honest limit: S:1 ceiling -- fictional.

[CASE:LOOPER]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING]
Looper [Rian Johnson -- 2012; film]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent;
    multiple quality proofs; S2 cap applies]
  Proof for: CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING failure as structural
    engine [primary]. Old Joe has a genuine axis [save Sara,
    prevent the Rainmaker, restore the future he lost]. His
    CC fires continuously. His self-examination is active.
    His axis is not captured or installed -- it is examined
    and felt. And his outputs confirm catastrophic harm
    because his causal model is wrong at the root: the
    Rainmaker is produced by old Joe's violence, not despite
    it. He is trying to prevent the future he is building.
    This is the McNamara configuration [CASE:MCNAMARA] at
    full resolution: genuine axis + continuous CC + absent
    CAG = harm confirmed regardless of intent or commitment.
    The moral intensity of old Joe's commitment is precisely
    what makes him unreachable -- he cannot be bargained
    with because he is not corrupt, he is wrong at the
    causal layer. The more he acts on his genuine values,
    the more certainly he produces the outcome he is acting
    against.
  Proof for: CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING [temporal inversion as
    CAG failure made visible]. The standard CAG failure is
    synchronic: actor has genuine axis but misreads which
    of their outputs is producing which outcome. Looper
    externalizes this as a temporal loop: old Joe's future
    behavioral record [what he does to Cid and Sara] is
    precisely what young Joe needs to read to understand
    the causal chain -- and it is the one record old Joe
    cannot access from inside his own formation event [his
    wife's death]. Young Joe is the external witness whose
    vantage point is structurally outside old Joe's
    formation. This is the CAG+MI CORRECTION STACK's
    external-witness requirement [WIP 35] in narrative
    form: the correction requires someone outside the
    formation to hold the behavioral record the person
    inside cannot read alone.
  Proof for: FORMATION CAPTURE [Cid / the Rainmaker
    arc]. Cid is a genuine Nascent case [WIP 23] --
    telekinetic, pre-axial, formation not yet complete.
    The violence old Joe deploys against telekinetic
    children is exactly what forms Cid's axis toward
    destruction rather than away from it. Old Joe's
    intervention is the weapon that creates the thing
    it is trying to prevent. Formation inside violent
    controlled environment [old Joe's targeting, Sara's
    death in the alternate timeline] produces the
    Rainmaker's installed trajectory. Classic NASCENT
    primary vulnerability: axis formed inside traumatic
    event produces permanent directional set. The
    alternate-timeline Cid who witnesses his mother's
    death becomes the Rainmaker; the timeline where
    Sara survives produces a Cid who is frightened and
    dangerous but not yet fixed. The intervention window
    is the Nascent window -- once the formation event
    fires, trajectory locks.
  Proof for: EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION [the loop system
    itself]. The looper economy is ECC at civilizational
    scale: participants are locked into a system they
    entered voluntarily [young Joe chose to be a looper]
    whose exit cost is death, with the exit condition
    structured so the extractor owns the moment of exit
    [the mob in the future decides when the loop closes
    and sends the older self back]. No participant can
    exit without the mechanism's permission. The
    language "closing the loop" is the framework's Exit
    Cost Construction named directly by the film: the
    exit was always built into the entry; the participant
    just didn't read it that way at entry time. This is
    the ECC weapon's defining structure: exit cost rises
    until it equals total substrate, at which point the
    exit event is managed by the extractor.
  YOUNG JOE'S SOLUTION [structurally necessary]:
    Young Joe cannot correct old Joe from within the
    system -- old Joe's formation is too complete and
    his behavioral momentum too high. He cannot argue
    old Joe out of a causal misread old Joe has staked
    his entire remaining purpose on. The correction
    pathway from inside the loop is closed. Young Joe
    exits by eliminating his own future self: not
    reform, not persuasion, not a better argument --
    substrate removal. He removes the node that the
    extraction depends on. This is the only available
    structural move once: (1) ECC has made every other
    exit impossible, (2) CAG failure has made
    persuasion impossible, (3) the formation event
    [Sara's death] is about to execute in a way that
    locks Cid's trajectory permanently. Young Joe's
    sacrifice is not altruism -- it is the only
    intervention that reaches all three layers
    simultaneously. Its cost is that it works by the
    same substrate-removal logic the looper system
    itself runs on. The counter and the weapon have
    the same shape at the terminal node.
  STRUCTURAL READING [the loop as extraction model]:
    The film's title is the weapon's name. The loop
    is not a time-travel puzzle -- it is an extraction
    architecture in which the same substrate [Joe]
    is made to pay the exit cost twice [once as young
    Joe executing the loop; once as old Joe being the
    target]. The mob in the future extracts labor from
    young Joe by promising him a future, then extracts
    the accumulated substrate from old Joe by closing
    the loop. The generative-looking portion [young
    Joe's well-paying job, his future freedom] is the
    legitimacy surface. The extraction architecture
    [the loop always closes; the mob always collects;
    the laborer always pays twice] is the hidden
    operative axis. The film is named for the weapon.
  T3: S2 cap. Fictional case. Do not use for primary
    theory confirmation.

    old Joe arc]; FORMATION CAPTURE [Cid / Rainmaker

[CASE:DWMA_SOUL_EATER]

Death Extraction mechanism Meister Academy [Soul Eater]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: Correction instrument as extraction mechanism factory
    [people cultivated as institutional instruments];
    GENUINE AXIS within captured institution [Maka/Soul
    partnership]; MEDUSA as high-CL deliberate extraction
    actor; person/tool boundary as extraction surface.

  CORE STRUCTURAL TENSION:
    DWMA's stated function: fight the Kishin, maintain
    sanity in the world, train meisters and extraction mechanisms.
    Actual structural position: Shinigami's order requires
    extraction mechanisms. Students are the extraction mechanisms. The institution
    cultivates people as instruments of an existing power
    structure. The correction mandate [stop madness] and
    the extraction structure [produce extraction mechanisms for the
    death god's order] run simultaneously inside the same
    institution.
    The meister/extraction mechanism partnership is the framework stress
    test: the partnership works when both sides have
    genuine axis and genuine correction capacity [Maka
    and Soul]. It fails into extraction when one side
    treats the other as pure instrument [Black Star's
    early arc; Crona/Ragnarok as the terminal failure
    case -- extraction mechanism fully captures meister].

  MAKA/SOUL AS GENUINE AXIS CONFIRMATION:
    The soul resonance mechanic is the operational
    definition of what the framework means by genuine
    partnership: both axes present, both correction
    capacities functional, the output exceeds what either
    produces alone. When one axis is captured or absent
    the resonance fails -- you can't fake soul resonance
    with a captured or absent axis. The series uses
    resonance as an empirical test for genuine vs
    performed partnership.
    Cross-domain confirmation: the same logic applies
    outside the fictional frame. Genuine collaboration
    produces outputs that neither party could produce
    alone. Performed collaboration produces neither
    party's full output. The resonance mechanic is
    fiction making the invisible visible.

  MEDUSA [high-CL deliberate extraction]:
    Genuine axis, genuine CAUSAL LEGIBILITY, full
    awareness of the extraction architecture, deliberate
    choice to accelerate it. Not captured -- she sees
    the system clearly and finds correction uninteresting.
    Specific failure mode: correct instrument, correct
    readings, axis aimed at extraction by choice not
    capture. This is the most dangerous configuration
    in the framework -- high CL + uncaptured axis +
    deliberate extraction means standard counter
    [restore legibility, break capture] doesn't work.
    The axis isn't captured. It's pointed the wrong way
    by design.
    Framework implication: not all extraction actors are
    captured. Some are legible and choosing. The
    intervention for captured axis is different from the
    intervention for deliberate extraction. Medusa
    distinguishes these.

  KISHIN [extraction to terminal state]:
    Asura as the endpoint of extraction consuming its
    own substrate -- fear consuming the capacity to
    experience anything other than fear. The madness
    wavelength is extraction architecture at biological
    substrate level: it converts the substrate [people's
    sanity and generative capacity] into the fuel for
    its own continuation. Terminal extraction confirmed.
    The series' answer [Maka's courage wavelength
    defeating madness] maps onto the framework's
    optimistic scenario: generation only needs to win
    once. Anti-coherence [madness] must win continuously.
    One correctable transmission [Maka reaching Asura's
    genuine fear underneath the madness] is sufficient
    to break the loop.

[CASE:HOMURA_REBELLION]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Homura Akemi [fictional -- Puella Magi Madoka Magica /
  Rebellion film; magical girl, looper]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: GENUINE AXIS degrading into imposed axis through
    CC absence over extended timeline; distinct from standard
    GA failure [imposed axis from origin] -- this is genuine
    axis at origin becoming extraction architecture through
    refusal of correction. The origin was real; the failure
    is CC absence, not malice at source.

  GENUINE AXIS AT ORIGIN [confirmed]:
    Homura's axis [protect Madoka] was internally generated,
    examined at formation, and grounded in genuine relational
    substrate [L3.5 RECOGNITION confirmed; the relationship
    was real]. At origin: high GA, high BI, genuine CC firing
    against the system [each timeline reset is a correction
    attempt]. The axis was not imposed; it was chosen under
    full awareness of cost. T1.1 [proportional response]
    confirmed: correction fired against Kyubey's system at
    maximum personal cost across infinite iterations.

  CC ABSENCE AS DEGRADATION MECHANISM:
    The axis was never subjected to internal CC. Homura
    examined the external system [Kyubey, the contract
    architecture, each timeline's failure mode] relentlessly
    but never applied the same correction capacity to the
    axis itself. The question "is protecting Madoka still the
    correct axis, or has it become the axis I cannot examine"
    was structurally unavailable -- the emotional substrate
    the axis was built on prevented CC from firing against
    the axis itself. This is the load-bearing distinction:
    CC absence is not the same as CC inversion. Homura is
    not extracting consciously; the axis has become a
    formation-captured instrument that cannot self-examine.

  REBELLION ENDPOINT:
    By Rebellion, the axis [protect Madoka] has become the
    only thing Homura lives for regardless of Madoka's own
    feelings or choices. She constructs an entire false
    reality to preserve a version of Madoka that would not
    choose to sacrifice herself. The false reality is an
    act of love that is structurally identical to Formation
    Capture: it installs Homura's axis into Madoka's
    formation environment, removes Madoka's access to
    competing axis sources [her own memory, her own choice],
    and requires Madoka to misunderstand her situation.
    Genuine axis at origin + CC absence over extended
    timeline + sufficient emotional pressure = the instrument
    eventually produces extraction architecture without ever
    becoming malicious. The show does not frame this as
    clean evil -- accurate. The mechanism is CC absence,
    not bad faith.

  FRAMEWORK DISTINCTION [load-bearing]:
    Standard GA failure: axis was imposed from outside or
    never genuine. Homura case: axis genuine at origin,
    degraded through CC absence under sustained pressure.
    These require different interventions. Standard GA
    failure: remove the imposed axis, build genuine one.
    Homura case: the axis IS genuine at the substrate level;
    the failure is that it was never examined. Intervention
    requires CC restoration on the axis itself, not axis
    replacement. The love is real; the problem is that real
    love was allowed to become the thing that cannot be
    questioned.

  Extraction mechanisms active [Homura operates in Rebellion]:
    Formation Capture [installs her axis into Madoka's
      environment without Madoka's informed consent].
    MSC [false reality prevents accurate measurement].
    Exit Cost Construction [Madoka cannot choose her
      original sacrifice within the false reality].

  Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [quality definition -- degradation
    pathway under CC absence]; [REF:KYUBEY] [system Homura
    correctly resisted for 100+ timelines]; CORRECTION
    CAPACITY [CC absence as distinct failure from CC
    inversion]; Formation Capture [weapons index -- genuine
    axis can become FC instrument without malice].

[CASE:KYUBEY]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Kyubey [fictional -- Puella Magi Madoka Magica; alien intelligence,
  Incubator species; contract-granting entity]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: EXTRACTION operational definition [trickery as
    foundation; prey required to misunderstand the relationship];
    Formation Capture [deliberate targeting of formation window];
    ICD + Formation Capture spontaneous stack; MSC [Measurement
    Surface Capture -- information asymmetry as extraction mechanism at contract
    level]; EXTRACTION CYCLE at civilizational scope.

  EXTRACTION MECHANISM:
    Kyubey's contract is technically not dishonest -- terms are
    disclosed in principle. The extraction operates through total
    information asymmetry: Kyubey understands the full system
    [Soul Gem degradation endpoint, witch transformation, grief
    seed harvesting loop, thermodynamic purpose]; the contracting
    girls do not. The relationship requires them to misunderstand
    what they are entering. Operational extraction confirmed:
    trickery as foundation. Distinct from PREDATION [honest
    violence; boundary visible] and COMPETITION [both parties
    know the terms]. The optimistic surface maintained throughout
    is MSC: the measurement surface [Kyubey's presentation of
    the contract] does not reflect the actual causal chain.

  FORMATION CAPTURE:
    The contract is structurally unavailable to adults with
    settled axes. Kyubey exclusively targets girls at peak
    formation-window vulnerability -- grief, longing, or
    unrealized desire at maximum, axis not yet stable, no
    competing axis sources strong enough to interrupt. This
    is not incidental targeting; the system selects for it.
    The wish mechanism amplifies formation-window instability:
    the wish resolves the surface desire while leaving the
    underlying axis more capturable. Sayaka's wish [heal
    Kyousuke] resolves the external object while installing
    a martyrdom axis that was already present but unexamined.
    FC fires at the moment the instrument is least protected.

  ICD + FORMATION CAPTURE SPONTANEOUS STACK:
    Soul Gem degradation mechanic creates ongoing dependency.
    The grief seed harvesting loop requires continuous witch-
    fighting, which requires continuous emotional expenditure,
    which accelerates Soul Gem degradation, which requires
    more grief seeds. Consumption required to maintain
    baseline continuously increases. The stack is emergent
    from the thermodynamic system design -- Kyubey did not
    consciously deploy ICD as a extraction mechanism. It is structurally
    built into the system. Confirms the framework finding:
    ICD+FC spontaneous stack is the most common current
    configuration because the structural conditions produce
    it without deliberate design.

  EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational scope]:
    The girls are substrate. Hope and despair are fuel for
    thermodynamic entropy reversal. The system accumulates
    emotional energy [hope at contract] -- substrate degrades
    [witch transformation] -- harvests degradation [grief
    seeds] -- repeats. The cycle IS the product specification.
    The death and suffering are not side effects; they are
    the substrate cost being externalized onto the girls so
    the Incubators can maintain universal entropy balance.
    The extraction architecture operates at civilizational
    scope across time -- not one actor extracting from a
    population but an intelligence extracting from an entire
    species across generations. No exit condition available
    from inside the system; CC is monopolized by Kyubey.

  MADOKA'S INTERVENTION AS SILENT ALTERNATIVE:
    The correct intervention type when CC is monopolized at
    the top node: do not fight the node directly [confirmed
    failed across Homura's timelines] -- remove the substrate
    on which the system operates. Madoka cannot destroy
    Kyubey's architecture; she rewrites the rules such that
    the primary product [witches] never materializes. The
    extraction architecture survives; its substrate is
    removed. SILENT ALTERNATIVE at structural level: exit
    the layer on which extraction operates rather than
    contesting it directly. See [REF:HOMURA_REBELLION] for
    the failure mode of the solution's aftermath.

  Extraction mechanisms active [Kyubey operates]:
    Formation Capture [primary -- structural selection for
      formation-window targets].
    MSC [contract surface does not reflect causal chain].
    ICD [emergent -- grief seed dependency loop].
    Exit Cost Construction [witch transformation as ultimate
      exit cost; no exit available once contracted].

  Cross-ref: EXTRACTION [operational definition]; Formation
    Capture [weapons index]; ICD [weapons index]; MSC [extraction mechanism
    index]; EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational]; SILENT
    ALTERNATIVE [coherence type -- Madoka's intervention];
    [REF:HOMURA_REBELLION] [aftermath failure mode].

[CASE:GUTS]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Guts [Berserk]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: Mixed individual-scale [stable]; internal tension
    not external extraction mechanism. Mixed-stable; directionality
    window-dependent. Three-axis: center, variable by window,
    front to mid.
  Extraction mechanisms active: none [internal tension].

  CHAPTER 384 UPDATE [v22 -- "no self" reveal; T9 RESOLUTION
  candidate]:

  FUNCTIONAL READ [in-universe structure]:
    Guts has run for the entire series on RAGE as a captured
      drive [Griffith's betrayal as permanent external trigger]
      layered over GENUINE AXIS [the actual underlying desire:
      to live, to protect Casca, to find meaning past mere
      survival]. The rage was functional -- it produced power
      and forward motion -- but extractive: the Berserker
      Armor converts health and sanity directly into power,
      the textbook ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY signature
      [extraction works short-term; cost compounds with use].
    Chapter 384's "no self" reveal [Guts born half-astral, from
      the same cosmic substrate as the godhand, and confronting
      his full origin via Flora's fire-spirit vision] reframes
      the upcoming power-up not as more force layered onto the
      same rage-engine, but as the removal of the parasitic
      rage-structure entirely. This is GENUINE AXIS reaching
      actual resolution rather than perpetual costly management
      -- T9 RESOLUTION: the axis was always there underneath
      the captured drive; the chapter is the integration event,
      not a new power source.
    Griffith's established power [reality bending to his desire;
      Dragon Slayer being one of very few things that can land
      damage on a godhand, yet nothing touches Griffith himself]
      is read here as FORMATION CAPTURE operating at narrative-
      structural scale within the fiction: Griffith's desire
      reshapes events because the story's own causality serves
      his arc -- in-fiction, the routing boundary and the
      measurement surface are the same entity, his will. Guts
      achieving "no self" and being framed as someone who can
      "exist outside the author's pen" is GENUINE AXIS reaching
      a state the captured system's rules cannot process --
      not winning within Griffith's rules by being stronger,
      but exiting the frame those rules operate inside. Same
      structural shape as [CASE:WHEEL_OF_TIME]'s resolution-by-
      exiting-the-corrupted-pattern rather than fighting it on
      its own terms.
    CONNECTION TO [CASE:IDEA_OF_EVIL]: Guts's origin [condensed
      from the astral world's cosmic ocean, the same substrate
      that produces the godhand and is adjacent to the Idea of
      Evil's domain] ties his GENUINE AXIS resolution directly
      to the closed-loop topology IDEA_OF_EVIL represents.
      Where the Idea of Evil is a system generated by and
      consuming the population that produces it [T3 unreachable
      as a topology property -- see IDEA_OF_EVIL entry], Guts
      achieving "no self" is the individual-scale exit from
      that same closed loop: ego-driven wish-making is what
      feeds the Idea of Evil; "no self" is structurally the
      one stance that cannot be converted into more fuel for
      it. Read together, Guts's arc is the individual-scale
      proof-of-concept for escaping the civilizational-scale
      trap IDEA_OF_EVIL names.

  META READ [production/authorial layer -- distinct from the
  in-universe read above]:
    The chapter's in-story theme [a character achieving
      independence from authorial/causal control within the
      fiction] lands at the exact moment its real-world
      production circumstances mirror that theme closely: Kouji
      Mori, taking over the story after Kentaro Miura's death,
      executing a reveal Miura reportedly planned roughly 20
      years prior, reconstructed through repeated conversation
      with Miura's longtime editor rather than from Miura's
      direct hand. This is TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY and
      SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY playing out in reality at the same
      moment the fiction depicts a protagonist breaking free of
      its author's narrative control.
    Two readings, not resolved here: [a] striking coincidence --
      the thematic content of a 20-year-old planned reveal
      happened to land at a moment when its execution required
      exactly the kind of successor-reconstructs-original-intent
      process the chapter's content also describes; or [b]
      deliberate choice -- Mori folding the real succession
      story into how he chose to execute the reveal, aware of
      the parallel.
    HONEST LIMIT: this meta-layer claim rests on a single
      secondary source [a YouTube video summarizing and
      interpreting the chapter, including a quoted statement
      attributed to Mori about the reveal's 20-year planning
      and reconstruction process via Miura's editor]. Not
      independently verified against a primary source. Treat
      the functional read above as the more confidently
      supportable claim; treat the meta read as a flagged,
      lower-confidence interpretive layer pending verification.
    Cross-ref: SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY [Section One -- the quality
      this meta-layer is actually about]; TRANSMISSION
      SELECTIVITY [what determines how much of Miura's original
      intent survives reconstruction through an intermediary].

  Cross-ref: [REF:GUTS]; [CASE:GRIFFITH] [primary antagonist;
    FORMATION CAPTURE at narrative-structural scale]; [CASE:
    IDEA_OF_EVIL] [closed-loop topology Guts's origin and
    resolution connect to directly]; T9 [RESOLUTION -- axis
    integration rather than perpetual management]; GENUINE
    AXIS [Section One -- primary case for resolution after
    long captured-drive management]; ANTI-COHERENCE COST
    ASYMMETRY [Berserker Armor as the clean extraction-cost
    signature this resolution removes]; SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
    [meta read -- real-world production parallel].
