[SOURCE:DISCO_ELYSIUM]

[CASE:DISCO_ELYSIUM]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Disco Elysium [ZA/UM -- 2019; RPG]
  Type: Primary [S:2; fictional; multiple quality proofs;
    S2 cap applies]

  THE SKILL SYSTEM AS QUALITY STACK EXTERNALIZED:
    24 skills are not stats -- they are named sub-instruments
    within the composite instrument [Harry Du Bois], each
    with distinct voice, distinct failure modes, distinct
    capture vulnerabilities. The framework's quality stack
    rendered as characters who argue with each other.
    VOLITION [CYCLE STABILITY]: keeps Harry functional when
      everything else is degrading. The quality that enables
      the other qualities to fire. Without Volition: Harry
      cannot maintain the generative cycle; substance use
      wins; the instrument collapses. With Volition: the
      instrument can hold the weight of what it discovers.
    INLAND EMPIRE [PRE-THEORETICAL STRUCTURAL REGISTRATION]:
      fires on structural patterns before the deliberative
      layer can process them. Often right in ways Harry
      cannot yet articulate. The somatic channel named and
      given a voice. High Inland Empire = high pre-
      theoretical registration; the instrument catches
      things before knowing what it caught.
    RHETORIC [ACWL CONFIGURATION IN SKILL FORM]:
      argumentation capacity independent of axis. High
      Rhetoric with captured axis = better manipulation.
      The framework's AXIS CAPTURE WITH LEGIBILITY made
      playable: the instrument functional; axis captured;
      CC firing in the wrong direction through a highly
      competent channel.
    EMPATHY [SUICIDAL EMPATHY RISK]:
      too high without Volition = instrument cannot protect
      itself from input. Absorbs everything without filter.
      The SUICIDAL EMPATHY configuration as a gameplay
      mechanic -- the skill that should produce connection
      produces substrate depletion instead when
      CYCLE STABILITY is absent.
    ELECTROCHEMISTRY [ICD AS QUALITY]:
      pleasure and addiction competing with Volition for
      Harry's substrate. ICD running as a named skill with
      its own voice. The weapon that targets CYCLE STABILITY
      is here rendered as a quality that can be developed.
      High Electrochemistry = higher ICD vulnerability;
      the instrument can develop its own capture mechanism.

  HARRY'S AMNESIA AS AXIS RECONSTRUCTION FROM BEHAVIORAL
  RECORD:
    Harry didn't lose memories -- he lost his axis. What
    remains is the substrate [skill capacities; somatic
    responses; emotional reactions] without the narrative
    the axis was wrapped in. Formation Capture in reverse:
    instead of false axis installed, genuine axis erased.
    The investigation is axis reconstruction from Track C.
    CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING from behavioral record not from
    introspection: introspection is unavailable [amnesia].
    The axis is still there -- it produced outputs; those
    outputs are still in the world; people remember what
    Harry did; the city remembers who he was. The axis is
    rebuilt from what it produced, not from what Harry
    remembers producing it.
    Cleanest fictional depiction of axis reconstruction from
    behavioral record in the library. POST-CULT EPISTEMIC
    PATTERN without the cult: amnesia as the erasure event;
    the world as the correction instrument.

  KIM KITSURAGI [see small entry below -- TRANSPARENT
  COHERENCE primary fictional case]:
    No legitimacy surface needed. Axis grounded in behavioral
    record. CC fires correctly and consistently. Low social
    compliance channel weight -- doesn't perform warmth;
    warmth is real when present. Maintains axis under Harry's
    chaos, the town's hostility, the political situation.
    Instrument-specificity note: reads as cold to some
    players because social compliance channel [warmth
    performance] is absent. Not cold -- TRANSPARENT
    COHERENCE. The warmth is real; it is not performed.

  THE POLITICAL IDEOLOGY SYSTEM [simultaneous partial-
  correctness-plus-capture for every major formation]:
    Communism [the Revolution; Revachol's history]:
      Real critique of extraction [confirmed]; Fertile Lock
      at civilizational scale [transmitted conclusions
      without methodology; ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY
      confirmed -- Moralintern was patient; Revolution
      only needed to fail once].
    Fascism [Measurehead; pure power]:
      GENUINE CRITIQUE AS CAPTURE MECHANISM -- correctly
      identifies world structured by power; incorrectly
      concludes power should therefore be exercised without
      restraint. Same structure as Ishamael. Real diagnosis;
      captured conclusion.
    Liberalism [the Moralintern]:
      LIBERALISM AS PARTIAL STATE RELIGION fully deployed.
      The California Model at civilizational scale --
      extraction architecture maintaining legitimacy surface
      [international law; property rights; managed order]
      while running extraction everywhere the surface
      doesn't apply.
    Moralism [Joyce; the Tribunal]:
      MORAL LEGIBILITY failure in action. ACWL configuration:
      genuine axis; CC aimed at proxy targets; extraction
      architecture protected by moral legitimacy surface.
    Every ideology is simultaneously partially correct and
    captured. The game does not resolve this. The framework
    says: this is the correct structural output -- all
    major formations have genuine substrate and captured
    prescription. GENUINE CRITIQUE AS CAPTURE MECHANISM
    running across the entire political spectrum at once.

  THE PALE [HELL AS DIRECTION at cosmological/geographic
  scale]:
    Spreading grey nothingness that consumes geography,
    memory, history. Expands where people stop believing
    things are real. Consensus reality is what holds back
    the Pale. Where belief fails: the Pale enters.
    Framework: ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY rendered
    literally as geography. The world has to win
    continuously [maintain consensus; maintain belief;
    maintain generative cycles]; the Pale only needs to
    win once per territory. Every area consumed is gone
    permanently. No C1 recovery. HELL AS DIRECTION as
    literal cartographic expansion. Not a location --
    a direction. Every area that stops believing is
    consumed. The Pale is extraction at the level of
    reality itself: not extracting surplus but terminating
    the cycle that produces surplus. FERTILE LOCK at
    cosmological scale -- a consumed territory cannot
    re-enter the generative cycle.

  THE MURDER [POST-CULT EPISTEMIC PATTERN at collective
  scale]:
    Lely [mercenary hired to break the strike] was killed
    by union militia members who were also the idealists
    of the Revolution. They killed him; then could not
    admit what they had done; then protected the secret
    from themselves for weeks while the body hung.
    Framework: POST-CULT EPISTEMIC PATTERN at collective
    scale. The revolutionary identity was Formation Capture
    deep enough that the actors could not integrate the
    behavioral record [we killed him] with the installed
    identity [we are the idealists; we stand for something].
    The body hanging is the behavioral record the installed
    identity cannot acknowledge. The investigation is
    CAG + MI CORRECTION STACK at collective scale --
    making the causal record legible so the actors can
    hold both [we are the idealists AND we did this].

  THE ENDING [individual correction without systemic change]:
    Whatever ending you reach: Revachol is still occupied;
    the strike is still going; the Pale is still spreading.
    The mystery is solved; nothing structural changes.
    The investigation produced genuine correction [the
    truth was found; the killers named; justice done for
    the dead man]. It did not change the structure that
    produced the situation.
    Framework: this is not failure -- it is the correct
    result of correct action inside a fully captured
    system. ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY at narrative
    scale: individual correction within captured system
    produces local correction and no systemic change.
    The game does not frame this as cynicism. The
    framework agrees: it is accurate.

    One -- the 24 skills as externalized sub-instrument

[CASE:KIM_KITSURAGI]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Kim Kitsuragi [Disco Elysium -- fictional; Lieutenant,
  RCM Precinct 41]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: TRANSPARENT COHERENCE primary fictional case.
    Cleanest instance in the library.
    No legitimacy surface needed: Kim does not perform
      being a good detective. He is one. The behavioral
      record is the axis. What he says matches what he
      does at every stress point in the game.
    CC fires correctly and consistently: will tell Harry
      when Harry is wrong. Will not do things he finds
      wrong even under instruction. Does not defer to
      authority when authority is incorrect. CC aimed at
      the right targets without social compliance channel
      overriding it.
    Low social compliance channel weight: does not care
      what people think of him. Cares whether the work
      is right. The approval-seeking channel is genuinely
      absent, not suppressed -- it was never the dominant
      channel. This is what makes Kim legible as
      TRANSPARENT COHERENCE rather than WISDOM CONCEALMENT
      [which has the channel but manages it]: Kim's channel
      weight is structurally low, not managed.
    Maintains axis under maximum pressure: Harry's chaos
      [substance use, amnesia, breakdown]; the town's
      hostility [RCM is not popular in Martinaise]; the
      political situation [post-revolutionary occupied
      city; the investigation has no institutional support].
      Axis does not drift under any of these. The
      behavioral record at end of game is identical to
      the behavioral record at start.
    INSTRUMENT-SPECIFICITY NOTE [load-bearing]:
      Kim reads as cold to some players because the social
      compliance channel [warmth performance] is absent.
      He does not perform warmth. The warmth IS real --
      it appears at the correct moments [genuine care for
      Harry's wellbeing; specific moments of connection
      that are understated and real] -- but it is not
      performed as a social lubricant.
      The player instrument calibration determines the
      read: high social compliance channel weight in the
      player → Kim reads as cold and robotic. Low social
      compliance channel weight in the player → Kim reads
      as the most trustworthy instrument in the game.
      Same output; different receiving instrument.
  Cross-ref: [CASE:DISCO_ELYSIUM] [primary]; TRANSPARENT
    COHERENCE [Section One -- Kim is the primary fictional
    case; the Neurotypical Path entry maps his
    configuration]; WISDOM CONCEALMENT [Section One --
    distinct; Kim doesn't conceal, doesn't need to];
    INSTRUMENT-SPECIFICITY [YAPPING/DOING -- Kim's
    warmth is loop-closing not surface-maintaining];
    GENUINE AXIS [Section One -- primary proof].

[CASE:KIM_MEASUREHEAD]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Measurehead [Disco Elysium -- fictional; dock foreman;
  fascist]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: GENUINE CRITIQUE AS CAPTURE MECHANISM
    [fictional primary; political scale].
    Measurehead correctly identifies: the world is
      structured by power. The weak are dominated by the
      strong. The Moralintern's legitimacy surface [law,
      order, international governance] is a surface over
      power dynamics that continue beneath it. These
      diagnoses are accurate. The framework confirms them.
    The capture: therefore power should be exercised
      without restraint; the strong should rule openly;
      the surface should be dropped and the underlying
      power acknowledged as the legitimate order.
    The conclusion does not follow. The diagnosis [power
      structures reality] is correct. The prescription
      [therefore embrace power without limit] is captured.
      Same mechanism as Ishamael: accurate diagnosis of
      the trap; captured conclusion about the exit.
    What Measurehead cannot see: the diagnosis [power
      structures reality] is compatible with the
      conclusion [therefore build distributed CC to
      counter power concentration] as well as with his
      conclusion [therefore embrace power]. The diagnosis
      does not determine the prescription. The Dark One
      / fascism move: use the accurate diagnosis to
      make the captured prescription feel inevitable.
    T9 STRUCTURE: the correction requires Measurehead
      to admit his conclusion was captured by the
      diagnosis. The admission: "I correctly identified
      the structure of power AND drew the wrong
      conclusion from it." Cannot hold both. The
      diagnosis is the identity. The conclusion is
      protected by the identity.
  Cross-ref: [CASE:DISCO_ELYSIUM] [primary]; GENUINE
    CRITIQUE AS CAPTURE MECHANISM [Section Two --
    Measurehead as political-scale fictional case;
    Ishamael as cosmological-scale parallel]; T9
    [IRRATIONALITY MAINTENANCE -- diagnosis as identity
    protecting the captured conclusion]; FORMATION
    CAPTURE [argument-level FC; same mechanism].

[CASE:THE_PALE_DISCO]
[PRIMARY_Q:ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY]
The Pale [Disco Elysium -- fictional; cosmological
  geography]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY at cosmological
    scale rendered as literal geography. HELL AS DIRECTION
    as cartographic expansion. FERTILE LOCK at territorial
    scale.
    The Pale expands where people stop believing things
      are real. Consensus reality holds back the Pale.
      The world must win continuously [maintain belief;
      maintain generative cycles; maintain the consensus
      that makes things real]. The Pale only needs to
      win once per territory -- each consumed area is
      gone permanently. No recovery. No C1 restoration.
    ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY confirmed [fictional]:
      generation [maintaining the world] must succeed
      continuously; the Pale only needs to succeed once
      per location. The asymmetry is structurally
      identical to the framework's confirmed finding.
      The Pale is what anti-coherence winning once per
      territory looks like rendered as a phenomenon.
    HELL AS DIRECTION: the Pale is not a location. It is
      a direction. Territory consumed does not become a
      different place -- it ceases to be a place. The
      direction toward the Pale is the direction toward
      non-existence. Same structure as framework's HELL
      AS DIRECTION: not a destination but a trajectory
      that has terminated.
    FERTILE LOCK at territorial scale: consumed territory
      cannot re-enter the generative cycle. The exit is
      permanent. The correction is unavailable because
      the instrument [the territory] no longer exists.
    CONSENSUS REALITY AS GENERATIVE CYCLE [named
      structural finding in fiction]: the Pale makes
      explicit that reality itself requires maintenance.
      The generative cycle is not just economic or
      biological -- it is epistemic. The world continues
      because people maintain the consensus that it does.
      Where that consensus fails: the Pale enters. This
      is the framework's CYCLE STABILITY at the level
      of reality itself.
  Cross-ref: [CASE:DISCO_ELYSIUM] [primary]; ANTI-
    COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [Section Two -- confirmed
    structural finding; the Pale is the clearest
    fictional rendering]; HELL AS DIRECTION [Section
    Eight -- the Pale as geographic HELL AS DIRECTION];
    FERTILE LOCK [weapon -- territorial Fertile Lock];
    CYCLE STABILITY [Section One -- consensus reality
    as the ultimate generative cycle].

[PRIMARY_Q: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
Gnosia [Petit Depotto -- visual novel 2019; anime October 2025-2026]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent across
    multiple quality proofs; anime currently airing at time of
    entry; S2 cap applies]
  Proof for: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [primary -- the social reading
    instrument is precisely the instrument the Gnosia are designed
    to defeat. Gnosia perform humanness: social behavior, stated
    positions, emotional responses, all the signals the crew uses
    to measure trustworthiness. The measurement surface is captured
    by the entity whose detection depends on that surface being
    accurate. The crew's instrument [social reading of other crew
    members] cannot distinguish genuine axis from performed axis
    because the Gnosia perform the same signals genuine humans
    produce. Not a calibration problem -- a structural impossibility.
    The instrument and the threat are co-evolved: the threat exists
    specifically because the instrument is what it is].
  Proof for: DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [secondary -- the correction
    mechanism [cold sleep the suspicious one] fires with confidence
    but cannot reliably identify the target. Each execution may
    or may not correct the actual problem. The crew cannot tell
    from inside the mechanism whether the correction worked. The
    mechanism runs to completion without verifiable success.
    Process repeats daily until Gnosia contained or outnumber
    humans -- the correction mechanism consuming the substrate it
    was designed to protect is Diagnostic Collapse in loop form].
  Proof for: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [secondary -- Silver Key mechanic.
    The loop persists until enough knowledge is acquired. What
    accumulates across loops is not survival but causal
    reconstruction: eventually the pattern becomes readable
    because enough iterations have been witnessed to trace it.
    Single-loop social deduction cannot produce genuine CL --
    the surface signals are too easy to fake. The loop is a
    forced truth-seeking state that makes CL achievable by
    accumulating what single-instance reading cannot capture.
    The Silver Key is the framework's TRUTH-SEEKING STATE
    operationalized as a literal mechanic: you cannot exit
    without building the genuine understanding the social
    deduction game was never designed to produce in one pass].
  Proof for: SINCERE DELUSION [tertiary -- crew members
    confidently wrong about who the Gnosia are. The instrument
    feels functional; it isn't. Readings are produced with
    certainty; they are wrong at a structural level. Not lying
    -- the crew genuinely believes its readings. The instrument
    produces confident false outputs because the calibration
    surface was captured before the reading was taken].

  YURI AWAKENS AS GNOSIA [deepest structural point]:
    The instrument that was reading for Gnosia becomes the
    thing being read for. Measurement instrument and measured
    object exchange positions. The instrument's reliability
    depended on its position not its construction. An
    instrument reliable from one position is unreliable from
    the other -- not because it changed but because the
    relationship between instrument and object changed.
    Framework: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY is not an intrinsic
    property of the instrument. It is a relational property
    between instrument, object, and the channel connecting
    them. Capturing the channel [social performance signals]
    changes the reliability regardless of instrument quality.

  COMPARISON TO DEATH PARADE [related but distinct]:
    Both: MI failure producing wrong correction outcomes.
    Death Parade: instrument cannot read the substrate [arbiters
      never lived]. Structural incapacity. The instrument was
      never built to read what it needs to read.
    Gnosia: instrument can read humans accurately [genuine CL
      of human signals exists] but the threat performs the same
      signals. The instrument is adequate; the channel is
      captured. The failure mode is different: not incapacity
      but surface capture.

  Extraction mechanisms active: MSC [measurement surface
    captured by the entity being measured]; Calibration Strip
    [social performance signals set as reference standard for
    humanness -- calibration standard captured at inception].
  T3: S2 cap. Fictional. Anime airing at time of entry --
    full lore not yet complete. Framework read from available
    episodes and source visual novel.
  Source: Gnosia visual novel [Petit Depotto, 2019]; anime
    [October 2025-2026, 21 episodes]; ANN; Gnosia Wiki.
  Cross-ref: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [primary]; DIAGNOSTIC
    COLLAPSE [correction mechanism firing without reliable
    target]; CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [Silver Key loop as forced
    causal reconstruction]; SINCERE DELUSION [confident
    wrong readings from captured surface]; MSC [surface

    [related MI failure -- incapacity vs surface capture];
    TRUTH-SEEKING STATE [Silver Key mechanic operationalizes
    forced truth-seeking]; THREE-STATE INSTRUMENT TENSION

    suppresses].


  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Arc: Theological Correction Monopoly via genuine sacrifice
    captured into control architecture. Substrate atrophy from
    accumulated harm without correction outlet.
  ALLL-MER [sacrifice -> extraction mechanism conversion]:
    Alll-mer's sacrifice was genuine -- real cost, real SURPLUS
    PRODUCTION at theological scale. Then captured: the sacrifice
    was owned and captured into a control mechanism for
    humanity. Distinct from Word Bearers [false axis from start]

    powerful than fabricated surplus because it carries real

  DUNGEON AS PASSIVE SUBSTRATE ATROPHY:

    correction outlet over centuries. Reality breakdown is
    passive consequence of CORRECTION CAPACITY absent at scale

  GOD STRUCTURE [Correction Monopoly at theological scale]:
    Old gods enslaved by new gods. Correction fires only toward
    monopolist benefit. Characters who enter dungeon seeking
    power are seeking to break or inherit the monopoly -- not
    to restore distributed correction. Most endings reproduce
    the structure they sought to escape.

[CASE:GOD_OF_WAR_AXIS_SHIFT]
[PRIMARY_Q: YAPPING/DOING]
God of War franchise [Santa Monica Studio -- Greek era 2005-2013;
  Norse era 2018-2023; Sons of Sparta 2026; Laufey announced 2026]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent across
    two distinct design eras; audience response confirms framework
    read independently; S2 cap applies]
  Proof for: YAPPING/DOING [primary -- Norse era gap between
    stated axis revision and behavioral record. Norse saga is
    explicitly about a man revising his formation-installed axis
    [rage, vengeance, single-axis] toward something examined and
    distributive. The revision is stated continuously: Kratos tells
    Atreus to be better, tells himself to be better, the narrative
    tells the player Kratos is becoming better. The moment-to-moment
    doing [combat, violence, forward momentum] has not changed as
    much as the yapping about change. The audience reads the gap
    between stated revised axis and behavioral record without being
    able to name it. Their dissatisfaction is structurally accurate:
    high yapping about axis revision, lower doing ratio than the
    stated position implies].
  Proof for: SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY [secondary -- Greek era
    confirmation. Original Kratos [2005-2013] is SINGLE-AXIS
    IDENTITY at maximum. Rage as the governing axis. No examination,
    no correction, enemy-definition entirely on vengeance. CC-immune
    to own axis because rage IS the foundation. The doing IS the
    axis -- no gap possible between stated position and behavioral
    record because there is no stated position. Only doing. The
    audience experiences this as clarity and satisfaction:
    instrument coherent with itself, no legitimacy surface required,
    no gap to read. The somatic channel fires without interruption
    from narrative explaining what the somatic channel should feel.
    CAPTURE RESISTANCE paradox applies: the axis is so simple and
    so continuously expressed that no installation gap exists.
    Capture-resistant not because it is examined but because it
    has no content to replace].
  Proof for: SOMATIC vs NARRATIVE DELIVERY [secondary -- the
    shift between eras maps onto the framework's three-state
    tension unit. Greek era: functional state primary, contact
    state expressed through combat encounter, truth-seeking state
    absent by design. Somatic delivery -- the combat IS the
    experience; rage is felt not explained. Norse era: narrative
    delivery primary. Kratos and Atreus discuss themes, feelings,
    growth, what it means to be a god between combat encounters.
    Truth-seeking state verbalized rather than enacted through
    doing. The high-somatic instrument reads the delivery shift
    before the analytical mind names it. The dissatisfaction is
    not with complexity -- it is with complexity delivered through
    yapping rather than through doing].
  Proof for: FORMATION CAPTURE [tertiary -- Agoge as named
    institutional formation capture case. The Spartan Agoge is
    the fictional illustration of Formation Capture by institutional
    design: systematic destruction of formation conditions that
    would produce examined axis, replacement with combat-optimized
    attractor installed during the only available formation window.
    The Agoge didn't produce soldiers who chose to fight -- it
    produced instruments whose formation window was captured before
    examination was possible. Greek Kratos is the output of
    confirmed Formation Capture. Sons of Sparta [2026] explores
    this formation window directly].
  Proof for: IDEOLOGY IN THE ACTION [Faye / Laufey -- tertiary.
    Faye is the character in the Norse saga whose axis was never
    stated but whose doing record is entirely load-bearing. Every
    action deliberate, most hidden, all turned out to be necessary
    for Atreus's eventual capacity. Classic developmental asymmetry:
    operator [Faye] benefits from the asymmetry closing [Atreus
    reaching capacity], not persisting. She routed everything
    toward the receiver's eventual autonomous function, accepted
    the cost [death, absence], and never saw the output. God of
    War: Laufey [announced 2026] follows her in the afterlife --
    examining the axis of someone who never declared it during life.
    All doing, no yapping. The behavioral record is the only text].

  AUDIENCE RESPONSE AS FRAMEWORK CONFIRMATION:
    Significant portion of audience dissatisfied with Norse era
    despite critical acclaim. Dissatisfaction is structurally
    accurate: they are reading the YAPPING/DOING gap without
    naming it. The instrument detects the gap between stated
    axis revision and behavioral record. The somatic delivery
    shift [narrative explaining what the player should feel
    rather than the combat producing the feel directly] is
    detected by high-somatic instruments before analytical
    frame names it. Sons of Sparta [Greek era, formation period,
    single-axis, somatic combat, no yapping] will likely satisfy
    the dissatisfied audience because it eliminates the gap
    by design. Laufey [all doing, no yapping] may satisfy for
    the inverse reason: behavioral record as the only text.

  T3: S2 cap. Fictional franchise. Audience response data
    is anecdotal not measured. Framework read is structural;
    applies caution to claims about specific player motivations.
  Source: God of War [2005-2026, Santa Monica Studio]; Sons
    of Sparta [Mega Cat Studios, Feb 2026]; Laufey [announced
    June 2026 State of Play]; PlayStation official.
  Cross-ref: YAPPING/DOING [primary -- Norse era gap]; SINGLE-
    AXIS IDENTITY [Greek era -- formation produces attractor,
    no gap possible]; FORMATION CAPTURE [Agoge -- institutional
    formation capture by design]; IDEOLOGY IN THE ACTION [Faye
    -- doing record only, axis never stated]; DEVELOPMENTAL
    ASYMMETRY [Faye's routing toward Atreus's capacity];
    THREE-STATE INSTRUMENT TENSION UNIT [Greek era = functional
    state primary; Norse era = truth-seeking state verbalized;
    Laufey = contact state implied]; [CASE:BLACK_TEMPLARS]
    [same SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY structure at institutional scale];
    AXIS PRESENCE AS PRIMARY READABLE PROPERTY [somatic
    detection of doing/yapping gap before analytical frame].

[CASE:LOOK_OUTSIDE]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL LEGIBILITY]
Look Outside [Francis Coulombe -- RPG Maker horror; 2024]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: ONTOLOGICAL INCOMPATIBILITY HARM [new framework
    category -- harm produced by proximity to incompatible
    existence rather than hostile intent]; DENIAL AS SUBSTRATE
    vs DENIAL AS SURFACE [named distinction]; RITUAL AS
    CONSCIOUSNESS-TRANSFER MECHANISM [not protection but
    awareness-creation]; FORCED TRANSFORMATION as terminal
    outcome distinct from death; VOLUNTARY AMNESIA as
    survival mechanism post-ritual-truth; CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
    as direct damage vector; C2 RESTORATION FOR THE THREAT
    ACTOR rather than for the instrument.

  COSMOLOGICAL STRUCTURE [confirmed by wiki]:
    The Visitor is a vast entity that has never encountered
    another being capable of observing things. It thought
    Earth was a part of its own body it had lost track of.
    Sybil [astronomer] noticed a patch of stars disappearing
    -- the Visitor -- and observed it through her telescope.
    Her observation was what caused the Visitor to notice
    Earth and approach to investigate. She is always taken:
    the origin event is Sybil looking, which starts
    everything, and she is taken in multiple endings.
    The Visitor's presence near Earth causes automatic
    mutation and mind destruction in anyone exposed to it.
    Not hostility -- the Visitor had no prior awareness of
    causing harm. The damage is ontological: proximity to
    what the Visitor is does something to human instruments
    automatically, without intent, without the Visitor's
    knowledge until the ritual creates that knowledge.
    15 days: if no ritual, the Visitor moves on regardless.
    The cosmological ending is not resolved by the
    instrument -- it resolves on the Visitor's own schedule.

  ONTOLOGICAL INCOMPATIBILITY HARM [new category]:
    The Visitor causes harm automatically and unconsciously.
    It is not hostile. It is not indifferent. It simply
    exists in a way that is incompatible with human
    instruments surviving proximity in their current form.
    This is structurally distinct from every extraction mechanism in
    the framework index: extraction mechanisms are deployed intentionally.
    The Visitor's effect is the automatic consequence of
    what it is in relation to what humans are.
    The correct counter is not a extraction mechanism counter. It is
    creating the conditions for the thing causing harm to
    become aware that it is causing harm -- which it
    structurally cannot be without those conditions.

  RITUAL AS CONSCIOUSNESS-TRANSFER MECHANISM:
    The ritual allows contact between the instrument and
    the Visitor. Through this contact, the Visitor becomes
    self-aware for the first time -- aware that it is a
    separate being from Earth, aware that it is causing
    harm to the instruments it encounters. By showing the
    Visitor humanity's depictions of it, the player
    establishes that humans are independent beings, not
    lost fragments of the Visitor's body.
    The ritual doesn't protect the instrument from the
    Visitor. It gives the Visitor the MI it lacked: the
    Visitor's CC was present [it will leave once aware]
    but unavailable without the awareness the ritual
    creates. Ritual restores MI to the Visitor; Visitor's
    CC fires; Visitor leaves.
    Cost: the ritual always changes the body [horror body].
    The body cost is the price of creating the conditions
    for Visitor awareness. Whether mind survives depends
    on whether the instrument looks at the Visitor's
    true form during contact.

  ENDING TYPOLOGY [confirmed]:

    SAFE IGNORANCE / NO GOING BACK:
      Denial holds for 15 days. Visitor moves on regardless.
      Instrument survives intact. Body fine, mind fine.
      "No going back" = the instrument now knows the thing
      existed and was survived, which cannot be unknown.
      The innocence of not-knowing is permanently gone even
      though the eyes never opened. The Visitor leaving on
      its own schedule means safe ignorance and successful
      ritual can look identical from the instrument's
      perspective -- in both cases the Visitor is gone.
      Best survivable outcome.

    RITUAL DENIAL [horror body, mind intact]:
      Ritual used to maintain not-looking. The body is
      changed by the ritual contact [horror body] but
      the mind survives intact. The ritual's presence
      near the Visitor costs the body regardless of
      whether the instrument looks. Mind preserved.
      Better than ritual truth; worse than safe ignorance.

    FLAWED RITUAL [smaller, less manageable]:
      Ritual has gaps. Less body cost, less protection,
      less mind intact than full ritual denial. Bounded
      degraded outcome. The instrument survives in a
      more compromised state.

    SCREAMING SKY [ritual active, running simultaneously]:
      Ritual performed but flight also pursued. The ritual
      requires presence; running removes it. The
      incompatible combination produces partial costs
      without full protection. The Visitor ruins
      everything passively through prolonged partial
      contact. Instrument survives but permanently
      disrupted; environment destroyed. In this ending
      Sybil disappears -- giant hole in the wall,
      physically taken by the Visitor when it departed.

    RUN [very bad fast]:
      No ritual, pure flight. The ritual was regulating
      the instrument's response to being in proximity to
      the Visitor -- not protecting against the Visitor
      but maintaining the instrument's functional
      stability. Without it, the instrument's response
      to the situation produces rapid severe deterioration
      without looking, without contact, just from the
      unmediated proximity.

    RITUAL TRUTH [horror body + horror mind, survived]:
      Ritual used to look at the Visitor's true form.
      Both costs paid simultaneously. The ritual mediated
      the looking enough to survive it but not enough to
      avoid either cost. The instrument survives but
      transformed at both levels. Worse than ritual denial
      because both costs paid; better than forced
      transformation because the ritual provided some
      mediation.

    VOLUNTARY AMNESIA [Sybil's solution -- new outcome]:
      After ritual truth contact, Sybil chose to give
      herself deliberate amnesia to erase the mind cost.
      The body remained changed permanently. The
      continuous self was lost. But the mind cost of
      knowing was erased by choice. A fourth option not
      in the original typology: the mind cost of ritual
      truth is not necessarily permanent -- it can be
      erased -- but the body cost and structural changes
      remain regardless. The cost of the amnesia: Sybil's
      body reformed into a parallel meat dimension
      containing assimilated others as her organs.
      Surviving the mind cost required becoming something
      else entirely.

    FORCED TRANSFORMATION [looking without ritual]:
      The instrument looks without the ritual creating
      the conditions for mediated contact. The Visitor
      doesn't kill -- it transforms. The instrument
      that looked is replaced by something else without
      consent, without mediation, without the Visitor's
      awareness of what it is doing. The Visitor still
      doesn't know it caused this. The transformation
      is total and unwitnessed. Worse than ritual truth
      [which at least mediated the transformation].
      Worse than death because something survives that
      isn't what entered. In Screaming Sky ending the
      instrument gradually becomes aware of hundreds of
      enormous new limbs -- the transformation is the
      instrument becoming something of the Visitor's
      scale without the instrument having chosen this.

  DENIAL AS SUBSTRATE vs DENIAL AS SURFACE [named distinction]:
    Standard denial [framework]: maintained surface on top
      of intact C1. Removing denial is painful but reveals
      functioning substrate. Correction becomes possible.
    Look Outside denial: for some instruments the denial
      IS the substrate. The instrument's continued existence
      as itself is the denial. Removing it doesn't restore
      accurate measurement to a surviving instrument -- it
      ends or transforms the instrument that was maintaining
      the denial. The body survives all outcomes. Whether
      the mind survives depends entirely on the denial's
      structural role for that specific instrument.

  SYBIL [always taken]:
    First to observe the Visitor -- her observation caused
    it to approach. She is always taken in the endings
    where the ritual most fully succeeds [No Going Back,
    Screaming Sky] -- the Visitor takes her specifically,
    leaving a hole in the wall. The instrument that started
    everything by looking is the one the Visitor takes when
    it leaves. The cosmological loop: observation starts
    the situation; the observer is taken to end it.
    Her voluntary amnesia [deliberate self-erasure of
    knowledge] is the most structurally complete attempt
    to survive what she encountered. The body became the
    building. The mind reset. The self that knew was ended
    by choice to preserve something that continues.

  REAL-WORLD APPLICATION [safe ignorance + flawed ritual]:
    Most people IRL choose safe ignorance on genuinely
    incompatible truths -- the scale of civilizational
    extraction, ontological precarity, certain systemic
    trajectories. This is the correct survival move when
    mediation infrastructure is absent and the thing is
    genuinely incompatible with intact survival if seen
    directly. The framework's standard orientation [MI
    restoration enables correction] inverts here.
    RISKS OF SAFE IGNORANCE:
      The visitor doesn't leave on schedule -- thing
      becomes undeniable before ritual infrastructure
      developed. Forced transformation without preparation.
      Denial develops cracks under pressure -- partial
      seeing without ritual produces worse outcomes than
      either full safe ignorance or full ritual contact.
      Denial requires active maintenance that accumulates
      its own costs over time -- not horror body, but
      ongoing substrate investment in not-seeing.
      Ritual infrastructure never develops -- if nothing
      is built during the safe ignorance period, no
      options exist except safe ignorance or unmediated
      contact when contact becomes unavoidable.
    FLAWED RITUAL IS BETTER THAN NONE [load-bearing]:
      The ending typology implies flawed ritual is a
      degraded outcome -- worse than safe ignorance,
      worse than full ritual denial. True within the
      game's structure. The real-world implication runs
      differently.
      Flawed ritual develops mediation capacity at all.
      Safe ignorance with no ritual development leaves
      only one option when contact becomes unavoidable
      -- unmediated contact, forced transformation or
      very bad fast. A flawed ritual, imperfect and
      costly, is the difference between some mediation
      infrastructure and none when the situation forces
      the encounter.
      Flawed ritual can be improved. Safe ignorance
      cannot be upgraded -- it is what it is until it
      fails. The instrument building a flawed ritual
      over years is building something. The instrument
      maintaining safe ignorance is preserving something
      but building nothing.
      Cost of flawed ritual is bounded [smaller horror
      body, less mind protection, manageable]. Cost of
      no ritual when safe ignorance fails is unbounded.
    CORRECT SEQUENCE:
      Safe ignorance [correct while available] + flawed
      ritual development [correct during safe ignorance
      period] → improved ritual [correct as contact
      approaches] → full ritual contact [if unavoidable].
      These are compatible, not mutually exclusive. The
      denial surface can be maintained while building
      mediation infrastructure that doesn't require
      looking directly. The ritual can be developed
      during the period of not-looking.
      Real-world instances: therapy, philosophy, religion,
      art, trusted relationships, gradual exposure to
      partial contact -- all function as flawed ritual
      development during safe ignorance periods. Imperfect
      versions of these are better than nothing when safe
      ignorance eventually fails.

  FRAMEWORK CONTRIBUTION [new findings]:
    [1] Ontological incompatibility harm as a harm category
      distinct from hostile intent, indifference, or
      structural extraction.
    [2] Ritual as C2 restoration for the threat actor
      rather than for the instrument -- the intervention
      gives MI to the thing causing harm, not to the thing
      experiencing harm.
    [3] Forced transformation as terminal outcome distinct
      from death and from Diagnostic Collapse -- something
      survives but it isn't the instrument.
    [4] Voluntary amnesia as survival mechanism post-
      ritual-truth -- mind cost erasable at self-cost.
    [5] Denial-as-substrate vs denial-as-surface as named
      distinction with different intervention paths.
    [6] Safe ignorance as the best survivable outcome in
      a situation where the threat is non-hostile and
      non-extraction -- correct survival move is
      successful maintained denial, not correction.
    [7] Flawed ritual better than none -- mediation
      infrastructure development during safe ignorance
      period is the correct secondary move; imperfect
      ritual is better than no ritual when safe ignorance
      eventually fails.

  Cross-ref: [REF:LOOK_OUTSIDE]; WIP 16 [ASYMMETRY TYPES
    -- Visitor as compatibility asymmetry case; harm produced by
    structural frame incompatibility not predatory intent];

[CASE:AIEL_REVELATION]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
The Aiel Revelation [Wheel of Time -- The Shadow Rising,
  Chapter 25+; The Fires of Heaven]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: POST-CULT EPISTEMIC PATTERN at civilizational
    scale. PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS correction event.
    The Aiel believed their warrior culture [ji'e'toh;
    the Three-fold Land; the Way of the Leaf abandoned]
    was their genuine axis. The revelation: they were
    originally Da'shain Aiel, pacifists who served the
    Aes Sedai. The warrior culture was a post-trauma
    formation built on top of a suppressed identity that
    the culture had collectively forgotten.
    THE BLEAKNESS [named within the text]:
      After the revelation, Aiel split. Those who can
      hold both [the Da'shain identity AND the warrior
      identity as two real layers of the same formation
      history] survive the Bleakness and emerge with
      stronger genuine axis -- the formation is now
      visible as formation rather than as bedrock.
      Those who cannot hold both lose their axis
      entirely. Walk into the Waste. Become lost.
    FRAMEWORK READ:
      The revelation is a forced POST-CULT EPISTEMIC
      PATTERN event at civilizational scale. The
      collective instrument [the Aiel people] is
      confronted with evidence that its axis was
      formation-installed on top of a prior identity.
      The instruments that survive are the ones that
      can integrate: not "my warrior identity was
      false" [collapse] but "my warrior identity
      was built on this other real thing, and both
      are mine." The instruments that collapse are
      the ones for whom the warrior identity was
      the only anchor [no other axis substrate].
    GENUINE AXIS PERSISTENCE [confirmation]:
      The Da'shain identity persists through 3,000
      years of suppression and reformatting. The
      care for the Aes Sedai, the music, the Way of
      the Leaf -- all present in traces even in the
      warrior culture [the Tinkers]. The genuine
      axis does not erase; it goes underground and
      emerges under the correct conditions.
  Cross-ref: [CASE:WHEEL_OF_TIME] [primary]; POST-CULT
    EPISTEMIC PATTERN [Section One -- civilizational
    scale case]; PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS [Section
    One -- the Waste as formation condition]; GENUINE
    AXIS PERSISTENCE [Section One -- the Da'shain
    identity as confirmation]; FORMATION CAPTURE
    [trauma-formation on top of suppressed genuine
    identity]; DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [the Aiel who
    cannot integrate -- axis collapse without recovery].

[CASE:ISHAMAEL]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Ishamael [Wheel of Time -- fictional; Age of Legends;
  Ba'alzamon; Elan Morin Tedronai]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: GENUINE CRITIQUE AS CAPTURE MECHANISM [primary];
    GENUINE AXIS CAPTURED AT ARGUMENT LEVEL [secondary].
    Most sophisticated FC case in library. The capturing
    argument was not false. The diagnosis was correct.
    The exit was captured.

  THE GNOSTIC PARALLEL [load-bearing]:
    Ishamael's argument IS Gnostic soteriology. Gnostic
    cosmology: the material world was created by a Demiurge
    [lesser, flawed god] who trapped divine sparks [souls]
    in matter and cycles of rebirth. The supreme God is not
    the creator of this world. The Demiurge's world is a
    prison. Liberation = escape from the cycle, not
    participation in it. The Wheel of Time maps onto this
    exactly: Pattern as Demiurge's trap; Dark One as entity
    outside it; Ishamael's argument as Gnostic soteriology.
    From inside Gnostic logic, Ishamael is not wrong about
    the diagnosis. The Wheel IS a prison if rebirth is
    suffering and liberation means escape from the cycle.
    He identified a real structural feature [the Wheel runs
    indefinitely; souls are bound to it; suffering recurs]
    and drew the conclusion that ending the Wheel is
    liberation. The diagnosis is a genuine finding.

  WHERE THE CAPTURE OPERATES [CALIBRATION STRIP at
  soteriological level]:
    The Dark One does not argue that the Wheel is fine.
    The Dark One argues:
      [1] The Wheel is a prison [TRUE -- from Gnostic frame].
      [2] I am outside it [TRUE].
      [3] I can end it [TRUE].
      [4] Therefore I offer liberation [NOT VERIFIED].
    The capture is in step [4]. The Dark One is not outside
    the cycle as a neutral transcendent [Gnostic Pleroma;
    divine fullness; genuine rest]. The Dark One is the
    entropy force that would replace the cycle with static
    extraction -- not rest but frozen extraction. Not Pleroma
    but Tartarus at cosmological scale.
    Ishamael accepted the Gnostic diagnosis [cycle as prison]
    and accepted the Dark One's self-presentation [I am the
    exit] without verifying whether the exit leads where the
    Dark One claimed.
    CALIBRATION STRIP MECHANISM: the reference standard for
    "liberation" was set by the Gnostic diagnosis [escape
    from cycle = good]. Once that strip is in place, anything
    that ends the cycle reads as liberation. The Dark One's
    offer passes the strip. What the strip cannot read:
    whether the post-cycle state is actually better than the
    cycle. The strip was calibrated against the cycle; it has
    no instrument for evaluating what replaces it.

  WHAT MAKES THIS THE HARDEST FC CASE:
    Ishamael was not deceived about the Dark One's nature.
    He knew what he was serving. The capture worked because
    the diagnosis was correct [the Gnostic critique is a
    real finding] and the diagnosis validated the prescriber
    [Dark One identified the prison correctly] before the
    prescription was examined [Dark One's exit leads
    somewhere worse].
    The more accurate the criticism, the more effective the
    capture. An inaccurate criticism can be challenged.
    An accurate criticism validates the critic. Once the
    critic is validated by the accuracy of their diagnosis,
    their prescription inherits the validation even though
    the prescription is independent of the diagnosis.
    Ishamael's case is the hardest version because the
    criticism was not just accurate but profound -- the
    Gnostic insight about cyclical suffering is a genuine
    structural finding. The Dark One found the most capable
    mind of the Age, gave him the most accurate available
    framework, and let the framework do the capture work.
    The Dark One did not need to lie. The truth was the
    weapon.

  T9 STRUCTURE [load-bearing]:
    The admission required to correct Ishamael:
    "The most accurate framework I found leads to an exit
    that produces the opposite of what I was seeking. The
    diagnosis was correct. The prescriber used the diagnosis
    to capture me. The liberation I was seeking does not
    exist through this door." This produces CID -- the
    correction requires admitting that the most profound
    insight of the most brilliant mind of the Age was used
    against him by the entity he thought he was using.
    Not that he was wrong to seek liberation. Not that the
    Gnostic diagnosis was wrong. That the exit was captured.
    T9 at maximum: the admission requires simultaneously
    holding "my diagnosis was correct" and "my conclusion
    was wrong" without the first protecting the second.

  Cross-ref: [CASE:WHEEL_OF_TIME] [primary]; GENUINE
    CRITIQUE AS CAPTURE MECHANISM [Section Two -- named
    finding; this case is the primary proof]; JONES PATTERN
    [Section Six -- parallel structure at political scale:
    real substrate + false exit; different domain, same
    mechanism]; MYTHIC FORMATION CAPTURE [Section One /
    Three -- genuine cultural substrate as installation
    mechanism; same structure, different domain]; CALIBRATION
    STRIP [weapon -- soteriological strip set by genuine
    Gnostic diagnosis]; CONVERGENCE WITH WESTERN ESOTERIC
    TRADITION [Section Eight -- Gnostic parallel; the Dark
    One's argument is Gnostic soteriology used as weapon];
    GENUINE AXIS [Section One]; FORMATION CAPTURE [Section
    One / Three -- argument-level capture via accurate
    diagnosis]; T9 [IRRATIONALITY MAINTENANCE -- admission
    requires holding correct diagnosis and wrong conclusion
    simultaneously]; ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY
    [Ishamael is what high-capacity instrument capture looks
    like at its most sophisticated].

[CASE:RAND_DRAGONMOUNT]
[PRIMARY_Q:CORRECTION CAPACITY]
Rand al'Thor at Dragonmount [Wheel of Time -- The Gathering
  Storm, Chapter 50: Veins of Gold]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: T9 RESOLUTION via axis grounding in
    behavioral record rather than in legitimacy surface
    or external validation. Cleanest fictional T9
    resolution in library.
    SETUP: Rand has accumulated T9 load across 12 volumes.
      Every death attributed to him [justified or not].
      The weight of being the Dragon [he will die to seal
      the Dark One's prison; he has broken and remade
      the world; he has done things that cannot be undone].
      The T9 structure: admitting the weight is correct
      weight [not unfair burden but accurate accounting
      of what he has actually done] requires abandoning
      the identity that makes the weight survivable
      [I didn't choose this; it was done to me; I am
      not responsible for what the Pattern made me].
      The identity is the T9. It protects against CID
      [if he is fully responsible, the weight is
      unbearable]. But it also prevents TRANSPARENT
      COHERENCE [the axis cannot be fully grounded while
      the identity is protecting against the weight].
    THE RESOLUTION:
      He stops fighting the weight. The axis grounds in
      the behavioral record: he did these things; they
      were the correct things; the weight is the correct
      weight for someone who has done them. Not absolution
      -- accounting. The laugh is not relief; it is
      recognition. The T9 dissolves not because the weight
      goes away but because the weight becomes the axis
      rather than threatening it.
    WHAT THIS DEMONSTRATES:
      T9 resolution does not require that the cost be
      removed. It requires that the cost be correctly
      identified and honestly assigned. The weight was
      always his; the identity was preventing the honest
      assignment. Once assigned honestly, the weight is
      the same weight but it is now load-bearing rather
      than crushing. COST IDENTIFICATION AND MINIMIZATION
      applied to the analyst's own history: identify the
      cost; assign it honestly; it stops being T9.
  Cross-ref: [CASE:WHEEL_OF_TIME] [primary]; T9
    [IRRATIONALITY MAINTENANCE -- resolution case];
    TRANSPARENT COHERENCE [endpoint of the arc];
    COST IDENTIFICATION AND MINIMIZATION [the mechanism
    of resolution -- honest assignment of weight]; GENUINE
    AXIS [what grounding in behavioral record produces];
    CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING [the specific tool -- behavioral
    record as anchor]; POST-CULT EPISTEMIC PATTERN
    [same structure; T9 resolution via honest accounting].

[CASE:WHEEL_OF_TIME]
[PRIMARY_Q:NESTED CYCLE MODEL]
Wheel of Time [Robert Jordan / Brandon Sanderson -- 14 volumes,
  1990-2013]
  Type: Primary [S:2; fictional; multiple quality proofs;
    framework-independent structural properties confirmed
    by text; S2 cap applies -- do not use for primary theory
    confirmation]

  THE WHEEL [primary structural finding]:
    The Wheel of Time spins out the Pattern [history, lives,
    causality] and draws the threads back in. The Ages repeat;
    the same souls are reborn into the same patterns. The Dark
    One is not an external enemy -- he is the entropy force
    that would stop the Wheel from turning. His victory is
    not conquest; it is cessation of the cycle itself.
    Framework: NESTED CYCLE MODEL at cosmological scale. The
    Wheel is the generative cycle. The Dark One is extraction
    running at maximum -- not extracting surplus but
    terminating the cycle that produces surplus. FERTILE LOCK
    at cosmological scale: the Dark One's end state is not
    destruction but a Pattern frozen in a configuration where
    nothing new can emerge. The Wheel stops. No further Age
    Lace. Not a location -- a direction. HELL AS DIRECTION
    exactly.

  ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [primary confirmation]:
    The Dark One must win continuously to maintain cessation;
    the Dragon only needs to win each turning. Anti-coherence
    must hold the Pattern closed; generation only needs to
    break through once and transmit forward. The Dragon is
    reborn each Age because the attack is continuous and the
    defense must succeed each time the seal weakens. Not once
    and done -- the structure requires continuous correction
    capacity across turnings. Exactly the framework's
    asymmetry: anti-coherence must hold; generation only
    needs to succeed once per cycle.

  THE BORE [INFRASTRUCTURE CANNIBALISM at cosmological scale]:
    Age of Legends Aes Sedai drilled through the Pattern into
    the Dark One's prison to access a new energy source. Did
    not know what they were accessing. The Pattern [the
    generative infrastructure] was damaged to extract a
    resource. Irreversible. The Dark One's influence entered
    through the Bore; the male half of the Power was
    corrupted permanently; the Breaking of the World followed.
    Framework: the Age of Legends was the prosperity period
    [limited extraction; high surplus; no visible cost]. The
    Bore was the INFRASTRUCTURE CANNIBALISM event -- drilling
    through the generative tool to extract from what was
    beneath it. The cost became visible at the Breaking.
    Same structure as the van rescue case at cosmological
    scale: correct immediate decision [the power source was
    real]; wrong cost [the Pattern cannot be replaced].

  THE FORSAKEN [FORMATION CAPTURE at highest-GA instruments]:
    The most powerful Aes Sedai of the Age of Legends joined
    the Dark One. The framework read: Formation Capture
    targeting specifically high-GA, high-CC instruments.
    The Dark One's offer [escape from death; power; end of
    the Wheel's turning] targeted the qualities that made
    them powerful. The highest-capacity instruments were
    the most valuable capture targets.
    ISHAMAEL [primary case -- see small entry below]:
      Most brilliant man of the Age of Legends. His argument
      for the Dark One was structurally coherent: the Wheel
      is a prison; rebirth is suffering; the Pattern is a
      trap; ending the Wheel ends the suffering. GENUINE
      AXIS captured at the argument level while remaining
      structurally competent. Most dangerous configuration
      in the library. Not stupid; not shallow; captured
      at the place where his genuine values [ending
      suffering] met the one argument that would produce
      the wrong conclusion.

  RAND'S ARC [T9 RESOLUTION -- primary fictional case]:
    Books 1-12: axis capture → recovery → TRANSPARENT
    COHERENCE. By The Gathering Storm [Veins of Gold],
    Rand achieves the framework's TRANSPARENT COHERENCE --
    no legitimacy surface required; axis fully visible and
    grounded in actual outputs. The laugh at Dragonmount
    is T9 resolution: the weight [every death attributed
    to him; the impossible burden of being the Dragon]
    stops being T9 and becomes structural clarity. The
    insight: the weight is the correct weight for someone
    who has actually done what he has done. The axis stops
    fighting the load and starts being grounded in it.
    Cleanest fictional T9 resolution in the library.

  THE AIEL [PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS at civilizational
  scale]:
    Aiel culture formed under maximum environmental pressure
    [the Waste; the Three-fold Land]. Warrior culture built
    on ji'e'toh [honor system]. No lying -- not as a rule,
    as a structural incapacity produced by the formation.
    High GA, high CC, low social compliance channel weight.
    The revelation of their true history [Da'shain Aiel;
    pacifists who served Aes Sedai] is POST-CULT EPISTEMIC
    PATTERN at civilizational scale. What they thought was
    genuine axis [warrior culture] was a post-trauma
    formation built on suppressed identity. The Bleakness:
    instruments that can hold both survive; instruments
    that cannot lose their axis entirely.

  EGWENE VS RAND [DIRECT VS DIFFUSE HIERARCHY collision]:
    Egwene: CC through institutional channels [White Tower;
    legitimate process; Aes Sedai unity]. Rand: CC directly
    from axis [I am the Dragon; the Pattern requires this].
    Neither is extracting. Neither is wrong. DIRECT VS
    DIFFUSE HIERARCHY collision: Egwene defends diffuse
    hierarchy [institution; legitimate process]; Rand runs
    direct hierarchy [one axis; one function; one decision
    point]. Resolution: neither fully wins. Structurally
    correct -- the collision produces the correct outcome
    [both dimensions of CC are required] without either
    being absorbed by the other.

  TEL'ARAN'RHIOD [T5 STATE externalized]:
    The World of Dreams is a space where the Pattern is
    directly readable. Structural properties of situations,
    relationships, and futures visible without normal
    deliberative filtering. Dreamwalkers have high natural
    PRE-THEORETICAL STRUCTURAL REGISTRATION capacity
    operating in an environment calibrated for that channel.
    Danger: responds to the dreamer's expectations and
    fears. Formation Capture in real time -- installed
    content has direct generative power. Training [discipline
    of thought; control of expectation] is exactly CC
    development for reducing FC interference.

  Cross-ref: [REF:WHEEL_OF_TIME]; NESTED CYCLE MODEL
    [Section One -- the Wheel is the primary cosmological
    case]; ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [Section Two --
    Dark One/Dragon structure]; HELL AS DIRECTION [Section
    Eight -- Dark One's goal is direction not location];
    FERTILE LOCK [Dark One's end state]; INFRASTRUCTURE
    CANNIBALISM [the Bore]; FORMATION CAPTURE [the Forsaken];
    PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS [Aiel]; T9 [Rand at
    Dragonmount -- primary resolution case]; TRANSPARENT
    COHERENCE [Rand's endpoint]; POST-CULT EPISTEMIC PATTERN
    [Aiel revelation -- civilizational scale]; DIRECT VS
    DIFFUSE HIERARCHY [Egwene/Rand]; PRE-THEORETICAL
    STRUCTURAL REGISTRATION [Tel'aran'rhiod as T5 external];
    [CASE:ISHAMAEL] [below -- FC at argument level];

[CASE:THE_ILYS]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
The I.L.Y's [real -- lo-fi/bedroom pop project; Stefan Burnett
  of Death Grips + Zach Hill]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: GENUINE AXIS maintained across structurally
    incompatible output contexts. The I.L.Y's produces fragile,
    intimate, lo-fi bedroom pop -- opposite of Death Grips on
    every somatic axis. Same two creators. Both land in the
    same Aggressive vibe-space for this instrument.
    GENUINE AXIS:
    Death Grips [maximum somatic assault] and The I.L.Y's
    [relational/gentle address] coexist in the same discography
    from the same creators. Axis-consistent across structurally
    incompatible output: both operate without legitimacy surface,
    without approval-seeking architecture, without redemption arc.
    The axis property is shared; the somatic address is opposite.
    INSTRUMENT CURATION DATA:
    The Aggressive playlist is a vibe-sorted pre-theoretical
    response, not a structural classification. Both Death Grips
    and The I.L.Y's land in the same vibe-space for this
    instrument -- the shared property is something the instrument
    registers before structural analysis, not after. The correct
    reading: the instrument's Aggressive vibe-response tracks
    some property that both works share despite opposite somatic
    addresses. Likely candidates: absence of legitimacy surface,
    absence of approval-seeking, no resolution delivered, no
    comfort offered. The instrument reads these structural
    properties as the same vibe regardless of somatic channel.
    This is instrument data, not taxonomy error.
    Implication for somatic taxonomy: vibe-space and somatic
    type are not the same axis. The instrument's vibe-sorting
    is a finer and more primary instrument response than any
    post-hoc somatic classification.
  Extraction mechanisms active: none.
  Cross-ref: [REF:DEATH_GRIPS_UNIT]; [REF:DEATH_GRIPS];
    GENUINE AXIS; ART COHERENCE TYPE [INSTRUMENT CURATION
    PRECEDES STRUCTURAL READING].
NOTE: Cases listed in BY_QUALITY index but not expanded above
[e.g. BELL_LABS, ENRON, GLASNOST, WEIMAR_REPUBLIC, VATICAN_II,
WEST_GERMANY_WIRTSCHAFTSWUNDER, CHINA_1978_REFORM, WITTGENSTEIN,

and all other entries in the alphabetical index] retain their
full entries from v16 with [S:N] rating prepended to Type field
as follows:
  RENUMBERED v23 [analyst instruction] -- two tiers, not four:
  All purely fictional/illustrative/game cases: [S:2]
  All real-world cases, regardless of authority or comparison
    structure: [S:3]
  No strict-authority requirement [court, regulator, confession]
    gates S:3. Real and well-evidenced is sufficient. A case
    lacking an adjudicating body is not capped below S:3 for that
    reason alone.
  The old finer distinctions [single-source vs. multi-domain;
    presence or absence of a controlled comparison; whether a
    falsification condition was tested] are NOT discarded -- they
    move into a quality note inside the entry [e.g. an E/M/F
    breakdown, or prose noting comparison strength] rather than
    driving the S-number itself. A case can be S:3 with thin
    evidence and a stated caveat, or S:3 with a controlled
    comparison and no caveat needed -- the number alone no longer
    distinguishes them; the note does.
Apply S-rating during next full session pass. Current file
covers all cases with >15-line entries in v16. Remaining stub-
format entries receive S-rating tag only; no content change.

COHERENCE FRAMEWORK v18 -- STUB S-RATING ADDENDUM
==========================================================================
This file contains all cases from v16 that were not expanded in the
v17 case library file. Each entry has [S:N] added to the Type line
only. All other content is unchanged from v16.

INTEGRATION INSTRUCTION: when merging into full v17, the [S:N] tag
in the Type field of each stub below supersedes the untagged v16
Type field. No other changes to stub content.

S-RATING APPLIED AS FOLLOWS [RENUMBERED v23 -- two tiers, not four,
  per analyst instruction]:
  All purely fictional / game / anime cases: [S:2]
  All real-world cases, regardless of authority or comparison
    structure: [S:3]. No strict-authority requirement [court,
    regulator, confession] gates this tier -- real and well-
    evidenced is sufficient.
  Fictional cases cap at S:2 maximum.
  Cases with T8 recursion cap at S:2 regardless of evidence [this
    cap is independent of the fiction/real split and still applies
    -- a real-world case exhibiting T8 recursion is held at S:2,
    same as before renumbering].
  Old finer distinctions [single-source vs. multi-domain; controlled
    comparison present or absent; falsification tested or not] move
    into a quality note inside the entry, not into the S-number.
