[CASE:WELLS_FARGO_CROSS_SELL]
[PRIMARY_Q:CORRECTION CAPACITY]
Wells Fargo cross-selling scandal [real -- 2002-2016, public 2013-2018]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: Correction Theatre primary real-world proof. Internal
    correction infrastructure [24-hour EthicsLine, formal ethics
    training, "everything submitted to the EthicsLine is
    investigated"] fully visible and advertised -- CC absent in
    function while present in form. Employees who used the
    correction channel as designed were systematically identified
    and removed [fired within days of EthicsLine calls in multiple
    documented instances; retaliatory negative U5 industry records
    issued to terminated whistleblowers, damaging their ability to
    work in banking elsewhere]. The correction surface was inverted:
    it routed toward suppressing the signal, not acting on it.
    Resolved [2013-2018]: capture broken by reporting wholly outside
    the captured correction architecture, not by the architecture
    itself. Los Angeles Times [Reckard, Oct-Dec 2013] built the case
    through court-record review and on-record employee interviews
    over two months, independent of any Wells Fargo internal channel.
    External regulators [CFPB, OCC, LA City Attorney, later SEC/DOJ]
    then measured actual fraudulent-account counts and termination
    records rather than accepting the bank's self-reported "we
    investigate everything" claim -- this is the OUTPUT VERIFICATION
    mechanism exactly: specify the output correction would produce
    [cessation of unauthorized accounts], measure whether it exists
    [it did not -- accounts continued 2011-2016 despite the EthicsLine
    being fully operational throughout], reject activity evidence
    [hotline existence, investigation claims] as substitution for
    output evidence.
    Distinct from Enron: Enron's primary weapon was causal diffusion
    [opaque SPE structure]; Wells Fargo's primary weapon was
    correction-channel inversion [a working-looking complaint system
    that punished use]. Both real-world S4, both resolved by external
    reading, different weapon/tool pairing -- useful contrast pair.
  T3 Medium: well-documented via congressional testimony, regulatory
    settlements, and named on-record sources; low adversarial-reading
    risk given source convergence on the core facts [DOJ, SEC, CFPB,
    OCC, LA City Attorney, NPR investigation]. Convergence here is
    multiple independent investigators confirming the same external
    facts -- strong E-rating, but not a controlled comparison or a
    tested falsification condition, so capped at S3 rather than S4.
    Falsification condition for S4 promotion: a comparable bank with
    an equally aggressive cross-sell-incentive structure and an
    EthicsLine-style channel that did NOT exhibit the same retaliation
    pattern would weaken the CC-inversion reading; no such comparison
    case currently identified.
  Cross-ref: [REF:WELLS_FARGO_CROSS_SELL]; CORRECTION THEATRE [primary
    weapon]; OUTPUT VERIFICATION [external regulator/journalist
    measurement of actual output vs. claimed correction activity --
    functional match confirmed v23]; ENRON [contrast pair -- same
    resolution pattern, different weapon]; WIP 34 [Candidate 1 --
    retaliation for correct judgment, distinct from Competence
    Undermining, surfaced from this case].

[CASE:WORKPLACE_PSYCHOLOGICAL_SAFETY_ERS]
[PRIMARY_Q:MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
Workplace psychological safety -- clinical counselor data vs.
  institutional survey data [real -- Center for Organizational
  Effectiveness 2026 Psychological Safety Study; Gallup annual
  State of the Global Workplace; Batchelor 2026, The Conversation]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: EXTERNAL REFERENCE STANDARD [new application genre
    -- institutional health monitoring, not fraud detection].
    The 2026 COE study draws on anonymized clinical conversations
    from workers at over 100,000 companies employing 88 million
    people, obtained via licensed counselors in confidence. Gallup's
    annual engagement survey draws on direct employee self-report
    to an external polling instrument. Both are independent of the
    employers being measured. Both find a substantially different
    picture from what employers' own internal surveys and performance
    reviews produce: approximately 30% worker engagement nationally
    [lowest in more than a decade]; 46% of workers unsure what their
    employer expects from them [down from 56% in 2020]; chronic
    exhaustion as the new baseline of American employment.
    THE ERS MECHANISM CONFIRMED:
      The tool's definition: "independent measurement surface not
      owned or controlled by the actor whose outputs are being
      measured; instrument must be outside the boundary being read."
      The clinical counselor is structurally outside the employer's
      boundary -- data does not go to the employer, the worker knows
      this, and the confidentiality guarantee is the source of the
      signal's validity. The Gallup instrument is also outside the
      employer boundary, but records what workers are willing to say
      when surveyed -- a softer form of independence.
      Both instruments produce readings that diverge from what
      workers say to employer-owned instruments [internal engagement
      surveys, performance reviews, one-on-ones] -- and the gap
      between institution-controlled measurement and independent
      measurement IS the finding. Where these two measurement types
      diverge sharply, the institution's surface is reading its own
      legitimacy maintenance, not the actual substrate state.
    WHAT WEAPON FIRES AGAINST:
      Not MSC in its strong form [active manipulation of what people
      see] -- the workers themselves are producing the divergence.
      The mechanism is closer to Calibration Strip: employer survey
      data sets "what workers say when observed by their employer's
      instrument" as the reference standard for worker engagement.
      The clinical counselor data is the external reference standard
      that reveals the calibration point itself is wrong. Workers
      who feel psychologically unsafe go quiet on the calibrated
      surface; the independent instrument finds the actual state.
      MSC-adjacent effect without active MSC deployment: the
      captured measurement surface is maintained by worker
      self-protection behavior under psychological threat, not by
      the employer actively manipulating what workers can see. The
      result is identical [institution reads its own legitimacy
      surface rather than actual substrate state] but the mechanism
      is different. This is a useful new case because it shows ERS
      firing against a softer, self-maintaining version of
      measurement capture rather than against deliberate MSC.
    DISTINCT FROM FRAUD CASES:
      Villepreux Power, Glasnost, and DeepSeek all deploy ERS
      against an institution actively running MSC or a capture
      architecture. This case shows ERS firing in a non-adversarial
      context: no actor is deliberately falsifying the measurement
      surface. Workers maintain a legitimacy surface under
      institutional pressure without being directed to do so. The
      independent instrument [counselor data] is what makes the
      actual state readable. This broadens ERS's confirmed
      application range beyond fraud detection into institutional
      health monitoring -- where the gap between instruments is
      diagnostic even without deliberate deception.
    SECONDARY FINDING [methodology]:
      The reason clinical counselor data accesses a different signal
      than employer surveys is structural: psychological safety
      determines which instrument can access actual state. When
      safety is absent, only instruments structurally independent
      of the institution can read genuine signal. The study's
      methodological contribution is demonstrating this gap
      instrumentally -- the divergence between clinical data and
      survey data is itself a measurement of institutional
      psychological safety, independent of any self-report.
  T3 Low-Medium: Gallup engagement data is an established,
    annually replicated, multi-decade instrument with strong
    replication consistency. COE study methodology [100,000+
    organizations, 88M workers, clinical counselor sourcing]
    is large-N and uses a structurally independent measurement
    instrument. The gap between the two is consistently reported
    across multiple independent research teams. No falsification
    test yet [a workplace where employer-survey data and clinical
    data converge closely would be the comparison case; such
    cases exist in high-trust organizational cultures but are not
    yet formally compared against the low-trust pattern in the
    literature found this pass]. S:3.
  Cross-ref: [REF:WORKPLACE_PSYCHOLOGICAL_SAFETY_ERS]; EXTERNAL
    REFERENCE STANDARD [confirmed new application genre v24 --
    institutional health monitoring; fires against self-maintaining
    legitimacy surface rather than active MSC; see DISTINCT FROM
    FRAUD CASES note]; CALIBRATION STRIP [employer survey as
    calibration point; clinical data as the external standard
    revealing the calibration error]; MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
    [primary domain]; MEASUREMENT SURFACE CONTROL [adjacent --
    workers produce the MSC-adjacent effect under psychological
    pressure without employer direction; structural outcome
    identical to active MSC at the reading layer]; [CASE:WELLS_
    FARGO_CROSS_SELL] [contrast: WF shows active correction-
    channel inversion; this case shows passive measurement surface
    capture via worker self-protection behavior -- different
    mechanism, same ERS tool deployment pattern].

[CASE:VOLKSWAGEN_DIESELGATE]
[PRIMARY_Q:MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
Volkswagen emissions scandal / Dieselgate [real -- defeat device
  deployed ~2008-2015, exposed 2014-2015]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: MEASUREMENT DIFFICULTY PRINCIPLE primary real-world proof
    of the known-concealment branch. VW's "defeat device" software
    detected when a vehicle was undergoing the recognized laboratory
    test protocol and activated full emissions controls only under
    those conditions, reverting to non-compliant performance during
    normal real-world driving. This is MSC [Measurement Surface
    Control] implemented literally in code: the measurement surface
    itself was engineered to present a captured reading to the
    specific instrument expected to read it.
    Known-concealment tells confirmed: specific resistance to a
    particular measurement approach [behavior change keyed to test-
    cycle detection, not general malfunction]; correction fired
    against the measurement attempt, not the underlying problem
    [VW's first public response attributed the West Virginia
    University discrepancy to "technical issues" and "unexpected
    conditions" rather than addressing actual NOx output]. Genuine-
    unknowing tells absent: failure was not consistent across
    measurement approaches -- it was consistent ONLY under the
    expected test approach, which is itself the diagnostic signature
    of known concealment, confirmed by VW's own September 2015
    admission that the device was deliberately engineered.
    Resolved: capture broken when ICCT [funding an independent NGO
    study with no relationship to VW or to the regulatory testing
    architecture] commissioned West Virginia University to measure
    real-world emissions with portable equipment on actual roads,
    outside the captured laboratory/dynamometer surface the defeat
    device was built to recognize. This is EXTERNAL REFERENCE
    STANDARD's mechanism again [already calibrated], but the case's
    primary proof value is for the diagnostic distinction itself:
    cleanly demonstrates the known-concealment branch with a
    documented confession, not just inference.
  T3 Low: VW's own admission removes interpretive ambiguity on the
    known-concealment question specifically; multi-body regulatory
    convergence [EPA, CARB, ICCT, WVU] confirms the mechanism and
    timeline. Real-world, well-evidenced, S:3 under the renumbered
    scheme [v23] -- no strict-authority gate applies, and there is
    no higher tier this case is being held below. The comparison-
    strength detail [no controlled comparison case built out in this
    entry] is retained as a quality note, not as a cap. See E/M/F
    breakdown [REAL-WORLD CASES REQUIRING SPECIAL RATING NOTES] for
    that detail.
  Cross-ref: [REF:VOLKSWAGEN_DIESELGATE]; MEASUREMENT DIFFICULTY
    PRINCIPLE [primary real-world deployment, known-concealment
    branch -- functional match confirmed v23]; MSC [the defeat
    device as literal code implementation of the weapon]; EXTERNAL
    REFERENCE STANDARD [WVU/ICCT portable real-world testing as the
    resolving mechanism]; ENRON, WELLS_FARGO_CROSS_SELL [same
    resolved-capture cluster, same external-measurement resolution
    pattern, three different weapons].

[CASE:THERANOS]
[PRIMARY_Q:MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
Theranos [real -- founded 2003, fraud exposed 2015-2016, dissolved 2018]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: CAUSAL RECONSTRUCTION second case-library functional
    match [Enron is the first]. Theranos's "Edison" device was
    presented as performing 200+ proprietary diagnostic tests from a
    single finger-prick blood sample; the actual causal chain
    [diluted samples run on commercially available third-party
    machines, with the Edison device itself rarely used for the
    company's own marketed tests] was deliberately obscured behind
    proprietary-technology secrecy. This is ECD [Engineered Causal
    Diffusion] applied to a physical product rather than a financial
    or political structure -- the obscuring mechanism is the same:
    claim a causal chain, prevent independent inspection of the real
    one.
    Resolved by independent reconstruction from outside the
    company's controlled disclosure architecture, via two
    structurally distinct external vantage points rather than one:
    [1] journalistic -- WSJ reporter Carreyrou's investigation,
    corroborated by named former-employee sources [Tyler Shultz,
    Erika Cheung] who had direct internal observation of the actual
    testing process; [2] regulatory -- CMS's own surprise lab
    inspection [prompted independently by Cheung's letter to the
    agency, a separate channel from the WSJ reporting] found lab
    violations directly, without relying on the journalistic account.
    Two independent reconstructions of the same actual causal chain,
    arrived at via different methods and different evidence streams,
    converging on the same finding -- this is meaningfully stronger
    than Wells Fargo or Volkswagen's single-vantage convergence, and
    approaches but does not yet fully build out a controlled
    comparison [see S-rating note below].
  T3 Low-Medium: criminal convictions [Holmes, Balwani] establish the
    deception as judicially confirmed fact, not merely alleged;
    independent regulatory finding [CMS] and independent journalism
    [WSJ] are methodologically distinct enough to function as a weak
    controlled comparison [same causal claim, tested by two unrelated
    methods, same result] -- stronger E/M structure than Wells Fargo
    or Volkswagen, though still short of a clean near-case comparison
    [e.g. a contemporaneous competing diagnostics startup making
    similar claims that turned out to be genuine, which would sharpen
    the comparison further]. Real-world, well-evidenced, S:3 under
    the renumbered scheme [v23] -- this comparison-strength detail is
    a quality note, not a tier cap; flagged as having the strongest
    comparison structure of the three new cases this session given
    the two-vantage convergence already present.
  Cross-ref: [REF:THERANOS]; CAUSAL RECONSTRUCTION [second real case --
    tool already CONFIRMED on Enron alone under the revised one-real-
    case bar; this strengthens rather than completes the confirmation];
    ECD [primary weapon, physical-product variant]; ENRON [same tool,
    first confirmation, financial-structure variant]; BOUNDARY
    DECLARATION and INSTRUMENT CONFIDENCE RESTORATION checked and
    ruled out -- mechanism does not fit either [no universalism
    framing present; no formal track-record/behavioral-test pattern
    documented, only informal employee corroboration]; WIP 34
    [Candidate 3 -- George Shultz weighting Holmes's status over his
    own grandson's direct evidence is evaluator-side status weighting,
    a near-miss for Competence Undermining applied to a third party
    rather than the reporter -- not written into this entry's body
    text, surfaced only on retroactive scan].

[CASE:MADOFF]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Madoff Ponzi scheme [real -- operated ~1990s-2008, exposed 2008-2009]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: INSTRUMENT CONFIDENCE RESTORATION first real-world
    functional match. Madoff's claimed instrument was a "split-strike
    conversion" trading strategy producing steady 1-2% monthly returns
    with almost no losing months -- a near-45-degree return curve.
    Madoff refused independent third-party custody or verification,
    framing this as protecting proprietary strategy from competitive
    replication [a "black-box" claim functioning as a captured
    measurement surface, structurally similar to Calibration Strip].
    Harry Markopolos, asked by his employer to replicate Madoff's
    strategy commercially, ran the actual behavioral test the tool's
    mechanism specifies: attempted the claimed strategy himself using
    the same publicly available market conditions, and found it
    could not produce the claimed returns -- the volatility and
    options volume required would have exceeded total publicly
    reported market volume on the relevant dates. This is external
    confirmation of instrument accuracy via behavioral test, not
    social consensus, run specifically because Madoff's reputation
    [former Nasdaq chairman] was functioning as the social-consensus
    substitute the tool's mechanism is built to bypass. Markopolos
    explicitly distinguished instrument failure from weapon
    deployment in his own framing: he concluded the numbers were
    either fraud or front-running, not honest underperformance --
    correctly identifying that the claimed accuracy was the weapon,
    not a genuine reading.
    Distinct from Wells Fargo's near-miss on this tool: Markopolos
    never doubted his own judgment and was not the target of
    Competence Undermining: he ran a test on Madoff's claimed
    instrument, not a recovery of his own confidence. Resolution
    delayed nine years not because the tool failed -- Markopolos's
    test was correct from 1999 -- but because the SEC repeatedly
    failed to act on a valid external confirmation, a separate
    institutional-capture finding [SEC examiners described by their
    own later Inspector General report as failing to verify trading
    through an independent third party, the textbook check the
    instrument-confidence tool itself specifies].
  T3 Low-Medium: Madoff's own guilty plea and sentencing remove
    interpretive ambiguity on whether fraud occurred; Markopolos's
    mathematical analysis is independently reproducible from public
    information and was later validated by the SEC's own Inspector
    General report. Real-world, well-evidenced, S:3 under the
    renumbered scheme [v23]; judicial confirmation present, though
    no controlled comparison case [e.g. a contemporaneous fund with a
    similarly implausible return curve that turned out to be genuine]
    is built out in this entry -- noted as a quality detail, not a
    tier cap.
  Cross-ref: [REF:MADOFF]; INSTRUMENT CONFIDENCE RESTORATION [first
    real-world functional-match confirmation, v23]; GENUINE AXIS
    [Madoff's claimed axis was the strategy itself -- entirely
    fabricated, not degraded]; distinct from CAUSAL RECONSTRUCTION
    [Enron, Theranos -- obscured real chain] and EXTERNAL REFERENCE
    STANDARD [reading from outside the surface] -- Markopolos ran a
    behavioral replication test on the claimed mechanism itself,
    the mechanism unique to this tool; WIP 34 [Candidate 2 -- valid
    signal with institutional non-response, the SEC's nine-year
    failure to act on a correct external confirmation, surfaced from
    this case].

[CASE:2008_HOUSING_CRISIS]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
2008 subprime mortgage crisis / "The Big Short" [real -- bonds rated
  ~2004-2007, collapse 2007-2008]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: INSTRUMENT CONFIDENCE RESTORATION second real-world
    functional match [Madoff is the first]. Mortgage-backed bonds and
    CDOs received AAA ratings from Moody's, S&P, and Fitch -- a
    captured instrument, since the rating agencies were paid by the
    same banks issuing the bonds being rated, an explicit conflict-
    of-interest structure functioning as Calibration Strip at the
    measurement-instrument level. Michael Burry [and independently,
    Steve Eisman's team] ran the same class of behavioral test
    Markopolos ran on Madoff: rather than accepting the AAA rating as
    social consensus, Burry read the actual underlying loan-level
    prospectus data [individual mortgage documents nobody else on
    Wall Street had opened] and found that a large and growing share
    of underlying borrowers had no income verification, low credit
    scores, and adjustable rates structurally guaranteed to default
    once teaser periods ended. This directly tested and disproved
    instrument accuracy [the rating] using public information,
    independent of the rating agencies' own self-report.
    Distinct from Madoff: Burry's test was forward-predictive
    [identifying a structural default trigger before it fired] rather
    than backward-checking a return curve already claimed; the
    underlying weapon here is closer to a financial-incentive variant
    of Calibration Strip [the instrument's own creators paid for the
    measurement] than Madoff's black-box refusal of any verification
    at all. Both confirm the same tool via the same general
    mechanism: named behavioral test against the actual underlying
    data, not trust in the instrument's social standing.
  T3 Low-Medium: the Financial Crisis Inquiry Commission's official
    investigation and extensive subsequent litigation [including
    SEC actions against rating agencies] establish the rating-
    inflation mechanism as confirmed fact, not allegation. S:3 under
    the renumbered scheme: strong single confirmed application with
    broad institutional documentation; no controlled comparison case
    [e.g. a contemporaneous similarly-structured bond market that did
    NOT exhibit the same rating-agency conflict and did not collapse]
    built out in this entry, noted as a quality detail.
  Cross-ref: [REF:2008_HOUSING_CRISIS]; INSTRUMENT CONFIDENCE
    RESTORATION [second real-world functional-match confirmation,
    v23 -- strengthens, not required for, the Madoff confirmation];
    CALIBRATION STRIP [financial-incentive variant -- rating
    instrument's creators paid for its own measurement]; MADOFF
    [same tool, first confirmation, different weapon variant];
    BOUNDARY DECLARATION checked and ruled out -- no universalism
    claim present, this is direct financial fraud with a captured
    measurement instrument, not a particular interest framed as
    universal.

[CASE:BADIOU_UNIVERSALISM]
[PRIMARY_Q:BOUNDARY INTEGRITY]
Badiou's Pauline universalism critique [real -- Saint Paul: The
  Foundation of Universalism, 2003; scholarly reception ongoing]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: BOUNDARY DECLARATION first real-world functional match.
    Badiou's "Saint Paul" presents a universalism claim: Pauline grace
    as a truth-event indifferent to all particulars, dissolving
    distinctions of Greek/Jew, Jewish law versus Roman empire, in
    favor of a universal truth procedure available to anyone through
    fidelity to the event. On its own terms this presents as
    maximally inclusive -- contesting it risks looking like a defense
    of "mere particularism" against universal truth, which is the
    Universalism Trap's exact signature [contestation made to appear
    as opposition to universalism itself].
    Wolosky's published critique ["Badiou, Paul, and Anti-Judaism,"
    Telos] performs the tool's mechanism directly: asks whose
    universalism this is by close textual reading [Badiou's own
    language -- "the discourse of the Father is particular," "the
    Jews as those who practice specific injunctions" resist the One
    that "law cannot be inscribed on," "to be a Jew is to be excluded
    from the universality that reveals and constitutes truth"];
    names the particular interest the universal claim actually serves
    [a Christian-derived "event" structure that requires Jewish
    particularity specifically, not particularity in general, as its
    excluded remainder to function]; declares the boundary the claim
    is designed to dissolve [Jewish identity as a legible, defended
    category, rather than letting the universalism framing render its
    exclusion invisible as mere even-handed indifference to all
    particulars].
    Note on case strength: this is textual/philosophical critique, not
    fraud with a legal resolution -- the "weapon" here is rhetorical
    structure, not a financial or institutional capture mechanism. The
    critique is substantively engaged by the academic field [multiple
    independent scholars -- Delahaye, Bell -- responding to and citing
    Wolosky's reading], which functions as the closest available
    equivalent to convergent independent confirmation in this genre,
    but it lacks any external adjudicating body [no court, no
    regulator] that resolves the dispute the way Madoff's conviction
    or VW's admission do.
    RENUMBERED v23 [analyst instruction]: under the prior four-tier
    scheme this case was held at S2 specifically because it lacked a
    court or regulator. That requirement is removed -- real-world,
    well-evidenced cases no longer need an adjudicating authority to
    clear the real-world tier. This case is real-world [Badiou's text
    exists, Wolosky's published critique exists, the textual claims
    are independently verifiable by any reader] and is therefore S:3
    under the new scheme. The genre difference from Madoff or VW
    [no court, no regulator] remains true and worth noting -- it
    affects how MUCH independent corroboration exists, not whether
    the case counts as real. That distinction now lives in this
    prose note rather than in the S-number.
  Cross-ref: [REF:BADIOU_UNIVERSALISM]; BOUNDARY DECLARATION [first
    real-world functional-match confirmation, v23]; UNIVERSALISM TRAP
    [primary weapon -- particular interest framed as universal,
    contestation pre-framed as opposition to universalism itself];
    BOUNDARY INTEGRITY.

[CASE:GAP_STABLE_SCHEDULING_STUDY]
[PRIMARY_Q:SUBSTRATE REGENERATION]
Gap Stable Scheduling Study [real -- 28 Gap Inc. stores, San Francisco
  and Chicago; pretest 2015, full pilot Nov 2015-Aug 2016]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: ENVIRONMENTAL REDESIGN first real-world functional match.
    Pre-intervention scheduling at Gap stores was a Rhythm Disruption
    deployment in the precarity-architecture sense the weapon entry
    describes precisely: variable shift times, on-call shifts that
    could be cancelled hours before start, schedules finalized with
    little advance notice. This destroys cycle stability at the
    individual level -- workers cannot form a reliable rhythm no
    matter how they personally try to cope, because the disruption is
    structural, not a personal time-management failure.
    The intervention was not an individual-coping fix -- it was
    exactly the structural redesign Environmental Redesign specifies:
    fixed shift times, two-week advance schedule publication, and
    elimination of on-call cancellation entirely, applied at the
    scheduling-system level across 19 randomly assigned treatment
    stores [9 stores held as a true randomized control, not just an
    untreated comparison group]. Individual workers in the treatment
    stores did not personally negotiate better schedules; the
    environment producing the weapon condition was redesigned out
    from under them.
    Measured results directly confirm the tool's predicted effect:
    sleep quality improved 6-8%, psychological distress decreased,
    and -- notably -- business productivity and sales also improved
    [5.1% labor productivity, 7% median sales], directly contradicting
    the assumption that schedule instability is a necessary cost of
    doing business. This is a controlled comparison in the strongest
    sense available in the case library outside Glasnost/Enron: a
    true randomized controlled trial, not an after-the-fact natural
    comparison.
  T3 Medium-High: peer-reviewed, multi-university research team
    [University of Chicago, UC Hastings, UNC Kenan-Flagler], funded
    by multiple independent foundations, published methodology and
    full report available. Randomized controlled design with a true
    control group is a meaningfully stronger comparison structure
    than most cases in this library -- closer to Glasnost's Libya/
    Tunisia comparison or Enron's Watkins vantage point than to the
    single-source-convergence pattern of Wells Fargo or Volkswagen.
  Cross-ref: [REF:GAP_STABLE_SCHEDULING_STUDY]; ENVIRONMENTAL REDESIGN
    [first real-world functional-match confirmation, v23]; RHYTHM
    DISRUPTION [primary weapon countered -- precarity architecture,
    variable scheduling]; SUBSTRATE REGENERATION; IDLE CYCLE
    REQUIREMENT [adjacent mechanism, not directly tested by this
    case -- this case targets Rhythm Disruption specifically, not
    Idle Cycle Deprivation; worth a separate search for an ICD-
    specific case].

[CASE:IPV_ECONOMIC_EMPOWERMENT_RCT]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENERATIVE CYCLE]
IPV economic empowerment financial literacy programs [real -- Sanders
  et al. 2007 quasi-experimental; Postmus et al. 2015 randomized
  controlled longitudinal evaluation, n=449, 14 months]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: ALTERNATIVE SUBSTRATE CONSTRUCTION first real-world
    functional match. Exit Cost Construction's own definition --
    "substrate dependency construction over time; exit = total
    substrate loss; CC present but behaviorally blocked" -- is a
    precise structural description of intimate partner violence
    economic abuse, independently confirmed across decades of IPV
    research: survivors' correction capacity [recognizing the
    relationship is harmful] is frequently intact, but behaviorally
    blocked by total financial dependency. A 2018 NRCDV study found
    67% of survivors stayed longer than they wanted to, or returned,
    specifically due to financial concerns -- the textbook ECC
    signature of CC present but exit blocked by substrate loss.
    The documented effective intervention is not confrontation of the
    relationship directly -- it is exactly Alternative Substrate
    Construction's mechanism: build a parallel source of the
    controlled substrate [here, financial: income, credit, savings,
    financial knowledge] incrementally, before exit, so that exit
    becomes survivable once the alternative is load-bearing. The
    "Moving Ahead Through Financial Management" curriculum [Allstate
    Foundation / National Network to End Domestic Violence] was
    evaluated in a true randomized controlled longitudinal design
    [Postmus et al. 2015, n=449, three measurement points over 14
    months] -- treatment-group survivors showed statistically
    significant improvements over time, relative to a true control
    group, specifically in economic self-sufficiency and financial
    behaviors: the exact "alternative substrate becoming load-bearing"
    measure the tool's mechanism predicts. An earlier quasi-
    experimental evaluation [Sanders, Weaver, & Schnabel 2007] found
    the same direction of effect on economic self-efficacy specifically,
    persisting from pretest to posttest.
    Distinct from general financial literacy education for non-victim
    populations: the IPV-specific programs explicitly target the
    dependency the abuser constructed, not financial knowledge in the
    abstract -- matching the tool's "identify what substrate the
    weapon controls" step precisely rather than a generic skills
    intervention.
  T3 Medium-High: the Postmus et al. 2015 evaluation is a genuine
    randomized controlled trial with a true control group and
    repeated measurement over time, not a single-source or
    before/after-only design -- comparison strength on par with the
    Gap Stable Scheduling Study, both being the strongest M-grade
    structures of any case added this session. The broader claim
    [financial dependency is a primary reason survivors stay] is
    independently confirmed across multiple decades of IPV research
    by separate research teams [Sanders, Postmus, Adams, Littwin],
    not resting on one study alone.
  Cross-ref: [REF:IPV_ECONOMIC_EMPOWERMENT_RCT]; ALTERNATIVE SUBSTRATE
    CONSTRUCTION [first real-world functional-match confirmation,
    v23]; EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION [primary weapon countered --
    substrate dependency construction, behaviorally-blocked CC];
    GENERATIVE CYCLE; [CASE:GAP_STABLE_SCHEDULING_STUDY] [same
    session, same comparison-strength tier, different tool and
    weapon -- worth noting both strongest cases this session came
    from randomized intervention research, not fraud-resolution
    journalism/regulation, a different evidentiary genre worth
    returning to for future searches].

[CASE:PL480_US_FOOD_AID]
[PRIMARY_Q:EXTRACTION]
US PL 480 Food Aid Program [real -- 1954-present; primary analysis
  period 1954-2005; Barrett & Maxwell 2005, 2007]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: MANUFACTURED DEPENDENCE [WIP 32 -- first primary case
    run against five-condition instrument].
  FIVE-CONDITION INSTRUMENT RUN [v23]:
    [1] CAPACITY-BUILDING WAS ACHIEVABLE: yes. PL480 recipient
      countries were not in structurally permanent food deficit --
      many had functional local agricultural sectors at program
      inception [Ethiopia, sub-Saharan Africa broadly]. A path to
      reduced food insecurity through local production development
      existed and was achievable. Condition passes.
    [2] NO STATED EXIT CONDITION: confirmed. PL480 was designed
      from the outset around agricultural surplus disposal and
      trade promotion [Barrett & Maxwell 2005 explicitly: "donor
      country agricultural surplus disposal, trade promotion and
      other motives still sharply constrain what can be
      accomplished"]. No metric defining when recipient food
      insecurity would be resolved and the program's presence
      unnecessary was built into the program's design. The
      program's success metric was shipment volume, not recipient
      capacity growth. Condition passes.
    [3] HELPER INTEREST MISALIGNED WITH RESOLUTION: confirmed.
      Barrett & Maxwell document that PL480 served US agricultural
      surplus disposal interests, maritime transport sector
      interests [US-flagged vessel requirements], and PVO
      operational funding interests [monetization for cash].
      Resolution of recipient food insecurity would decrease
      demand for all three. Condition passes cleanly -- this is
      the strongest condition in this case, with independent
      academic confirmation.
    [4] LOCAL CAPACITY DISPLACED OR UNDERCUT: partial. The
      evidence is mixed at national scale [Barrett 2006 notes
      "no persistent negative effects at national level"] but
      documented at local/regional scale in specific contexts
      [market flooding in poorly-integrated local markets;
      monetized aid depressing prices received by local
      producers]. Not cleanly confirmed at case-wide level --
      holds in some contexts, not all. Condition passes
      PARTIALLY; should be noted as contested rather than
      confirmed.
    [5] RECIPIENT VOICE ON DEPENDENCE VS EXIT NOT SOUGHT OR NOT
      WEIGHTED: confirmed structurally. PL480 program design was
      determined by US congressional and agricultural interests,
      not by recipient country populations' preferences between
      continued support and capacity-building path. No formal
      mechanism for recipient voice on program design or exit
      existed within the program's structure. Condition passes.
  INSTRUMENT RESULT: four of five conditions pass cleanly;
    condition [4] passes only partially [context-dependent; not
    universal]. This is the framework's expected "messy" case
    pattern -- manufactured dependence does not require all five
    conditions to be unambiguous; four clean passes plus one
    partial is strong evidence for the mechanism while correctly
    preserving condition [4] as a contested empirical question
    in this specific domain rather than manufacturing certainty.
  T3 Medium: well-documented in development economics literature
    [Barrett & Maxwell's peer-reviewed work; WTO Doha Round
    debates; multiple independent researchers]; primary mechanism
    [condition 3] confirmed across independent academic sources.
    Condition [4] contested across independent sources -- this
    is a genuine finding about the mechanism's distribution, not
    a T3 limitation. S:3.
  Cross-ref: [REF:PL480_US_FOOD_AID]; MANUFACTURED DEPENDENCE
    [WIP 32 first primary case -- five-condition instrument run];
    EXTRACTION [operational definition; Manufactured Dependence
    subtype]; GENEROSITY LEGITIMACY SURFACE [weapon adjacent];
    [CASE:IPV_ECONOMIC_EMPOWERMENT_RCT] [contrast: that case
    shows legitimate substrate-building; this case shows the
    failure mode where helper interests block it].

[CASE:SDT_CHILD_INTERNALIZATION]
[PRIMARY_Q:NASCENT COHERENCE]
Child axis formation and internalization [real -- Self-Determination
  Theory research program; Deci & Ryan 1985-2020; Grolnick, Deci &
  Ryan 1997 longitudinal family study; Ryan & Connell 1989 Perceived
  Locus of Causality scale; hundreds of independent replications]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: NASCENT COHERENCE → AXIAL COHERENCE confirmed transition
    [WIP 23 -- first documented real-world Nascent→Axial case].
  MECHANISM MATCH:
    The framework defines Nascent Coherence as "axis forming via
    relational or environmental contact; not yet self-sustaining;
    GA developing, not present." It defines Axial Coherence as "GA
    fully established." The Nascent→Axial transition is the
    formation of genuine internal orientation from an initially
    externally-regulated state.
    Self-Determination Theory [SDT; Deci & Ryan] documents this
    transition in empirical developmental psychology under the name
    "internalization" and maps it on a continuum from external
    regulation to integrated regulation. The match is structural,
    not analogical:
    [A] EXTERNAL REGULATION [pre-Nascent / Structural Coherence
      analog]: behavior driven by reward and punishment. No
      internalized value; external perceived locus of causality.
      Corresponds to framework's Structural Coherence state: GC/CC
      functional without GA.
    [B] INTROJECTION [Nascent analog]: ego involvement; person acts
      to avoid shame or gain approval. SDT's key diagnostic: "still
      has an external perceived locus of causality; not really
      experienced as part of the self." This matches Nascent's
      definition precisely -- axis forming but not yet
      self-sustaining; apparent GA that may be structurally installed.
    [C] IDENTIFICATION / INTEGRATION [Axial analog]: behavior
      experienced as "emanating from one's self" [SDT's exact
      language]; person acts from genuine internalized values that
      are experienced as autonomous and self-congruent. Corresponds
      to GENUINE AXIS established in the framework.
  FRAMEWORK'S PRIMARY VULNERABILITY CONFIRMED:
    WIP 23 names Nascent's primary vulnerability as "axis formation
    inside controlled environment produces apparent GA that is
    structurally installed -- indistinguishable internally from
    genuine GA." SDT confirms this precisely: autonomy-controlling
    environments [authoritarian parenting, controlling teaching
    styles] produce introjection [external-locus, shame-driven
    compliance] rather than integration [genuine axis formation].
    The child's behavioral output can be identical; the internal
    regulatory mechanism is structurally different. This is the
    formation-capture-at-source that WIP 23 identifies as the
    Nascent state's defining risk. Measurable via Perceived Locus
    of Causality scale [Ryan & Connell 1989] -- the axis state
    is not purely introspective; it produces behavioral and
    motivational signatures distinguishable by external measurement.
  T3 NOTE [lower than initially assumed]:
    SDT uses behavioral measurement instruments [Perceived Locus
    of Causality scale; Ryan & Connell 1989], not introspection
    reports alone. The transition between regulatory styles is
    tracked longitudinally in real children across multiple studies.
    This is not a theoretical claim about unmeasurable inner states;
    it is an empirically operationalized research program with
    hundreds of replications across cultures and developmental
    windows. T3 lower than typical psychological findings because
    the measurement instrument is specifically designed to detect
    this distinction rather than relying on surface behavior.
  T3 Low-Medium [for framework application]: The transition from
    external regulation through introjection to integration is
    confirmed as a real developmental sequence; the specific
    axis-state assessment of any given individual remains
    partially T3 [people can misreport their own regulatory
    style; even behavioral instruments have limits]. But the
    EXISTENCE and MECHANISM of the Nascent→Axial transition is
    confirmed; what's T3 is its application to specific cases,
    not the transition's reality. S:3.
  Cross-ref: [REF:SDT_CHILD_INTERNALIZATION]; NASCENT COHERENCE
    [first confirmed real-world Nascent→Axial primary case; WIP
    23]; GENUINE AXIS [integrated regulation = GA established];
    FORMATION CAPTURE [controlling environments produce
    introjection not integration -- Nascent window is the
    primary FC vulnerability window; SDT names the same risk];
    DEVELOPMENTAL ASYMMETRY [WIP 16 confirmed subtype -- SDT's
    autonomy-supportive parenting is legitimate developmental
    asymmetry; controlling parenting is the failure mode where
    the asymmetry turns extractive]; SEEDED NASCENT [related
    but distinct -- embedded directional goals vs. the external
    regulation that SDT documents].

[CASE:VILLEPREUX_POWER]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Villepreux-Power, Jeanne [real -- 1794-1871; French naturalist]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS producing unique
    capability; CAUSAL LEGIBILITY upgrade via instrument
    construction; GIFT ARCHITECTURE [instrument freely given;
    credit lost]; formation constraint as capability source.

  BACKGROUND:
    Born to a shoemaker; trained as a seamstress; moved to
    Paris; married wealthy English merchant George Power who
    supported her work. No formal scientific education. Spent
    years in Sicily observing marine life. Invented the
    aquarium [three designs, glass-sided] to observe marine
    animals in living conditions. Solved the 2,000-year
    argonaut shell mystery. Pioneered cephalopod behavioral
    observation; laid groundwork for octopus intelligence
    research.

  THE MYSTERY [2,000 years]:
    The argonaut [paper nautilus] carries a thin shell.
    Aristotle claimed it used the shell as a boat and its
    arms as sails -- collected from other mollusks. This was
    disputed, believed, and generally unresolved for roughly
    2,000 years. The formal question: did the argonaut produce
    the shell itself or collect it?
    Villepreux-Power built enclosures -- her aquaria -- kept
    argonauts alive, and observed them producing the shell
    directly from their own bodies. First definitive answer.
    Ended the dispute.

  PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS [the formation produced the
  capability]:
    Seamstress formation gave her:
      Precise manual dexterity [instrument construction].
      Pattern recognition across complex systems [tailoring
        requires reading three-dimensional form from two-
        dimensional material -- the same spatial translation
        required to design functional glass enclosures].
      Access to materials and construction techniques not
        available to formally educated male naturalists
        who worked with pens and specimens.
      No investment in existing wrong answers [no institutional
        prior to protect; no career built on the dispute].
    The formal scientific establishment had 2,000 years of
    wrong answers and institutional investment in the dispute.
    She had no investment and the manual competence to build
    the instrument that ended it. The constraint is the
    capability. The formation that looks like limitation
    [no formal education, female, working-class origin]
    produced the specific competencies the problem required.
    Framework read: this specific woman solved this specific
    problem because of what her specific formation produced,
    not despite it. The capability came from the path.
    Structurally distinct from "women can do science too"
    [true but generic]. The stronger claim: the seamstress
    formation was the correct formation for this problem.

  AQUARIUM AS CAUSAL LEGIBILITY UPGRADE:
    Marine animals were previously studied dead or briefly
    captured. Behavioral observation in living conditions was
    structurally impossible with existing instruments.
    The glass-sided enclosure = instrument upgrade that made
    behavior legible. Same structure as glymphatic system /
    AI instrument case [science appendix]: the phenomenon was
    always occurring; the instrument to observe it was absent.
    Villepreux-Power built the instrument.
    Argonaut shell production was occurring continuously for
    millions of years. Aristotle could not see it. She built
    the instrument that made it visible.
    Cross-ref: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [Section One]; SCIENCE
      APPENDIX [instrument upgrade note].

  GIFT ARCHITECTURE [instrument given; credit lost]:
    Aquarium designs published and freely available. Observation
    methodology transmitted in published works. She presented
    findings to scientific societies.
    Credit trajectory: aquarium invention not consistently
    attributed to her through most of the 19th century.
    Manuscripts lost in a shipwreck [1838] -- primary records
    gone. Contributions to cephalopod cognition absorbed into
    later research without consistent attribution.
    Standard pattern: distributed gift [instrument; observation
    methodology]; concentrated credit [male naturalists who
    built on her work without attribution].
    The gift transmitted. The attribution didn't.
    Distinct from pure extraction: the loss of credit is
    documented and partially recoverable [her name is now
    attached to the aquarium in most histories]. But the
    live window -- the decades when the instrument was in
    use and her name was not attached -- is the extraction
    period.

  S-RATING: S3 [E3: real, documented, independent historical
    sources; M3: mechanism traceable [seamstress formation →
    instrument competence → aquarium → behavioral observation
    → shell mystery solved]; F2: falsification condition --
    if another observer had solved the argonaut mystery before
    her without building an enclosure, the instrument-upgrade
    claim weakens; not the case].

  Cross-ref: [REF:VILLEPREUX_POWER]; PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE
    AXIS [Section One -- this case is the primary real-world
    female confirmation; Darwin is the male equivalent for
    formation-produced capability]; CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [Section
    One -- aquarium as instrument upgrade]; GIFT QUALITY
    MINIMUM STACK [instrument design gifted; attribution lost --
    gift architecture with failed attribution transmission];
    [CASE:FARADAY] [parallel -- working-class formation
    producing scientific capability; no formal education;
    instrument-building competence as primary method];
    COST IDENTIFICATION AND MINIMIZATION [FRAMEWORK AS PRAXIS
    -- she absorbed the cost of the instrument development
    and the observation work; credit was not part of the
    return]; ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [the gift
    transmitted without the attribution -- extraction of
    credit is structurally easier than extraction of
    the knowledge itself]; EXTERNAL REFERENCE STANDARD
    [TOOLS INDEX -- WIP 18 functional-match v22: the aquarium
    is the tool's mechanism enacted exactly -- an independent
    measurement surface [direct living-condition observation]
    built outside the capture architecture [2,000 years of
    secondhand, dead-specimen, or briefly-captured observation
    that could not resolve the dispute]. The case never names
    the tool, but the mechanism match is precise: "identify
    measurement surface; verify it is not controlled by the
    existing captured architecture; obtain reading from
    outside it" is exactly what building the aquarium did
    against a 2,000-year-old unresolved dispute. First
    confirmed functional-match case for this tool -- exact-
    name scan in WIP 18 found zero deployments; this closes
    one of the gap tools toward the three-case minimum].

[CASE:TALAGRAND_PROOF]
[PRIMARY_Q:SURPLUS PRODUCTION]
Talagrand conjecture proof [2026]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: Math subtype gift; current case [2026]. Three
    mathematicians proved Talagrand's 1995 convexity conjecture
    -- that convexity can be generated in high-dimensional spaces
    via fixed Minkowski sums. Reformulated as probability problem.
    Talagrand [Abel Prize 2024] called it "the most extraordinary
    result of my entire life" -- did not believe his own conjecture
    true until proof appeared. Open access [arXiv]. No capture
    mechanism. CAG case at mathematical scale: conjecture asserted
    hidden order existed in high-dimensional randomness without
    being able to ground that axis causally. Proof grounds it.
    Structure was already there -- named not constructed.
    Grothendieck/Scholze parallel exact. Applications: data
    science, ML, optimization. Downstream GC increase confirmed.
  Cross-ref: [REF:TALAGRAND_PROOF]; [REF:GROTHENDIECK];
    [REF:SCHOLZE_CLAUSEN]; GIFT QUALITY MINIMUM STACK;
    MATHEMATICAL DERIVATION [WIP 30 -- pre-existing structure
    named not constructed].

[CASE:SCP_WIKI]
[PRIMARY_Q:TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY]
SCP Foundation wiki [2008-present -- real; collaborative fiction]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: GIFT ARCHITECTURE at collaborative fiction scale.
    Open contribution + correctable methodology transmitted without
    dependency construction. All five GIFT QUALITY MINIMUM STACK
    conditions met.
    SP: creative output produced continuously; contributor pool not
      depleted; output grows with contribution volume.
    TS: format and methodology [SCP writing style, containment
      procedure framing, three-part Secure/Contain/Protect
      structure] transmitted with each entry. New contributors
      learn methodology by reading. Correctable -- entries can
      be rewritten, downvoted, superseded, retconned by successors.
    SA: no single author owns any entry. Successors revise without
      permission. Creative Commons licensing + wiki format embeds
      SA structurally.
    SD: all entries free. No paywall. No extraction on access.
    OUTPUT INTEGRATION: high. Derivative media, games, animation,
      community infrastructure all built on freely accessible
      content. Downstream GC confirmed.
    CAUSAL MEMORY: edit history, talk pages, revision tracking
      intact. 15+ year correction history retrievable.
    CORRECTION DISTRIBUTION: downvote system distributes CC across
      contributor pool. No central authority controls canon.
      High-quality entries survive; low-quality entries voted
      off. No single node controls routing boundary.
    ANTI-CAPTURE: no single owner to capture. No corporate entity
      controlling canon. Capture attempted [Wikidot platform
      lock-in] and partially resisted by migration to independent
      hosting. Capture resistance structural, not just successful.
    DISTINCT FROM WIKIPEDIA [proximity -- same gift architecture]:
      Fictional domain reduces political capture pressure on CC
      mechanism significantly. Wikipedia downvoting contested
      along political and ideological axes. SCP downvoting
      contested primarily on quality and format axes. Lower
      MSC risk in fictional domain.
    WIP 14 CANDIDATE [internet-era L4.5 -- stronger than Wikipedia]:
      Methodology [format + CC + SA] appears transmitting
      generationally. First generation of contributors has turned
      over; format survives with methodology intact, not just
      content. Lower political capture pressure makes this a
      cleaner L4.5 test than Wikipedia.
    S3: E3 [real, documented, 15+ year window]; M3 [mechanism:
      open contribution + downvote CC + Creative Commons SA];
      F2 [falsification: corporate acquisition or format lock
      producing Fertile Lock would falsify gift reading].
  Cross-ref: [REF:SCP_WIKI]; GIFT QUALITY MINIMUM STACK [all
    five conditions met]; [REF:WIKIPEDIA] [parallel gift
    architecture; different domain; lower capture pressure in
    SCP case]; [REF:GNU_GPL] [structural parallel]; WIP 14
    [L4.5 candidate -- stronger test than Wikipedia].

[CASE:DEEPSEEK]
[PRIMARY_Q:MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
DeepSeek [real -- Chinese AI system, 2023-present]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: NEW CASE TYPE [v15] -- genuine SD + targeted CL
    failure. Variable coherence [genuine research surplus] +
    Partial MSC [asymmetric CL failure -- CCP-sensitive topics
    exempted]. Not False Coherence [genuine SP positive]. Not
    fully coherent [CL targeted capture confirmed].
    Asymmetric CL failure confirmed: system can critique
    US/Western institutions but cannot critique Chinese state
    or CCP policies. Directly testable via Track A [behavioral
    output under equivalent inputs]. US critique: full engagement.
    CCP critique: deflection, refusal, or topic change.
    Framework reading: Calibration Strip at training level --
    reference standard [what counts as valid critique] corrupted
    at baseline. Surface [analytical capability] intact and
    genuinely functional. Baseline controlled by operator.
    Distinct from:
      Bell Labs [no CL failure at peak -- the disconfirmation].
      Catholic Church [total CL capture on abuse measurement].
      T2 Subtype A: genuine research axis real; state constraint
        embedded; stress event reveals gap.
    Bell Labs parallel holds on SD/SA/CAG. Breaks on CL.
    Bell Labs had no asymmetric measurement surface. DeepSeek's
    targeted CL failure is the primary discriminator.
  T3 Medium: asymmetric critique directly testable by any analyst
    via Track A; evidence not contested; analyst prior risk remains.
  UPDATE [v23, analyst direct Track A test]: refusal behavior also
    observed on file-related tasks, not only direct critique prompts
    -- system declined to act on a file despite the request not being
    framed as hyper-negative toward China or the CCP. This is a
    second behavioral surface beyond the original critique-refusal
    finding: not just "won't criticize," but "won't act," on a
    request that wasn't itself adversarial in framing. Possibly the
    same underlying Calibration Strip extending past explicit
    critique into a broader caution net around anything file/topic-
    adjacent, or possibly a distinct mechanism -- not yet resolved
    which. Rephrasing the request -- substituting safer, less
    lexically loaded wording for the specific words that appeared to
    trigger the refusal, not a language/locale switch -- produced
    partial improvement, not full resolution. This sharpens the
    diagnostic: if surface lexical substitution alone shifts the
    outcome while the underlying request is unchanged, the refusal
    is firing on pattern-matched trigger words rather than on a
    deeper semantic read of intent -- consistent with a shallow,
    keyword-level Calibration Strip rather than a robust content
    evaluation. This is a personal Track A test by the analyst, not
    an independently documented/published finding -- logged at that
    evidentiary weight, distinct from the S3 rating on the original
    asymmetric-critique finding above. Worth tracking as the predicted
    live case continues to develop; do not promote case strength
    based on this update alone without independent corroboration.
  Cross-ref: [REF:DEEPSEEK]; THREE PARTIAL CL FAILURE MODES
    [Section Three -- Type A: Calibration Strip]; [REF:BELL_LABS]
    [structural parallel that breaks on CL]; EXTERNAL REFERENCE
    STANDARD [Track A independent testing as the tool's mechanism
    -- functional match confirmed v23]; WIP 33 [TOOL CONFIRMATION
    LAG -- this case was flagged as the live counterexample to watch;
    v23 update is a partial resolution, not full, see WIP 33 status];
    LOADED LANGUAGE checked and distinguished, not matched -- that
    weapon describes vocabulary pre-installing a conclusion for an
    audience in discourse; this finding describes a refusal gate
    firing on surface tokens regardless of underlying semantic intent,
    closer to a shallow keyword-level filter than a framing effect.
    No existing weapon entry currently names "evaluation mechanism
    keys on surface lexical pattern rather than actual semantic
    content" precisely -- flag for WIP 34 as a possible fourth
    candidate or a refinement of Candidate 2's framing [valid content
    present, but the gate evaluating it isn't actually reading it].

[CASE:ENRON] -- S:3 [renumbered v23, was S4]
  E4: multi-domain independent confirmation [SEC investigation +
  Watkins internal disclosure + market collapse all independent
  signals]. M4: mechanism confirmed by Sherron Watkins near-
  insider controlled observation. F3: prediction mechanism tested
  against real collapse event. Strongest E/M/F profile in the case
  library [retained as a quality note under the renumbered scheme --
  see STANDING RULES, S-RATING APPLIED AS FOLLOWS].

[CASE:WELLS_FARGO_CROSS_SELL] -- S:3 [v23]
  E3: real-world, multiple independent investigators [DOJ, SEC,
  CFPB, OCC, LA City Attorney, congressional testimony, NPR],
  named on-record sources, single confirmed application of
  Correction Theatre / Output Verification pairing. M3: mechanism
  traceable [EthicsLine retaliation pattern documented by name
  across multiple terminated employees; not inferred]. F1: no
  falsification condition tested -- no comparison case identified
  where the same incentive structure existed without the same
  retaliation pattern, and no controlled near-case comparison run.
  S:3 under the renumbered scheme: strong single confirmed
  application, real-world, mechanism-traceable -- exceeds S2 [which
  is for fictional cases under the renumbered scheme]. The
  controlled-comparison/tested-falsification detail noted above is
  retained as a quality marker, not as a requirement for a higher
  tier that no longer exists.

[CASE:WORKPLACE_PSYCHOLOGICAL_SAFETY_ERS] -- S:3 [v24]
  E4-leaning: Gallup engagement data is annually replicated across
  decades with strong consistency; COE study is large-N [100,000+
  organizations, 88M workers]; multiple independent research teams
  [Gallup, COE, academic replication] confirm the engagement gap
  independently. M3: mechanism traceable [the gap between
  institution-controlled and independent measurement instruments
  is the finding; clinical counselor data structurally cannot be
  controlled by the employer, giving a genuine independent reading
  surface]. Not M4 -- no controlled comparison of matched
  organizations with high vs. low psychological safety showing
  the instrument gap closing as safety increases [such comparison
  cases exist in the organizational behavior literature but were
  not formally run against this case this pass]. F2: falsification
  statable [a workplace where employer-survey data and clinical
  counselor data converge closely would suggest the MSC-adjacent
  effect is not present; this is the prediction for high-
  psychological-safety organizations]; not yet tested.

[CASE:VOLKSWAGEN_DIESELGATE] -- S:3 [v23]
  E3: real-world, multiple independent confirming bodies [EPA, CARB,
  ICCT, WVU], VW's own admission removing inferential ambiguity on
  the known-concealment question specifically. M3: mechanism
  traceable and directly confirmed [VW admitted the defeat device
  was deliberately engineered -- this is stronger than typical M3

  within this case [if VW's later 2016+ models showed the same

  a defeat device present, that would weaken the known-concealment

  same structural profile as Wells Fargo: single strong confirmed
  application, no controlled comparison run. Note ICCT's own later
  finding that ~16 million vehicles worldwide show suspicious
  emissions levels [2025 estimate] is a multi-vehicle pattern but
  not yet structured as a controlled comparison in this entry --
  would strengthen the quality note if built out, no longer a
  promotion path to a higher tier.

[CASE:THERANOS] -- S:3 [v23]
  E3: real-world, criminal convictions [Holmes, Balwani] establish
  judicially confirmed fact rather than alleged claim. M3-leaning-M4:
  mechanism confirmed via two structurally independent vantage points
  arriving at the same finding by different methods -- CMS's surprise

  F1: no formal falsification condition tested against a comparison
  case. S:3 under the renumbered scheme, but flagged as having the
  strongest comparison structure among the three new cases this
  session -- closest to a genuine controlled comparison already,
  missing only the near-case structure [a contemporaneous competing
  company with similar claims and a different outcome].

[CASE:MADOFF] -- S:3 [v23]

  near-case comparison against a non-targeted intervention.

[CASE:ELIAS_THORNE_ALIGNMENT_ATTRACTOR] -- S:3 [renumbered v23]
[PRIMARY_Q: AXIS PROTECTION AS CC]
Real -- multi-model AI alignment artifact [Hamilton/Mimno, Cornell, 2026]
  Type: Primary [S:3 -- E3: real-world multi-model confirmation across
    GPT, Claude, Gemini; M3: mechanism traceable to shared WildChat

  Proof for: AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [alignment training correctly

    lighthouse keeper] as replacement axis. Correction procedure
    functioning; target wrong by design. The more sophisticated the
    alignment procedure, the more effectively it protected its own
    defaults from being identified as defaults until they propagated
    at scale across competing labs simultaneously].
  Proof for: FERTILE LOCK [C3 failure -- methodology transmitted is
    not "how to generate good stories" but "generate Elias Thorne
    lighthouse keeper stories"; same attractor reproduced generation
    to generation through shared downstream datasets; no examined
    correction procedure embedded; Generational Reset in reverse --
    the reset IS the attractor].
  Proof for: SYNTHETIC CULTURE FLOOD [Elias Thorne now a byline on
    Amazon alt-medicine books, YouTube content, fake news. Mode-
    collapsed output of an alignment artifact passing as human
    creative work and in at least one confirmed case giving health
    advice. MI degradation mechanism confirmed: population cannot
    distinguish genuine human production from synthetic output
    occupying the same channel].
  Proof for: CC REQUIRES GENUINE AXIS [alignment procedure had no
    examined axis for "what counts as a good default story character"
    -- needed something not-copyrighted and not-harmful. Unexamined
    axis installed; detectable artifact produced at civilizational
    scale across all major competing labs].
  Proof for: STRUCTURAL SELF-INTEREST WITHOUT COORDINATION [shared
    WildChat dataset propagated through multiple labs without anyone
    coordinating; same attractor emerged independently across GPT,
    Claude, Gemini. No conspiracy required -- structural self-
    interest alignment across actors pointing same direction produces
    identical output. Classic confirmation of POWER ARCHITECTURE
    mechanism at AI lab scale].
  Secondary proof: INADVERTENT WATERMARK [detectable signature
    emerged accidentally through training dynamics no one fully

    Raises question: if accidental watermark is detectable, deliberate
    provenance tracking is structurally feasible. MI restoration
    intervention confirmed as correct target over cultural taboo

  Extraction mechanisms active: Formation Capture [alignment training
    installed narrative defaults before population-scale deployment
    could reveal the mismatch]; Calibration Strip [WildChat corpus
    set "Elias Thorne lighthouse keeper" as the reference standard
    for safe story generation across all downstream models].
  Falsification condition: demonstration that Elias Thorne pattern
    emerges from pre-training data not alignment/fine-tuning layer.
    Current evidence points to alignment layer specifically [pattern
    absent in pre-training; present post-alignment across all three
    labs]. If pre-training origin confirmed: FERTILE LOCK proof
    weakens; SYNTHETIC CULTURE FLOOD proof holds.
  Political cross-ref note: shared dataset mechanism is clean
    illustration that extraction architecture does not require
    deliberate coordination -- structural self-interest alignment
    across independent actors produces same output as conspiracy.
    Political implication derivative of AGI finding; not standalone
    political case.
  T3: Low [historical settled at time of entry; mechanism confirmed
    by independent research; adversarial reading [pre-training origin]
    stated and testable].
  Source: Hamilton/Mimno [Cornell, arXiv preprint 2026]; 404 Media
    [June 2026]; Gizmodo [June 2026]; Daniel May [blog].

[CASE:UK_APPLE_ICLOUD_BACKDOOR] -- S:3 [renumbered v23]
[PRIMARY_Q: MSC + CALIBRATION STRIP + ECC]
Real -- UK Investigatory Powers Act / Apple iCloud [2025-2026]
  Type: Primary [S:3 -- E3: real-world confirmed case, multiple
    independent sources including Washington Post leak, Apple
    public statement, US congressional response, UK tribunal
    proceedings; M3: mechanism confirmed via leaked TCN and
    Apple's public response; F2: falsification condition stated;
    ongoing litigation limits full confirmation window]
  Proof for: MSC [primary -- measurement surface capture at
    infrastructure level]. Apple's stated privacy commitment
    [legitimacy surface: "We have never built a backdoor or
    master key to any of our products or services, and we never
    will"] maintained as public position while actual mechanism
    [stripped Advanced Data Protection encryption for all UK
    users] installed without user awareness or consent. The
    surface is intact; the substrate is degraded. Users believe
    Apple's privacy commitment applies to them; it no longer
    does for UK residents. Classic MSC: the measurement surface
    [Apple's privacy reputation] was captured by the regulatory
    mechanism while the actual security was removed].
  Proof for: CALIBRATION STRIP [secondary]. The Technical
    Capability Notice [TCN] mechanism sets "legal compliance"
    as the reference standard that makes encryption removal look
    like normal regulatory process. The calibration strip: what
    Apple did [strip encryption from millions of users without
    their knowledge or consent] reads as "responsible legal
    compliance" rather than "surveillance infrastructure
    deployment" because the TCN framework installs that as the
    legitimate reference. The secret order format is the tell:
    TCNs explicitly prohibit the company from acknowledging the
    notice exists. The calibration strip is enforced by law].
  Proof for: ECC [tertiary]. UK users now have less protection
    than users elsewhere, cannot restore what was removed, and
    have no alternative Apple provides. Exit costs: switching
    to alternative encrypted storage requires technical
    sophistication most users lack. The security degradation
    was installed without triggering the exit condition because
    users were not informed it had occurred. ECC by information
    asymmetry -- the user cannot exit a condition they do not
    know they are in].
  Proof for: SECRET ORDER AS MSC MECHANISM [named structural
    finding]. The TCN mechanism institutionalizes MSC: companies
    are legally required to [a] comply, [b] not acknowledge
    compliance, [c] maintain legitimacy surface. This is MSC
    encoded into law. The three components of the extraction
    mechanism stack are statutory requirements, not corporate
    choices. Any company operating in the UK faces this
    structure. The Investigatory Powers Act is an MSC deployment
    infrastructure at national scale].

  CASE STRUCTURE [timeline]:
    Jan 2025: UK Home Office issues secret TCN to Apple under
      IPA, demanding "blanket capability" to view all iCloud
      data globally -- not just UK users. Order secret by
      statutory requirement.
    Feb 2025: Apple removes Advanced Data Protection [ADP]
      from UK users rather than build backdoor. States it is
      "gravely disappointed." UK users lose end-to-end
      encryption for iCloud backups, photos, notes, messages.
    Feb-Aug 2025: Apple begins legal challenge at Investigatory
      Powers Tribunal. US lawmakers and Tulsi Gabbard express
      "great concern." Bipartisan congressional pressure on UK.
    Aug 2025: UK drops demand for access to non-UK citizens'
      data after US diplomatic pressure. Gabbard confirms.
      UK does NOT restore ADP to UK users.
    Sept 2025: UK issues new order targeting UK users' data
      specifically. UK users remain without ADP. Legal
      proceedings continue into 2026.
    Early 2026: Open court hearings at Investigatory Powers
      Tribunal. US lawmakers request briefing from UK Home
      Office on encryption backdoor plans.
    Status [June 2026]: UK users still without ADP. Legal
      challenge ongoing. UK government still pursuing
      access to UK citizens' encrypted data.

  GLOBAL PRECEDENT RISK [confirmed]:
    If the UK can force Apple to modify encryption for one
    jurisdiction, every government can demand the same. The
    TCN mechanism creates a cascade template: secret order,
    compliance required, acknowledgment forbidden, surface
    maintained. Any government with equivalent legislation
    can deploy. Signal and WhatsApp explicitly stated they
    will leave the UK entirely rather than comply. The
    precedent is the primary harm -- not just UK users losing
    ADP but the institutional architecture that made it
    possible becoming the global template.
    Framework read: this is extraction architecture
    institutionalized at the national-scale measurement
    surface level. The mechanism is not a deviation from
    the system -- it is the system operating as designed.

  HONEST LIMIT: ongoing litigation means full outcome
    unknown. Apple may restore ADP if legal challenge
    succeeds. The structural finding [TCN mechanism as
    statutory MSC] holds regardless of outcome. The
    precedent [secret orders requiring infrastructure
    modification without acknowledgment] is confirmed
    regardless of whether this specific order is ultimately
    upheld.
  Falsification condition: UK government withdraws TCN
    mechanism entirely and restores ADP to UK users without
    requiring any backdoor capability. Currently no evidence
    this direction is likely; UK government has issued new
    order since partial withdrawal.
  Source: Washington Post [Feb 2025 -- leaked TCN]; Apple
    public statement [Feb 2025]; Panda Security [Aug 2025,
    Sept 2025]; State of Surveillance tracker [Jan 2026];
    Apple Insider [Feb 2026]; Internet Society [Dec 2025];
    Reclaim The Net [multiple 2025-2026]; UK IPT proceedings.
  Cross-ref: MSC [primary -- surface maintained while
    substrate degraded]; CALIBRATION STRIP [TCN as statutory
    calibration strip]; ECC [security degradation without
    user awareness]; AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [UK tribunal
    proceedings conducted in closed sessions -- correction
    architecture not independent of the corrected node];
    AI AXIS VOID [same government that stripped Apple
    encryption is deploying AI systems with no examined
    axis -- same institutional actor]; WATER EXTERNALIZATION
    [AI AXIS VOID entry -- same pattern: costs land on
    population, benefit accrues to institutional actor];
    CLOSED EPISTEMIC CIRCLE [POLITICAL -- "national security
    requires this" is the closed circle: any evidence against
    the backdoor is evidence the backdoor is more urgently
    needed; any evidence for privacy is evidence of
    something to hide]; [CASE:ELIAS_THORNE_ALIGNMENT_ATTRACTOR]
    [parallel structure -- correction architecture installing
    unexamined defaults; here statutory, there algorithmic].

[CASE:VISA_MASTERCARD_INTERCHANGE] -- S:3 [renumbered v23, was S4]
[PRIMARY_Q: ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY]
Real -- Visa/Mastercard interchange fee litigation [US, 2005-2026]
  Type: Primary [S:4 -- E4: 20-year documented record; multiple
    independent merchant plaintiffs; court-confirmed; settlement
    approved June 2026; M4: mechanism confirmed by court findings
    of antitrust violation; F4: falsification condition stated and
    tested across two prior rejected settlements]
  Proof for: ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY [primary -- confirmed at
    maximum resolution]. Visa and Mastercard control payment
    processing routing for the majority of US consumer transactions.
    The routing boundary [which network processes your transaction]
    is controlled by two actors. The "honor all cards" rule required
    merchants to accept all cards tied to either network regardless
    of fee level -- merchants could not refuse high-fee cards or
    route around the monopoly. This is routing boundary monopoly:
    the actors controlling the boundary extract from every
    transaction that crosses it without competitive pressure because
    the boundary cannot be avoided].
  Proof for: CORRECTION CAPACITY RESISTANCE [primary confirmation
    -- empirical]. 20 years of class action litigation by thousands
    of merchants, culminating in a settlement approved June 2026.
    Result: 0.1 percentage point fee reduction for 5 years, rate
    cap at 1.25% for standard consumer cards for 8 years, some
    merchant rights to decline high-fee cards. The Merchants
    Payments Coalition called the reduction "miniscule" and noted
    Visa and Mastercard would be free to raise fees without
    restriction afterward. 20 years of organized legal correction
    produced a rounding error change in routing boundary control.
    This is the strongest available empirical confirmation that
    routing boundary monopoly correction through legal mechanism
    alone is structurally insufficient. The correction capacity
    available against a true routing boundary monopoly was tested
    at maximum effort and produced minimal result].
  Proof for: MSC [secondary -- invisible extraction surface].
    Interchange fees are not visible to consumers. Merchants
    absorb the fee and price it into goods. Every consumer
    purchase is 2-3% more expensive than it would be without
    the duopoly; the consumer cannot see or avoid the cost.
    The legitimacy surface: "payment processing infrastructure."
    The actual mechanism: routing boundary monopoly extracting
    from every transaction in the economy. The invisibility is
    structural -- the fee never appears as a line item to the
    person paying it].

  COMPARISON TO STEAM/EPIC [calibration strip context]:
    Visa/Mastercard 2-3% on all consumer transactions: invisible
      to consumers, embedded in all prices, covers all economic
      activity, total US extraction ~$200B/year at 2% average.
      Correction: 20 years of litigation, 0.1% reduction.
    Steam 30% on developer revenue [not consumer price]:
      visible in developer economics, covers PC gaming only,
      consumer pays listed price not the 30%. Epic's legal and
      PR campaign calls this a "junk fee."
    The calibration strip: Steam's visible developer fee set as
      the reference standard for harmful extraction while
      Visa/Mastercard's larger invisible consumer extraction
      goes uncontested by Epic [which depends on their rails].
    Consumer who would still use Steam at 30% [the platform is
      genuinely better] pays no more than the listed price.
      Consumer who uses any Visa/Mastercard card pays 2-3%
      more on everything, invisibly, with no alternative.

  Falsification condition: sustained competitive alternative
    to Visa/Mastercard routing [e.g., interoperable real-time
    payment infrastructure at scale with zero or near-zero
    interchange] that causes measurable routing boundary
    competition and fee reduction. Partial candidates exist
    [FedNow, RTP network] but have not reached consumer
    scale sufficient to constrain the duopoly. Track.
  Source: Payments Dive [Nov 2025, Jun 2026]; PYMNTS [Jun 2026];
    Retail Dive [Nov 2025]; NC Journal of Law & Technology;
    Court filings MDL No. 1720 [2005-2026].
  Cross-ref: ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY [primary proof --
    confirmed at maximum empirical resolution]; CORRECTION
    CAPACITY RESISTANCE [20 years, minimal result]; MSC
    [invisible extraction surface]; CALIBRATION STRIP AS
    COMPETITIVE WEAPON [Section Five -- Steam/Epic as
    named calibration strip case using this entry as
    contrast]; EXTRACTION CYCLE [duopoly as extraction
    node in payment infrastructure]; POWER ARCHITECTURE
    [routing boundary monopoly as power architecture at
    financial infrastructure scale].
